登革病毒 血清型与疾病严重程度和临床血液学参数的关联:基于医院的分析研究

Association of Dengue Virus Serotypes with Disease Severity and Clinical-Hematological Parameters: A Hospital-Based Analytical Study
作者： Mohammad Ali, Dr. Kailash jatav -
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:登革热疾病的严重程度因病毒血清型和 宿主 因素而异。本研究探讨了 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型、疾病严重程度和临床血液学参数之间的关联。目的和目的:研究循环登革热血清型的分子 监测,通过 RT-PCR 鉴定 DENV-1 至 DENV-4,确定其人口统计和临床分布,评估与疾病严重程度的关联,并生成数据以加强登革热 监测 和公共卫生干预措施。方法:2023 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月,对印多尔 Index 医学院医院和研究中心的 290 名临床疑似登革热患者进行了一项基于医院的横断面分析研究。使用 SD BIOLINETM Dengue Duo Rapid Test 筛选血清样本,然后使用 RealStar® Dengue Type RT-PCR Kit 1.0 进行病毒 RNA 提取和实时 RT-PCR。鉴定了登革热血清型,并根据疾病严重程度比较了临床和血液学参数。结果:实时 RT-PCR 证实 67 名患者 (23.1%) 感染登革热。DENV-2 是主要血清型(46.3%),其次是 DENV-3(26.9%)、DENV-1(20.9%)、DENV-4(3.0%)和混合感染(3.0%)。血清型与疾病严重程度显着相关(p=0.012)。平均血小板计数从无预警信号的登革热患者的 142,800/μL 显着下降至 重症登革热 患者的 42,300/μL(p<0.001)。血小板减少症是最常见的血液学异常(88.1%)。DENV-2感染、血小板计数<50,000/μL、出血表现和腹痛与重症登革热独立相关。结论:DENV-2 占主导地位,且与 重症登革热 显着相关。分子血清分型与临床和血液学评估相结合可能有助于早期识别患有严重疾病风险的患者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热疾病的严重程度因病毒血清型和 宿主 因素而异,因此需要澄清特定的 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型如何影响临床结果和血液学参数,以便为 监测 和公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
2023 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月,印多尔 Index 医学院医院和研究中心对 290 名临床疑似登革热患者进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面分析研究。
核心方法
使用 SD BIOLINETM Dengue Duo Rapid Test 筛选血清样本,然后使用 RealStar® Dengue Type RT-PCR Kit 1.0 进行病毒 RNA 提取和实时 RT-PCR,以确定 DENV 血清型,并根据疾病严重程度比较临床和血液学参数。
主要结果
实时RT-PCR证实67例(23.1%)患者感染登革热,其中以DENV-2为主(46.3%),其次是DENV-3(26.9%)、DENV-1(20.9%)、DENV-4(3.0%)和混合感染(3.0%)。血清型与疾病严重程度显着相关(p=0.012),平均血小板计数从无警告信号的登革热中的 142,800/μL 下降至 重症登革热 中的 42,300/μL(p<0.001)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,DENV-2 占主导地位,并且与 重症登革热 显着相关,这表明分子血清分型与临床和血液学评估相结合可能有助于早期识别处于严重疾病风险的患者。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过优先考虑 DENV-2 监测并将血清型特异性风险分层纳入临床管理来为登革热 监测 策略提供信息,以改善重症病例的早期发现。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和基于医院的抽样限制了因果推断和对更广泛人群的普遍性,并且由于潜在的选择偏差和无法测量的混杂因素,证据强度被评估为中等。

Association of Dengue Virus Serotypes with Disease Severity and Clinical-Hematological Parameters: A Hospital-Based Analytical Study

Authors: Mohammad Ali, Dr. Kailash jatav -
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Dengue disease severity varies by viral serotype and host factors. This study examined associations between dengue virus (DENV) serotypes, disease severity, and clinical-hematological parameters. Aims & Objectives: To investigate the molecular epidemiology of circulating dengue serotypes, identify DENV-1 to DENV-4 by RT-PCR, determine their demographic and clinical distribution, assess associations with disease severity, and generate data to strengthen dengue surveillance and public health interventions. Methods: A hospital-based cross-sectional analytical study was conducted among 290 clinically suspected dengue patients at Index Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Indore, from January 2023 to January 2026. Serum samples were screened using the SD BIOLINETM Dengue Duo Rapid Test, followed by viral RNA extraction and real-time RT-PCR with the RealStar® Dengue Type RT-PCR Kit 1.0. Dengue serotypes were identified, and clinical and hematological parameters were compared according to disease severity. Results: Real-time RT-PCR confirmed dengue infection in 67 (23.1%) patients. DENV-2 was the predominant serotype (46.3%), followed by DENV-3 (26.9%), DENV-1 (20.9%), DENV-4 (3.0%), and mixed infections (3.0%). Serotype was significantly associated with disease severity (p=0.012). Mean platelet count declined significantly from 142,800/μL in dengue without warning signs to 42,300/μL in severe dengue (p<0.001). Thrombocytopenia was the most common hematological abnormality (88.1%). DENV-2 infection, platelet count <50,000/μL, bleeding manifestations, and abdominal pain were independently associated with severe dengue. Conclusion: DENV-2 predominated and was significantly associated with severe dengue. Molecular serotyping combined with clinical and hematological assessment may facilitate early identification of patients at risk of severe disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue disease severity varies by viral serotype and host factors, creating a need to clarify how specific dengue virus (DENV) serotypes influence clinical outcomes and hematological parameters to inform surveillance and public health interventions.
Design and population
A hospital-based cross-sectional analytical study was conducted among 290 clinically suspected dengue patients at Index Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Indore, from January 2023 to January 2026.
Core methods
Serum samples were screened using the SD BIOLINETM Dengue Duo Rapid Test, followed by viral RNA extraction and real-time RT-PCR with the RealStar® Dengue Type RT-PCR Kit 1.0 to identify DENV serotypes, and clinical and hematological parameters were compared according to disease severity.
Main results
Real-time RT-PCR confirmed dengue infection in 67 (23.1%) patients, with DENV-2 predominating (46.3%), followed by DENV-3 (26.9%), DENV-1 (20.9%), DENV-4 (3.0%), and mixed infections (3.0%). Serotype was significantly associated with disease severity (p=0.012), and mean platelet count declined from 142,800/μL in dengue without warning signs to 42,300/μL in severe dengue (p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that DENV-2 predominated and was significantly associated with severe dengue, suggesting that molecular serotyping combined with clinical and hematological assessment may facilitate early identification of patients at risk of severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform dengue surveillance strategies by prioritizing DENV-2 monitoring and integrating serotype-specific risk stratification into clinical management to improve early detection of severe cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and hospital-based sampling limit causal inference and generalizability to the broader population, and the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to potential selection bias and unmeasured confounding.

气候变化对登革热的影响。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.
作者： Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了气候变化为何导致登革热和其他蚊媒疾病激增,并确定了理解气候和社会经济因素如何相互作用以改变登革热 传播 动态的关键知识差距。
设计与对象
该论文是一篇叙述性综述,综合了气候变化对登革热影响的证据,利用全球研究和荟萃分析来评估 传播 模式和风险因素。
核心方法
该综述采用荟萃分析方法来评估温度对登革热 发病率 的影响,整合气候和社会经济中介因素来模拟非线性关系。
主要结果
该评论报告称,气候变暖正在扩大美洲和亚洲的登革热负担,温度对登革热的影响是非线性的,并受到气候和社会经济因素的调节。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为气候变化正在通过复杂的相互作用重塑登革热 流行病学 的证据,强调需要采取综合气候适应策略来缓解 传播 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了加强 媒介 控制、监测 和气候信息疾病负担预测的紧迫性,以增强气候变化中登革热的抵御能力。
局限与证据强度
该审查依赖于综合证据而不是原始数据,限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于纳入研究的异质性和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.

Authors: Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
Original abstract (English)
Combined effects of hydrometeorological hazards and urbanisation on dengue risk in Brazil: a spatiotemporal modelling study. Climate-sensitive disease outbreaks in the aftermath of extreme climatic events: a scoping review. The impact of climate change on transmission season length: West Nile virus as a case study. A scoping review of mosquito vector range shifts: widespread expansions and evidence gaps in climate attribution. Climate change, extreme weather and socioeconomic change drive surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne infectious diseases. Strengthening dengue resilience in a changing climate requires improved vector control, surveillance, climate-informed disease burden projections, sustainable urban and environmental management, and stronger community engagement. Partnerships that span health, environment, climate, economic and community sectors are critical for creating climate-resilient systems that improve health, environment and well-being. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study examines why climate change is driving surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne diseases, identifying a critical knowledge gap in understanding how climatic and socioeconomic factors interact to alter dengue transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The paper is a narrative review synthesizing evidence on climate change impacts on dengue, drawing on global studies and meta-analyses to assess transmission patterns and risk factors.
Core methods
The review employs a meta-analysis approach to evaluate temperature impacts on dengue incidence, integrating climatic and socioeconomic mediators to model nonlinear relationships.
Main results
The review reports that climate warming is expanding the dengue burden in the Americas and Asia, with temperature effects on dengue incidence being nonlinear and mediated by climatic and socioeconomic factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that climate change is reshaping dengue epidemiology through complex interactions, highlighting the need for integrated climate-resilient strategies to mitigate transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the urgency of strengthening vector control, surveillance, and climate-informed disease burden projections to build dengue resilience in a changing climate.
Limitations and evidence strength
The review relies on synthesized evidence rather than primary data, limiting causal inference and generalisability; the strength of evidence is moderate due to heterogeneity in included studies and potential confounding.

重症登革热 内型特征的综合临床和蛋白质组精确亚组

Integrated Clinical and Proteomic Precision Subgrouping for Severe Dengue Endotype Signature
作者： Trupti Satish Kadni, Anoop T. Ambikan, Iva Filipovic, Muralidhar Varma, Devasmita Dutta, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay, Soham Gupta, Piya Paul Mudgal, Ujjwal Neogi
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 重症登革热 仍然难以预测,因为具有不同临床轨迹的患者可能表现出重叠的特征,并且传统的严重程度分类可能无法完全捕捉潜在的生物异质性。在这项研究中,我们应用综合的临床和蛋白质组内分型方法来剖析登革热疾病的异质性并确定与严重程度相关的分子特征。方法 对健康对照者和根据 WHO 2009 年严重程度标准分类的登革热患者的血浆蛋白质组谱以及详细的临床、生化、血液学、凝血和免疫学参数进行分析。高通量蛋白质组分析、无监督聚类、通路富集和基于机器学习的分类被用来识别登革热内型并定义与预测的严重疾病相关的分子特征。结果 登革热严重程度的增加与肝功能、凝血参数、血液学指标和炎症介质(包括 IL-6、IL-15、HGF 和 MUC-16)的进行性异常相关。然而,蛋白质组学分析显示传统严重程度类别之间存在大量重叠,表明仅临床分类并不能完全解决登革热 宿主 反应异质性。综合临床蛋白质组聚类鉴定出不同的登革热内型,包括预测的富含炎症、抗病毒药物 和细胞毒性淋巴细胞相关途径的严重内型。这种高风险内型的特点是 IL-15、IFN-γ 和颗粒酶升高,与细胞毒性淋巴细胞相关 抗病毒药物 反应的协调激活一致。机器学习分析进一步表明,蛋白质组学特征是这种内型的强有力的鉴别器,支持它们作为严重 宿主 反应状态的生物标志物的潜在用途。结论 整合的临床蛋白质组内分型提供了超越传统严重程度分级的分子分辨率,并确定了与 重症登革热 相关的免疫途径。该框架可以提高对登革热进展的生物学理解,并支持未来的风险分层和生物标志物开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重症登革热 仍然难以预测,因为具有不同临床轨迹的患者可能呈现重叠的特征,并且传统的严重程度分类可能无法完全捕捉潜在的生物异质性。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,采用综合的临床和蛋白质组内分型方法来剖析登革热疾病的异质性并识别与严重程度相关的分子特征。
核心方法
对健康对照者和根据 WHO 2009 年严重程度标准分类的登革热患者的血浆蛋白质组图谱以及详细的临床、生化、血液学、凝血和免疫学参数进行了分析。高通量蛋白质组分析、无监督聚类、通路富集和基于机器学习的分类被用来识别登革热内型并定义与预测的严重疾病相关的分子特征。
主要结果
登革热严重程度的增加与肝功能、凝血参数、血液学指标和炎症介质(包括 IL-6、IL-15、HGF 和 MUC-16)的进行性异常相关。蛋白质组学分析显示传统严重程度类别之间存在大量重叠,表明仅临床分类并不能完全解决登革热 宿主 反应异质性。综合临床蛋白质组聚类鉴定出不同的登革热内型,包括预测的富含炎症、抗病毒药物 和细胞毒性淋巴细胞相关途径的严重内型。
解释与创新
集成的临床蛋白质组内分型提供了超越传统严重程度分级的分子分辨率,并确定了与 重症登革热 相关的免疫途径。该框架可以提高对登革热进展的生物学理解,并支持未来的风险分层和生物标志物开发。
科研与公卫意义
确定的蛋白质组学特征和内型可以为 重症登革热 风险分层生物标志物的开发提供信息,并增强对驱动疾病进展的 宿主 反应机制的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,缺乏详细的方法、统计或数字报告,这限制了结论的强度。由于缺乏完整的方法和结果细节,证据强度被评估为低。

Integrated Clinical and Proteomic Precision Subgrouping for Severe Dengue Endotype Signature

Authors: Trupti Satish Kadni, Anoop T. Ambikan, Iva Filipovic, Muralidhar Varma, Devasmita Dutta, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay, Soham Gupta, Piya Paul Mudgal, Ujjwal Neogi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Severe dengue remains difficult to predict because patients with different clinical trajectories may present with overlapping features, and conventional severity classifications may not fully capture underlying biological heterogeneity. In this study, we applied an integrated clinical and proteomic endotyping approach to dissect dengue disease heterogeneity and identify molecular signatures associated with severity. Methods Plasma proteomic profiles were analyzed together with detailed clinical, biochemical, hematological, coagulation, and immunological parameters from healthy controls and dengue patients classified according to WHO 2009 severity criteria. High-throughput proteomic analysis, unsupervised clustering, pathway enrichment, and machine-learning–based classification were used to identify dengue endotypes and define molecular features associated with predicted severe disease. Results Increasing dengue severity was associated with progressive abnormalities in liver function, coagulation parameters, hematological indices, and inflammatory mediators, including IL-6, IL-15, HGF, and MUC-16. However, proteomic profiling revealed substantial overlap across conventional severity categories, indicating that clinical classification alone does not fully resolve dengue host-response heterogeneity. Integrated clinical-proteomic clustering identified distinct dengue endotypes, including a predicted severe endotype enriched for inflammatory, antiviral, and cytotoxic lymphocyte-associated pathways. This high-risk endotype was characterized by elevated IL-15, IFN-γ, and granzymes, consistent with coordinated activation of cytotoxic lymphocyte-associated antiviral responses. Machine-learning analysis further showed that proteomic features were strong discriminators of this endotype, supporting their potential utility as biomarkers of severe host-response states. Conclusion Integrated clinical-proteomic endotyping provides molecular resolution beyond conventional severity grading and identifies immune pathways associated with severe dengue. This framework may improve biological understanding of dengue progression and support future risk stratification and biomarker development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Severe dengue remains difficult to predict because patients with different clinical trajectories may present with overlapping features, and conventional severity classifications may not fully capture underlying biological heterogeneity.
Design and population
In this study, an integrated clinical and proteomic endotyping approach was applied to dissect dengue disease heterogeneity and identify molecular signatures associated with severity.
Core methods
Plasma proteomic profiles were analyzed together with detailed clinical, biochemical, hematological, coagulation, and immunological parameters from healthy controls and dengue patients classified according to WHO 2009 severity criteria. High-throughput proteomic analysis, unsupervised clustering, pathway enrichment, and machine-learning–based classification were used to identify dengue endotypes and define molecular features associated with predicted severe disease.
Main results
Increasing dengue severity was associated with progressive abnormalities in liver function, coagulation parameters, hematological indices, and inflammatory mediators, including IL-6, IL-15, HGF, and MUC-16. Proteomic profiling revealed substantial overlap across conventional severity categories, indicating that clinical classification alone does not fully resolve dengue host-response heterogeneity. Integrated clinical-proteomic clustering identified distinct dengue endotypes, including a predicted severe endotype enriched for inflammatory, antiviral, and cytotoxic lymphocyte-associated pathways.
Interpretation and novelty
Integrated clinical-proteomic endotyping provides molecular resolution beyond conventional severity grading and identifies immune pathways associated with severe dengue. This framework may improve biological understanding of dengue progression and support future risk stratification and biomarker development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The identified proteomic features and endotypes could inform the development of biomarkers for severe dengue risk stratification and enhance understanding of host-response mechanisms driving disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and lacks detailed methodological, statistical, or numerical reporting, which constrains the strength of the conclusions. The evidence strength is assessed as low due to the absence of full methodological and result details.

影响 Vero 细胞中 登革病毒 复制的参数

Parameters affecting dengue virus replication in Vero cells
作者： Md. Muzammal Haque Asim, Arman Mahmud Khan, Tasmina Ferdous Susmi, F.M. Ashik Mahmud, Ahmed Zubayer, Md. Tunvir Mozid, Rudhra Rayhan, Md. Muktadir Rahman Ashik, Shamima Nasrin, Imran Khan
摘要中文翻译登革热已成为一种全球威胁,每年影响数百万人,并造成巨大的 死亡率 负担。尽管 疫苗接种 是在登革热流行地区预防这种疾病的最有效方法,但迫切需要开发强大且可扩展的生产工艺,以最大限度地提高 疫苗 的产量。目前的研究旨在优化四种重组活减毒登革热血清型的关键生长参数,包括收获时间表、温度、感染复数 (MOI) 和代谢物浓度。生长动力学数据以及随后的放大研究确定了在规定的生产窗口内对所有四种血清型进行多次收获的可行性,第一次收获最佳发生在第 4 天和第 8 天之间。所有血清型都表现出对低 MOI 和温度变化的强烈响应,导致在较短的时间范围内滴度增加 2 至 25 倍。此外,代谢物分析揭示了不同的谷氨酰胺和葡萄糖消耗动态,凸显了其对于优化感染介质的重要性。总而言之,这些发现为 登革病毒 生产提供了宝贵的见解,并定义了可以支持未来优化研究和大规模生产的关键参数。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热造成了全球重大健康负担,虽然 疫苗接种 是最有效的预防措施,但可扩展的 疫苗 生产仍然是一个瓶颈。需要进行这项研究来确定可以优化 疫苗 制造的 登革病毒 复制的参数。
设计与对象
该研究在 Vero 细胞中研究了四种重组活减毒登革热血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4),以确定病毒复制的最佳生长条件。
核心方法
作者评估了关键生长参数,包括收获时间表、温度、感染复数 (MOI) 和代谢物浓度,然后进行生长动力学分析和放大研究。
主要结果
所有四种登革热血清型均对低 MOI 和温度变化表现出强烈的响应性,导致病毒滴度在较短的时间内增加 2 至 25 倍。代谢分析确定了不同的谷氨酰胺和葡萄糖消耗动态,这对于优化感染介质至关重要。
解释与创新
研究结果定义了 登革病毒 生产的关键参数,证明优化的 MOI、温度和代谢条件可以提高病毒产量并缩短生产时间。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果直接为扩大登革热 疫苗 生产的努力提供了信息,通过优化生物工艺参数,有可能提高 疫苗 在流行地区的可用性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外 Vero 细胞培养系统,并不涉及体内复制或临床结果,因此在将研究结果外推到 疫苗 制造或公共卫生影响时需要谨慎。

Parameters affecting dengue virus replication in Vero cells

Authors: Md. Muzammal Haque Asim, Arman Mahmud Khan, Tasmina Ferdous Susmi, F.M. Ashik Mahmud, Ahmed Zubayer, Md. Tunvir Mozid, Rudhra Rayhan, Md. Muktadir Rahman Ashik, Shamima Nasrin, Imran Khan
Original abstract (English)
Dengue has become a global threat affecting millions of people annually and contributing to a significant mortality burden. Although vaccination is the most effective approach to prevent this disease in dengue-endemic regions, the development of a robust and scalable production process is urgently required to maximize vaccine yield. The current study aimed to optimize the key growth parameters of four recombinant live attenuated dengue serotypes, including harvest schedule, temperature, Multiplicity of infection (MOI), and metabolite concentration. Growth kinetics data, followed by a scale-up study, established the feasibility of multiple harvests for all four serotypes within a defined production window, with the first harvest optimally occurring between Days 4 and 8. All serotypes demonstrated strong responsiveness to low MOIs and temperature shifts, resulting in 2- to 25-fold increases in titer within a reduced time frame. Furthermore, metabolite profiling revealed distinct glutamine and glucose consumption dynamics, highlighting its importance for optimizing infection media. Taken together, these findings provide valuable insights into dengue virus production and define critical parameters that can support future optimization studies and large-scale production.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue poses a major global health burden, and while vaccination is the most effective preventive measure, scalable vaccine production remains a bottleneck. The study was needed to identify parameters that could optimize dengue virus replication for vaccine manufacturing.
Design and population
The study investigated four recombinant live attenuated dengue serotypes (DENV-1 to DENV-4) in Vero cells to determine optimal growth conditions for virus replication.
Core methods
The authors evaluated key growth parameters including harvest schedule, temperature, multiplicity of infection (MOI), and metabolite concentration, followed by growth kinetics analysis and a scale-up study.
Main results
All four dengue serotypes showed strong responsiveness to low MOIs and temperature shifts, resulting in 2- to 25-fold increases in viral titer within a reduced time frame. Metabolite profiling identified distinct glutamine and glucose consumption dynamics critical for optimizing infection media.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings define critical parameters for dengue virus production, demonstrating that optimized MOI, temperature, and metabolite conditions can enhance viral yield and reduce production time.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results directly inform efforts to scale up dengue vaccine production, potentially improving vaccine availability in endemic regions by optimizing bioprocess parameters.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro Vero cell culture systems and does not address in vivo replication or clinical outcomes, warranting caution in extrapolating findings to vaccine manufacturing or public health impact.

Journal: Vector borne and zoonotic diseases (Larchmont, N.Y.)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-14  |  DOI: 10.1177/15303667261479462  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15303667261479462
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

受过教育的厄瓜多尔人对登革热 疫苗接种 的犹豫和看法。

Hesitancy and Perceptions Regarding Dengue Vaccination Among Educated Ecuadorians.
作者： Matias Panchana-Lascano, Flavio Veintemilla-Burgos, Carlos Vela Velásquez, Rodolfo Kronfle Cordovez, Geovanna Minchalo-Ochoa, Sebastian Balda, Thomas Leone-Berry, Ivo Diaz-Djevoich, Natasha Cedillo Santos
摘要中文翻译背景:近年来,全球登革热病例激增。在厄瓜多尔,报告病例从 2023 年的 27,906 例增加到 2024 年的 57,712 例。尽管其他国家可以使用多种 疫苗,但社会经济差异、气候变化和 疫苗 犹豫等因素继续阻碍预防工作。本研究旨在分析厄瓜多尔民众的看法,并探讨对登革热 疫苗 犹豫不决的可能原因。方法:这项横断面观察性研究是根据 STROBE 指南进行的。改编自 Kricorian 等人的在线调查,于 2024 年 6 月 1 日至 2025 年 6 月 25 日期间通过 Qualtrics 应用程序分发。符合条件的参与者是厄瓜多尔人,年满 18 岁,最低教育水平为学士。统计分析包括描述性百分比和卡方检验,显着性设置为 p < 0.05。结果:共有 735 名参与者被纳入。大多数人认为登革热 疫苗 是安全的(87.2%,n = 641),而 12.8%(n = 94)认为它不安全。总体而言,75.9%的人表示愿意接种疫苗。感知的 疫苗 安全性与 疫苗接种 意愿、居住地区和为后代接种疫苗的意愿显着相关(均 p < 0.001),但与教育水平、登革热了解(p = 0.747)或既往登革热经历(p = 0.111)无关。结论:大多数厄瓜多尔人认为登革热 疫苗 是安全的,但仍然犹豫不决。有针对性的教育工作对于提高接受度和覆盖率至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 疫苗 在厄瓜多尔急剧上升,尽管其他地方有 疫苗,但对 疫苗 的犹豫可能会破坏预防。需要这项研究来了解为什么受过教育的厄瓜多尔人不愿接受登革热 疫苗。
设计与对象
一项横断面观察性研究是利用 2024 年 6 月 1 日至 2025 年 6 月 25 日期间通过 Qualtrics 应用程序分发的在线调查进行的。符合资格的参与者是年满 18 岁或以上且至少拥有学士学位的厄瓜多尔居民。
核心方法
调查改编自 Kricorian 等人。并使用描述性百分比和卡方检验进行分析,显着性设置为 p < 0.05。
主要结果
在 735 名参与者中,87.2% (n = 641) 认为登革热 疫苗 是安全的,而 12.8% (n = 94) 认为它不安全。总体而言,75.9%的人表示愿意接种疫苗。
解释与创新
感知的 疫苗 安全性与 疫苗接种 意愿、居住地区和为后代接种疫苗的意愿显着相关(均 p < 0.001),但与教育水平、登革热了解(p = 0.747)或既往登革热经历(p = 0.111)无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对感知安全的有针对性的教育工作可以提高受过教育的厄瓜多尔人对 疫苗 的接受度和覆盖率。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和在线抽样限制了因果推断和对更广泛人群的普遍适用性,需要中等强度的证据。

Hesitancy and Perceptions Regarding Dengue Vaccination Among Educated Ecuadorians.

Authors: Matias Panchana-Lascano, Flavio Veintemilla-Burgos, Carlos Vela Velásquez, Rodolfo Kronfle Cordovez, Geovanna Minchalo-Ochoa, Sebastian Balda, Thomas Leone-Berry, Ivo Diaz-Djevoich, Natasha Cedillo Santos
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Global dengue cases have surged in recent years. In Ecuador, reported cases increased from 27,906 in 2023 to 57,712 in 2024. Despite the availability of multiple vaccines in other countries, factors such as socioeconomic disparities, climate change, and vaccine hesitancy continue to hinder prevention efforts. This study aims to analyze the perceptions of the Ecuadorian population and explore possible reasons for hesitancy toward the dengue vaccine. METHODS: This cross-sectional observational study was conducted in accordance with the STROBE guidelines. Online surveys, adapted from Kricorian et al., were distributed via the Qualtrics app between June 1st, 2024, and June 25, 2025. Eligible participants were Ecuadorian, at least 18 years old, and had a minimal education level of bachelor. Statistical analysis included descriptive percentages and chi-square tests, with significance set at p < 0.05. RESULTS: A total of 735 participants were included. Most perceived a dengue vaccine as safe (87.2%, n = 641), while 12.8% (n = 94) considered it unsafe. Overall, 75.9% expressed willingness to be vaccinated. Perceived vaccine safety was significantly associated with vaccination willingness, region of residence, and willingness to vaccinate offspring (all p < 0.001), but not with education level, dengue understanding (p = 0.747), or prior dengue experience (p = 0.111). CONCLUSION: Most Ecuadorians perceive dengue vaccines as safe, yet hesitancy persists. Targeted educational efforts are essential to improve acceptance and coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue incidence has risen sharply in Ecuador, yet vaccine hesitancy may undermine prevention despite available vaccines elsewhere. The study was needed to understand why educated Ecuadorians hesitate to accept a dengue vaccine.
Design and population
A cross-sectional observational study was conducted using online surveys distributed via the Qualtrics app from June 1, 2024, to June 25, 2025. Eligible participants were Ecuadorian residents aged 18 years or older with at least a bachelor’s degree.
Core methods
Surveys were adapted from Kricorian et al. and analyzed using descriptive percentages and chi-square tests with significance set at p < 0.05.
Main results
Among 735 participants, 87.2% (n = 641) perceived the dengue vaccine as safe, while 12.8% (n = 94) considered it unsafe. Overall, 75.9% expressed willingness to be vaccinated.
Interpretation and novelty
Perceived vaccine safety was significantly associated with vaccination willingness, region of residence, and willingness to vaccinate offspring (all p < 0.001), but not with education level, dengue understanding (p = 0.747), or prior dengue experience (p = 0.111).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational efforts addressing perceived safety could improve vaccine acceptance and coverage among educated Ecuadorians.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and online sampling limit causal inference and generalizability to the broader population, warranting a moderate strength of evidence.

因帕尔谷登革热的 血清学 和 血清学:机构研究

Epidemiology and Serology of Dengue in Imphal Valley: An Institutional Study
作者： Rabita Hijam, Pratibha Huidrom, Ranjeeta Sougaijam
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热是一种大流行病,给全球带来沉重负担,并且可能致命。有效的登革热控制措施应考虑 暴发 的位置、相关的人口统计参数以及所使用的诊断测试技术。目的:本研究评估了登革热的流行病学和人口统计特征以及因帕尔谷使用的诊断测试技术。材料和方法:这项前瞻性研究是在曼尼普尔邦因帕尔的 Escent 药物科学研究所(NABL 认可的诊断实验室)进行的。纳入了2023年9月至2025年12月期间因帕尔政府和私立医院报告的所有临床疑似登革热病例。使用Microsoft Excel 2019进行数据分析。结果:在临床疑似登革热病例的 3,424 份样本中,1,514 份(44.22%)呈阳性,其中男性 735 例,女性 779 例。仅 NS1 呈阳性的病例最为常见,表明早期感染,其次是 NS1 和 IgM 均呈阳性的病例。结论:因帕尔从2023年至2025年连续三年经历了登革热暴发。研究期间最大的暴发发生在2025年,由病例急剧增加推动。应加大力度控制高风险地区的蚊子孳生地,清除盛有积水的容器,以降低伊蚊的密度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热给全球带来了巨大的负担,并且可能致命,但有效的控制需要了解当地的 流行病学、人口统计数据和诊断测试实践。该研究旨在评估登革热的流行病学和人口统计特征以及因帕尔谷使用的诊断测试技术。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性研究是在曼尼普尔邦因帕尔的 Escent 药物科学研究所(NABL 认可的诊断实验室)进行的。2023年9月至2025年12月期间,因帕尔政府和私立医院报告的所有临床疑似登革热病例均被纳入。
核心方法
使用Microsoft Excel 2019进行数据分析。提供的证据不报告采样程序、分析、测序、干预方案、结果定义、统计分析或计算模型。
主要结果
在从临床疑似登革热病例收集的 3,424 份样本中,1,514 份(44.22%)呈阳性,其中男性 735 例,女性 779 例。仅 NS1 呈阳性的病例最为常见,表明早期感染,其次是 NS1 和 IgM 均呈阳性的病例。
解释与创新
因帕尔从2023年至2025年连续三年经历登革热暴发,其中2025年最大的暴发是由于病例急剧增加所致。该研究强调了早期感染标记物 (NS1) 和 NS1/IgM 双阳性在诊断谱中的主导地位。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要通过清除积水容器来减少高风险地区的伊蚊密度,从而加强对蚊子繁殖栖息地的控制。诊断模式建议优先考虑基于 NS1 的测试以进行早期 暴发 检测。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告研究局限性、偏倚、混杂因素、样本量合理性、对照或普遍性。仅从摘要中无法充分评估证据强度,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Epidemiology and Serology of Dengue in Imphal Valley: An Institutional Study

Authors: Rabita Hijam, Pratibha Huidrom, Ranjeeta Sougaijam
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Dengue is a pandemic disease with a substantial global burden and can be fatal. Effective dengue control measures should consider the location of outbreaks, relevant demographic parameters, and the diagnostic testing techniques used. Aims: This study evaluated the epidemiological and demographic characteristics of dengue and the diagnostic testing techniques used in Imphal Valley. Materials and Methods: This prospective study was conducted at the Escent Institute of Pharmaceutical Sciences & Research (NABL-accredited Diagnostic Laboratory), Imphal, Manipur. All clinically suspected dengue cases reported by government and private hospitals in Imphal were included from September 2023 to December 2025. Microsoft Excel 2019 was used for data analysis. Results: Of 3,424 samples received from clinically suspected dengue cases, 1,514 (44.22%) were positive, comprising 735 male and 779 female cases. Cases positive only for NS1 were the most frequent, indicating early infection, followed by cases positive for both NS1 and IgM. Conclusion: Imphal experienced dengue outbreaks in three consecutive years from 2023 to 2025. The largest outbreak during the study period occurred in 2025, driven by a sharp increase in cases. Efforts to control mosquito breeding habitats in high-risk areas should be intensified by removing containers of stagnant water to reduce the density of Aedes mosquitoes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue imposes a substantial global burden and can be fatal, yet effective control requires understanding local epidemiology, demographics, and diagnostic testing practices. The study aimed to evaluate the epidemiological and demographic characteristics of dengue and the diagnostic testing techniques used in Imphal Valley.
Design and population
This prospective study was conducted at the Escent Institute of Pharmaceutical Sciences & Research (NABL-accredited Diagnostic Laboratory) in Imphal, Manipur. All clinically suspected dengue cases reported by government and private hospitals in Imphal were included from September 2023 to December 2025.
Core methods
Microsoft Excel 2019 was used for data analysis. The supplied evidence does not report sampling procedures, assays, sequencing, intervention protocols, outcome definitions, statistical analyses, or computational models.
Main results
Of 3,424 samples received from clinically suspected dengue cases, 1,514 (44.22%) were positive, comprising 735 male and 779 female cases. Cases positive only for NS1 were the most frequent, indicating early infection, followed by cases positive for both NS1 and IgM.
Interpretation and novelty
Imphal experienced dengue outbreaks in three consecutive years from 2023 to 2025, with the largest outbreak in 2025 driven by a sharp increase in cases. The study highlights the predominance of early infection markers (NS1) and dual NS1/IgM positivity in the diagnostic profile.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for intensified mosquito breeding habitat control in high-risk areas by removing containers of stagnant water to reduce Aedes mosquito density. The diagnostic pattern suggests prioritizing NS1-based testing for early outbreak detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations, bias, confounding, sample size justification, controls, or generalisability. The evidence strength cannot be fully assessed from the abstract alone, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

了解登革热动态:控制失败、不平等和气候因素作用的时空证据

Understanding dengue dynamics: spatiotemporal evidence of control failure, inequalities, and the role of climate factors
作者： Gerson Laurindo Barbosa, Monica Pirani, Patricia Marques Moralejo Bermuni, Raquel Gardini Sanches Palasio, Marta Blangiardo, Francisco Chiaravalloti-Neto, Francisco Chiaravalloti Neto
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热在巴西仍然是一个紧迫的公共卫生问题,媒介 控制策略的有效性和可持续性往往有限。尽管巴西国家登革热控制计划 (PNCD) 按照标准化指南运作,但很少有研究评估其在地方一级的长期有效性,以及 PNCD 下一致的登革热 传播 历史和结构化干预工作。此外,之前的总体规模研究表明,社会经济发展与登革热 发病率 之间存在相互矛盾的关联,凸显了小区域时空分析的必要性。方法 我们使用综合嵌套拉普拉斯近似 (INLA) 进行贝叶斯时空分析,以评估 2012 年至 2024 年巴西普雷图河畔圣若泽 (SJRP) 的季度登革热 发病率。该模型结合了人口普查区的环境、昆虫学和社会经济协变量。结果 调查结果显示,媒介 控制行动主要是反应性的,并且在病例激增后得到加强。识字率的提高与登革热 发病率 率的降低相关,而相对剥夺程度的提高则增加了风险。这些结果不同于区域研究的结果,区域研究将较高的社会经济发展与登革热负担增加联系起来,强调了小规模分析发现当地脆弱性的价值。气候变量具有非线性效应,气温升高和降雨量增加与风险增加相关。空间风险模式随时间变化显着,没有一致的高风险集群。鉴于 PNCD 的标准化实施,SJRP 可以作为评估该计划在巴西各地更广泛有效性的合适代理。结论 本研究强调了反应性登革热控制策略的局限性,以及需要采取更主动、综合和社会背景化的方法来解决这一问题。小区域分析为疾病 传播 和社会脆弱性的异质动态提供了重要见解。扩大创新工具的使用,例如基于沃尔巴克氏体的控制和昆虫不育等,可以加强长期预防并指导制定更具弹性的国家政策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管国家控制工作已标准化,但登革热在巴西仍然是一个紧迫的公共卫生问题,但很少有研究评估地方一级的长期计划有效性,其中 传播 持续存在且干预措施是结构化的。
设计与对象
使用综合嵌套拉普拉斯近似 (INLA) 进行贝叶斯时空分析,以评估 2012 年至 2024 年巴西普雷图河畔圣若泽 (SJRP) 的季度登革热 发病率,其中纳入了人口普查区的环境、昆虫学和社会经济协变量。
核心方法
该研究使用贝叶斯时空模型和 INLA 来分析季度登革热 发病率 数据,整合人口普查区层面的环境、昆虫学和社会经济协变量。
主要结果
媒介 控制行动在病例激增后主要是反应性的和强化的,识字率的增加与登革热 发病率 的减少相关,较高的相对剥夺程度增加了风险,气候变量显示出与风险增加相关的非线性效应,气温升高和降雨量增加。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了反应性登革热控制策略的局限性,并表明小区域分析可以发现与区域模式不同的局部脆弱性,强调需要采取主动、综合和社会情境化的方法。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,扩大基于沃尔巴克氏体的控制和昆虫不育等创新工具可能会加强登革热的长期预防,并指导制定更具弹性的国家政策。
局限与证据强度
由于其对单个城市的依赖以及报告的纯抽象性质,证据受到限制,这限制了详细的方法和结果报告,需要对调查结果进行谨慎的解释。

Understanding dengue dynamics: spatiotemporal evidence of control failure, inequalities, and the role of climate factors

Authors: Gerson Laurindo Barbosa, Monica Pirani, Patricia Marques Moralejo Bermuni, Raquel Gardini Sanches Palasio, Marta Blangiardo, Francisco Chiaravalloti-Neto, Francisco Chiaravalloti Neto
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue remains a pressing public health issue in Brazil, where vector control strategies often suffer from limited effectiveness and sustainability. Although the Brazilian National Dengue Control Program (PNCD) operates with standardized guidelines, few studies have evaluated its long-term effectiveness at the local level, with a consistent dengue transmission history and structured intervention efforts under the PNCD. Moreover, previous research at aggregated scales has shown conflicting associations between socioeconomic development and dengue incidence, highlighting the need for small-area spatiotemporal analyses. Methods We conducted a Bayesian spatiotemporal analysis using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA) to assess quarterly dengue incidence in São José do Rio Preto (SJRP), Brazil, from 2012–2024. The model incorporated environmental, entomological, and socioeconomic covariates from census tracts. Results The findings revealed that vector control actions were predominantly reactive and intensified after case surges. Increased literacy rates were associated with a decreased dengue incidence rate, whereas higher relative deprivation increased the risk. These results differ from those of regional studies that associate higher socioeconomic development with an increased dengue burden, emphasizing the value of small-scale analysis to uncover local vulnerabilities. Climatic variables had nonlinear effects, with elevated temperatures and rainfall associated with increased risk. Spatial risk patterns varied significantly over time, with no consistent high-risk clusters. Given the standardized implementation of the PNCD, the SJRP serves as a suitable proxy for assessing the program’s broader effectiveness across Brazil. Conclusion This study underscores the limitations of reactive dengue control strategies and the need for more proactive, integrated, and socially contextualized approaches to address this issue. Small-area analyses provide critical insights into the heterogeneous dynamics of disease transmission and social vulnerability. Expanding the use of innovative tools, such as Wolbachia-based control and sterile insects, among others, may strengthen long-term prevention and guide the development of more resilient national policies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a pressing public health issue in Brazil despite standardized national control efforts, yet few studies have evaluated long-term program effectiveness at the local level where transmission is persistent and interventions are structured.
Design and population
A Bayesian spatiotemporal analysis using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA) was conducted to assess quarterly dengue incidence in São José do Rio Preto (SJRP), Brazil, from 2012 to 2024, incorporating environmental, entomological, and socioeconomic covariates from census tracts.
Core methods
The study used a Bayesian spatiotemporal model with INLA to analyze quarterly dengue incidence data, integrating environmental, entomological, and socioeconomic covariates at the census tract level.
Main results
Vector control actions were predominantly reactive and intensified after case surges, increased literacy rates were associated with decreased dengue incidence, higher relative deprivation increased risk, and climatic variables showed nonlinear effects with elevated temperatures and rainfall associated with increased risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the limitations of reactive dengue control strategies and reveal that small-area analyses can uncover local vulnerabilities that differ from regional patterns, emphasizing the need for proactive, integrated, and socially contextualized approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that expanding innovative tools such as Wolbachia-based control and sterile insects may strengthen long-term dengue prevention and guide the development of more resilient national policies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by its reliance on a single city and the abstract-only nature of the report, which restricts detailed methodological and result reporting, warranting cautious interpretation of the findings.

登革热 并发脑出血、急性呼吸衰竭和院内肺炎:A 病例报告

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report
作者： Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种由 登革病毒 引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病。大多数病例具有自限性病程,极少数情况下重症登革热会导致致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、一天感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。血清学检测证实登革热 IgM 呈阳性。格拉斯哥昏迷评分 (GCS) 从 15 分快速下降至 11 分,导致临床病程变得复杂,在肺部分泌物带血的情况下,需要在第二天进行气管插管。胸部高分辨率计算机断层扫描(CT)提示合并肺水肿。脑部CT显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血(IPH),周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室参数记录了严重的血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。第五天,气管内抽吸培养物中出现了广泛耐药的鲍曼不动杆菌,需要将抗菌治疗升级为美罗培南和多粘菌素。支持性治疗包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆(FFP)输注。患者在第九天成功拔管,并于第十二天出院回家。值得注意的是,在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血 (ICH)、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药 (MDR) 院内肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持以及针对 ICH 的保守治疗的靶向抗菌治疗仍然是良好结果的关键决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,病程主要为自限性,但严重病例可导致罕见但致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。该研究强调需要记录登革热感染的非典型和严重表现,以提高临床识别和管理。
设计与对象
这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。患者接受了支持治疗,包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆输注。
核心方法
血清学检测显示登革热 IgM 阳性,证实了登革热 诊断。支持治疗包括输血,并在整个住院期间进行临床监测。
主要结果
该患者的格拉斯哥昏迷评分从 15 分下降至 11 分,由于肺部分泌物带血,需要在第二天进行气管插管。脑部CT扫描显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血,周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室检查结果包括严重血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。
解释与创新
在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药医院肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持和靶向抗菌治疗在管理 重症登革热 并发症中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
作为单个 病例报告,证据的普遍性有限,无法建立因果关系。由于缺乏比较数据或更广泛的临床背景,证据强度较低。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report

Authors: Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness caused by the dengue virus. Majority of cases have self-limiting course, rarely severe dengue causes fatal neurological complications like encephalitis and haemorrhage. This is case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, one day of altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. Serological testing confirmed dengue IgM positivity. The clinical course was complicated by a rapid decline in Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS) from 15 to 11, necessitating endotracheal intubation on day two in the setting of blood-stained pulmonary secretions. Highresolution Computed Tomography (CT) of the thorax suggested consolidation with pulmonary oedema. CT of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm Intracerebral Parenchymal Haemorrhage (IPH) with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory parameters documented severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/ SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L. Endotracheal aspirate cultures grew extensively drug-resistant Acinetobacter baumannii on day five, requiring escalation of antimicrobial therapy to meropenem and polymyxin. Supportive management included platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma (FFP) transfusion. The patient was successfully extubated on day nine and discharged home on day twelve. Notably, the simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated Intracerebral Haemorrhage (ICH), acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and Multi-Drug Resistant (MDR) nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a triad that is rarely documented in the published literature. Early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy with conservative management of ICH remains critical determinants of a favourable outcome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness with a predominantly self-limiting course, but severe cases can lead to rare but fatal neurological complications such as encephalitis and haemorrhage. The study highlights the need to document atypical and severe presentations of dengue infection to improve clinical recognition and management.
Design and population
This is a case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. The patient received supportive management including platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma transfusions.
Core methods
The diagnosis of dengue was confirmed by serological testing showing dengue IgM positivity. Supportive care included blood product transfusions, and clinical monitoring was performed throughout the hospital course.
Main results
The patient developed a Glasgow Coma Scale decline from 15 to 11, requiring endotracheal intubation on day two due to blood-stained pulmonary secretions. A CT scan of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm intracerebral parenchymal haemorrhage with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory findings included severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L.
Interpretation and novelty
The simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated intracerebral haemorrhage, acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and multi-drug resistant nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a rarely documented triad in the published literature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case underscores the importance of early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy in managing severe dengue complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single case report, the evidence is limited in generalisability and cannot establish causality. The strength of evidence is low due to the absence of comparative data or broader clinical context.

单核细胞 TLR2/4 作为登革热预警信号进展为严重疾病的预测生物标志物

Monocyte TLR2/4 as predictive biomarkers for progression from dengue warning signs to severe disease
作者： Shiqian Chen, Peng Ye, Peiao Rao, Tiantian Sun, Jiali Qi, Ying Zheng, Xiao-Qiang Yu, Fan Zou, Xihua Fu, Ruonan Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景 世界卫生组织(WHO)分类系统,包括登革热(DF)、带有警告标志的登革热(DWS)和重症登革热(SD),已在全球广泛采用;然而,临床管理仍然具有挑战性。目前的警告信号标准缺乏特异性,常常无法区分进展为严重疾病的患者与短暂性疾病的患者,从而导致干预措施延迟和错过治疗窗口。尽管 Toll 样受体 (TLR) 介导先天 抗病毒药物 免疫并与 重症登革热 发病机制 相关,但它们在关键 DWS 转换窗口(干预可以防止进展为严重疾病的阶段)期间的表达动态仍然完全未知。方法进行了一项综合生物标志物发现研究,包括对公开的单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 数据 (GSE220969) 进行生物信息学分析,并在中国广东省 45 名登革热患者和 10 名健康对照 (H) 的独立临床 队列研究 中进行实验验证。从 scRNA-seq 数据中鉴定出阶段特异性免疫特征。通过多参数流式细胞术对 TLR 表达进行定量。使用 ROC 曲线分析评估诊断性能,并使用 Spearman 等级相关性评估与临床实验室参数的相关性。结果 23 个细胞簇的生物信息学分析表明,TLR2/4/7/8 表达在 DWS 阶段达到峰值,主要在单核细胞中。流式细胞术证实,与其他阶段相比,DWS 中表面 TLR2 (AUC 0.874) 和 TLR4 (AUC 0.763) 升高,具有区分 DWS 和 SD 的辨别能力 (TLR2: AUC 0.833;TLR4: AUC 0.810)。此外,TLRs表达与临床指标的相关性分析表明,TLR2和TLR4的表达水平与SD的发生发展相关。结论 DWS 期间单核细胞 TLR2/4 表达升高可识别患者在临床失代偿前有进展为 重症登革热 的风险。这些基于机制的生物标志物为流行地区的护理点风险分层提供了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决区分将发展为 重症登革热 的登革热警告信号 (DWS) 患者与短暂性疾病患者的挑战,因为当前的警告信号标准缺乏特异性。
设计与对象
利用 scRNA-seq 数据的生物信息学分析和在中国广东省 45 名登革热患者和 10 名健康对照的临床 队列研究 中进行的实验验证,进行了一项综合生物标志物发现研究。
核心方法
通过多参数流式细胞术对 TLR 表达进行定量,并使用 ROC 曲线分析评估诊断性能,并使用 Spearman 等级相关性评估相关性。
主要结果
生物信息学分析显示,TLR2/4/7/8 表达在 DWS 阶段达到峰值,主要在单核细胞中。流式细胞术证实,与其他阶段相比,DWS 中表面 TLR2 (AUC 0.874) 和 TLR4 (AUC 0.763) 升高,具有区分 DWS 和 SD 的辨别能力 (TLR2: AUC 0.833;TLR4: AUC 0.810)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DWS 期间单核细胞 TLR2/4 表达升高可识别患者在临床失代偿前有进展为 重症登革热 的风险,为早期风险分层提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些基于机制的生物标志物为流行地区的即时风险分层提供了基础,有可能改善 重症登革热 的早期干预和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖于仅抽象的证据基础而受到限制,并且研究结果的普遍性可能受到所研究的特定人群和地理位置的限制。

Monocyte TLR2/4 as predictive biomarkers for progression from dengue warning signs to severe disease

Authors: Shiqian Chen, Peng Ye, Peiao Rao, Tiantian Sun, Jiali Qi, Ying Zheng, Xiao-Qiang Yu, Fan Zou, Xihua Fu, Ruonan Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Background The World Health Organization (WHO) classification system, comprising dengue fever (DF), dengue with warning signs (DWS), and severe dengue (SD), has been widely adopted globally; however, clinical management remains challenging. Current warning sign criteria lack specificity, frequently failing to distinguish patients progressing to severe disease from those with transient illness, thereby contributing to delayed interventions and missed therapeutic windows. Although Toll-like receptors (TLRs) mediate innate antiviral immunity and are implicated in severe dengue pathogenesis, their expression dynamics during the critical DWS transition window, a phase in which intervention could prevent progression to severe disease, remain entirely unknown. Methods An integrated biomarker discovery study was conducted, comprising bioinformatics analysis of publicly available single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) data (GSE220969) and experimental validation in an independent clinical cohort of 45 dengue patients and 10 healthy controls (H) in Guangdong Province, China. Phase-specific immune signatures were identified from the scRNA-seq data. TLRs expression was quantified by multiparameter flow cytometry. Diagnostic performance was assessed using ROC curve analysis, and correlations with clinical laboratory parameters were evaluated using Spearman’s rank correlation. Results Bioinformatics analysis of 23 cell clusters revealed that TLR2/4/7/8 expression peaked specifically during the DWS phase, predominantly in monocytes. Flow cytometry confirmed elevated surface TLR2 (AUC 0.874) and TLR4 (AUC 0.763) in DWS versus other phases, with discriminatory capacity to distinguish DWS from SD (TLR2: AUC 0.833; TLR4: AUC 0.810). Moreover, correlation analysis of TLRs expression with clinical indicators demonstrated that the expression levels of TLR2 and TLR4 were associated with the development of SD. Conclusions Elevated monocyte TLR2/4 expression during DWS identifies patients at risk for progression to severe dengue before clinical decompensation. These mechanism-informed biomarkers provide a foundation for point-of-care risk stratification in endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of distinguishing patients with dengue warning signs (DWS) who would progress to severe dengue from those with transient illness, as current warning sign criteria lack specificity.
Design and population
An integrated biomarker discovery study was conducted using bioinformatics analysis of scRNA-seq data and experimental validation in a clinical cohort of 45 dengue patients and 10 healthy controls in Guangdong Province, China.
Core methods
TLRs expression was quantified by multiparameter flow cytometry, and diagnostic performance was assessed using ROC curve analysis, with correlations evaluated using Spearman’s rank correlation.
Main results
Bioinformatics analysis revealed that TLR2/4/7/8 expression peaked during the DWS phase, predominantly in monocytes. Flow cytometry confirmed elevated surface TLR2 (AUC 0.874) and TLR4 (AUC 0.763) in DWS versus other phases, with discriminatory capacity to distinguish DWS from SD (TLR2: AUC 0.833; TLR4: AUC 0.810).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that elevated monocyte TLR2/4 expression during DWS identifies patients at risk for progression to severe dengue before clinical decompensation, offering a novel approach for early risk stratification.
Scientific and public-health significance
These mechanism-informed biomarkers provide a foundation for point-of-care risk stratification in endemic regions, potentially improving early interventions and outcomes for severe dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on an abstract-only evidence base, and the generalizability of findings may be constrained by the specific population and geographic location studied.

基于混合代理的城市登革热 传播 模型:哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔的城市特定适应和验证

A Hybrid Agent-Based Model of Urban Dengue Transmission: City Specific Adaptation and Validation in Santa Marta, Colombia
作者： Paula Escudero, Luisa F. Londoño, Sara M. Cano, Gabriel Parra-Henao
摘要中文翻译登革热和其他埃及伊蚊传播疾病的城市 传播 是由 媒介 生态、人类流动性、气候和空间异质性的相互作用形成的。要捕获特定城市环境中的这些相互作用,需要足够详细的模型来表示本地 传播 过程,同时保持校准、验证和敏感性分析的计算可行性。本研究提出了一种由高性能计算 (HPC) 支持的基于混合代理的建模和模拟 (HABMS) 方法,用于模拟城市 媒介 传播的疾病 传播。人类居民被表示为具有随机感染动态的移动代理,而蚊子种群则通过离散时间方程在斑块级别上表示。该模型结合了地理空间结构、温度、基于土地利用的人类运动和当地上下文信息来表示城市环境中的 传播。该框架适用于哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔,作为特定城市的案例研究。传播 参数使用基于代理的贝叶斯优化进行校准,并通过敏感性分析评估其影响。高性能计算使得校准和灵敏度分析所需的大量随机模拟变得可行。校准模型再现了观察到的登革热疫情的规模和主要季节形状,包括高峰期和早期下降期。然而,该模型并未完全重现季节后期的低 发病率 尾部,这表明需要在未来版本中纳入感染的外部引入和降雨驱动的 媒介 招募的季节性强迫。在校准基线上运行的控制场景,幼虫承载能力减少了 30%,将模拟季节性攻击率降低了约四分之三,并将系统移至本地 传播 自我维持的阈值以下,说明了校准模型支持的相对比较。这项研究提供了一种适应性强的混合模型架构和高性能实现,使特定城市的校准和敏感性分析在计算上可行,并通过圣玛尔塔的案例研究证明了这一点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
城市登革热 传播 涉及 媒介 生态、人类流动性、气候和空间异质性之间的复杂相互作用,但现有模型往往缺乏特定城市校准和验证所需的细节。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种具有高性能计算的基于代理的混合建模和模拟 (HABMS) 框架,用于模拟城市登革热 传播,并将其应用于哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔的城市特定案例研究。
核心方法
人类居民被建模为具有随机感染动态的移动代理,而蚊子种群则使用离散时间方程在斑块级别上表示,并结合了地理空间结构、温度、基于土地利用的人类运动和当地背景信息。
主要结果
校准模型再现了在圣玛尔塔观察到的登革热疫情的强度和主要季节形状,包括峰值和早期下降,但没有完全捕捉到季节后期的低发病率尾部。
解释与创新
作者将该模型的性能解释为证明了使用混合架构进行特定于城市的校准和敏感性分析的可行性,同时指出需要结合外部感染引入和降雨驱动的 媒介 招募来改善季末适应性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性地减少幼虫携带能力可以大大降低季节性登革热发病率,并使 传播 低于自我维持阈值,从而为当地的 媒介 控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该模型无法重现季末尾部,并且缺乏针对外部引入的明确验证,这限制了信心,尽管计算可行性,但仍需要适度的证据强度。

A Hybrid Agent-Based Model of Urban Dengue Transmission: City Specific Adaptation and Validation in Santa Marta, Colombia

Authors: Paula Escudero, Luisa F. Londoño, Sara M. Cano, Gabriel Parra-Henao
Original abstract (English)
Urban transmission of dengue and other Aedes aegypti-borne diseases is shaped by the interaction of vector ecology, human mobility, climate, and spatial heterogeneity. Capturing these interactions in city-specific settings requires models that are detailed enough to represent local transmission processes, while remaining computationally feasible for calibration, validation, and sensitivity analysis. This study presents a hybrid agent-based modeling and simulation (HABMS) approach, supported by high-performance computing (HPC), for simulating urban vector-borne disease transmission. Human residents are represented as mobile agents with stochastic infection dynamics, while mosquito populations are represented at the patch level through discrete-time equations. The model incorporates geospatial structure, temperature, land-use-based human movement, and local contextual information to represent transmission within urban environments. The framework was applied to Santa Marta, Colombia, as a city-specific case study. Transmission parameters were calibrated using surrogate-based Bayesian optimization, and their influence was assessed through sensitivity analysis. High-performance computing made the large number of stochastic simulations required for calibration and sensitivity analysis feasible. The calibrated model reproduced the magnitude and main seasonal shape of the observed dengue epidemic, including the peak and early decline. However, the model did not fully reproduce the late low-incidence tail of the season, indicating the need to incorporate external introductions of infection and rainfall-driven seasonal forcing of vector recruitment in future versions. A control scenario run on the calibrated baseline, a 30% reduction in larval carrying capacity, lowered the simulated seasonal attack rate by about three quarters and moved the system below the threshold at which local transmission is self-sustaining, illustrating the relative comparisons the calibrated model supports. This study contributes an adaptable hybrid model architecture and a high-performance implementation that make city-specific calibration and sensitivity analysis computationally feasible, demonstrated through a case study in Santa Marta.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Urban dengue transmission involves complex interactions among vector ecology, human mobility, climate, and spatial heterogeneity, but existing models often lack the detail needed for city-specific calibration and validation.
Design and population
The study developed a hybrid agent-based modeling and simulation (HABMS) framework with high-performance computing to simulate urban dengue transmission, applying it as a city-specific case study in Santa Marta, Colombia.
Core methods
Human residents were modeled as mobile agents with stochastic infection dynamics, while mosquito populations were represented at the patch level using discrete-time equations, incorporating geospatial structure, temperature, land-use-based human movement, and local contextual information.
Main results
The calibrated model reproduced the magnitude and main seasonal shape of the observed dengue epidemic in Santa Marta, including the peak and early decline, but did not fully capture the late low-incidence tail of the season.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the model’s performance as demonstrating the feasibility of city-specific calibration and sensitivity analysis using a hybrid architecture, while noting the need to incorporate external infection introductions and rainfall-driven vector recruitment to improve late-season fit.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted reductions in larval carrying capacity could substantially lower seasonal dengue attack rates and push transmission below self-sustaining thresholds, informing local vector control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The model’s inability to reproduce the late-season tail and the absence of explicit validation against external introductions limit confidence, warranting a moderate evidence strength despite computational feasibility.

儿童肥胖与初次感染后 登革病毒 结合增加有关,但与中和抗体反应无关

Obesity in children is associated with increased dengue virus binding, but not neutralizing, antibody responses following primary infection
作者： Reinaldo Mercado-Hernandez, Sandra Bos, Guillermina Kuan, Angel Balmaseda, Eva Harris, Á. Balmaseda
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肥胖与 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和疾病的较高风险有关,但在本研究之前,肥胖对 DENV 抗体反应的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究分析了来自尼加拉瓜儿童登革热 队列研究 研究的 85 名在 2019 年经历过原发性 DENV 感染的儿童,评估了营养状况或身体脂肪标志物与抗体反应之间的关联。
核心方法
使用针对年龄、性别和 DENV 感染结果进行调整的线性模型来估计体重指数 z 得分 (BMIz)、瘦素、脂联素和 DENV 抗体反应之间的关联。
主要结果
Children with obesity had 1.89-fold higher DENV binding antibodies (95% CI 1.02, 3.48) but no difference in neutralizing antibodies compared to normal-weight children. Leptin was associated with 1.22-fold higher binding antibodies (95% CI 1.09, 1.37), while adiponectin was associated with 0.79-fold lower binding antibodies (95% CI 0.67, 0.94). Lower neutralizing efficiency was observed in children with obesity (FC 0.67, 95% CI 0.48, 0.93).
解释与创新
研究结果表明,原发性 DENV 感染后,肥胖与较高的抗体数量相关,但与较高的抗体质量(中和)无关,这意味着肥胖儿童的中和效率降低。BMIz、瘦素和脂联素模型之间的一致性表明脂肪因子可以补充 BMIz 分析或作为 免疫应答 的独立预测因子。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了在登革热 监测 和 疫苗 策略中考虑营养状况和脂肪因子水平的必要性,因为肥胖可能会影响抗体介导的保护和 疫苗 反应异质性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小、单一队列研究设计以及缺乏机制探索的限制,限制了普遍性和因果推理。由于这些限制和分析的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Obesity in children is associated with increased dengue virus binding, but not neutralizing, antibody responses following primary infection

Authors: Reinaldo Mercado-Hernandez, Sandra Bos, Guillermina Kuan, Angel Balmaseda, Eva Harris, Á. Balmaseda
Original abstract (English)
Obesity has been associated with higher risk of dengue virus (DENV) infection and disease, yet its influence on antibody responses to DENV remains undefined. We evaluated whether nutritional status -- based on BMI z-score (BMIz) -- or blood markers of body fat -- leptin and adiponectin --are associated with binding and/or neutralizing antibody responses to DENV in 85 children in the Nicaraguan Pediatric Dengue Cohort Study who experienced a primary DENV infection in 2019. Associations were estimated using linear models adjusting for age, sex, and DENV infection outcome. Compared to children with normal weight, those with obesity had higher quantities of DENV binding antibodies (fold-change [FC] 1.89, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.02, 3.48) but no difference in neutralizing antibodies. Likewise, leptin concentration was associated with higher quantities of binding antibodies (FC 1.22, 95%CI 1.09, 1.37), while adiponectin was associated with lower quantities (FC 0.79, 95%CI 0.67, 0.94), and neither was associated with neutralizing antibodies. Lower neutralizing efficiency (neutralizing/binding antibodies) was observed in children with obesity (FC 0.67, 95%CI 0.48, 0.93). Our results indicate that obesity is associated with higher antibody quantity (binding) but not higher quality (neutralization) post-primary DENV infection -- implying that antibodies generated by children with obesity have lower neutralization efficiency, requiring greater quantities to reach similar levels of neutralization than children with normal weight. Further, the agreement among the three models using distinct proxies of body fat -- BMIz, leptin, and adiponectin -- demonstrates that adipokines are useful in supplementing BMIz analysis or as independent predictors of immune responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Obesity has been linked to higher risk of dengue virus (DENV) infection and disease, but its impact on antibody responses to DENV remained unclear before this study.
Design and population
The study analyzed 85 children from the Nicaraguan Pediatric Dengue Cohort Study who experienced a primary DENV infection in 2019, evaluating associations between nutritional status or body fat markers and antibody responses.
Core methods
Associations between body mass index z-score (BMIz), leptin, adiponectin, and DENV antibody responses were estimated using linear models adjusted for age, sex, and DENV infection outcome.
Main results
Children with obesity had 1.89-fold higher DENV binding antibodies (95% CI 1.02, 3.48) but no difference in neutralizing antibodies compared to normal-weight children. Leptin was associated with 1.22-fold higher binding antibodies (95% CI 1.09, 1.37), while adiponectin was associated with 0.79-fold lower binding antibodies (95% CI 0.67, 0.94). Lower neutralizing efficiency was observed in children with obesity (FC 0.67, 95% CI 0.48, 0.93).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that obesity is associated with higher antibody quantity but not higher quality (neutralization) after primary DENV infection, implying reduced neutralization efficiency in children with obesity. The agreement among BMIz, leptin, and adiponectin models indicates adipokines may supplement BMIz analysis or serve as independent predictors of immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need to consider nutritional status and adipokine levels in dengue surveillance and vaccine strategies, as obesity may influence antibody-mediated protection and vaccine response heterogeneity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size, single-cohort design, and lack of mechanistic exploration, which constrains generalizability and causal inference. The evidence is moderate due to these constraints and the observational nature of the analysis.

登革热蚊子中气味受体的共表达和多重表达

Odorant receptor coexpression and multi-expression in the dengue mosquito
作者： Elisha David, Vitor L. dos Anjos, Sumer M. Kotb, Melanie Edwards, Hillery C. Metz, David Tian, Zhilei Zhao, Jessica L. Zung, Noah H. Rose, Carolyn S. McBride
摘要中文翻译果蝇嗅觉感觉神经元倾向于表达单一配体特异性受体,遵循每个神经元一个受体的规则,预计广泛适用于昆虫。最近的研究挑战了这一观点,表明埃及伊蚊的一些神经元同时表达多种受体。然而,Ae 中共表达的全部范围和性质。埃及伊蚊仍不清楚。我们对 46,000 个雌性触角神经元的转录组进行了测序,以解析所有嗅觉神经元亚型并鉴定表达的受体。所有亚型的一半共表达多种受体。然而,共表达几乎只发生在来自同一家族的受体之间。气味受体与离子型受体的跨家族共表达是罕见的。我们还记录了一种称为多重表达的现象,其中一种受体在多种神经元亚型中表达,通常与远处的旁系同源物一起表达。果蝇提供了共表达和多重表达的罕见例子,表明果蝇和伊蚊并没有本质上的不同,而是位于可能包含不同昆虫的连续体上的不同位置。已知登革热蚊子嗅觉神经元表现出复杂的受体表达模式,但这种复杂性的细节仍不清楚。在这里,作者建立了一个全面的触角神经元分子图谱,显示其中一半表达多种受体,并且一些受体在多个神经元中表达。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究登革热蚊子嗅觉受体表达的复杂性,挑战在果蝇中观察到的传统每个神经元一个受体的规则。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是登革热蚊子埃及伊蚊,分析了 46,000 个雌性触角神经元的转录组,以确定受体表达模式。
核心方法
作者对 46,000 个雌性触角神经元的转录组进行了测序,以解析所有嗅觉神经元亚型并鉴定表达的受体。
主要结果
所有亚型的一半共表达多种受体,并且一些受体在多种神经元亚型中表达,通常与远处的旁系同源物一起表达。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,登革热蚊子表现出与果蝇相似的受体表达模式连续体,表明昆虫具有更广泛的生物学趋势。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对登革热蚊子嗅觉感知的分子机制的见解,这可以为 媒介 控制和疾病预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于转录组数据,不包括功能验证,但由于生成了全面的分子图谱,证据是强有力的。

Odorant receptor coexpression and multi-expression in the dengue mosquito

Authors: Elisha David, Vitor L. dos Anjos, Sumer M. Kotb, Melanie Edwards, Hillery C. Metz, David Tian, Zhilei Zhao, Jessica L. Zung, Noah H. Rose, Carolyn S. McBride
Original abstract (English)
Drosophila olfactory sensory neurons tend to express a single ligand-specific receptor, following a one-receptor-per-neuron rule expected to apply broadly across insects. Recent work challenges this view by showing that some neurons in the mosquito Aedes aegypti coexpress multiple receptors. However, the full extent and nature of coexpression in Ae. aegypti remains unclear. We sequenced the transcriptomes of 46,000 female antennal neurons to resolve all olfactory neuron subtypes and identify expressed receptors. Half of all subtypes coexpress multiple receptors. However, coexpression occurs almost exclusively among receptors from the same family; cross-family coexpression of odorant receptors with ionotropic receptors is rare. We also document a phenomenon called multi-expression, wherein one receptor is expressed in multiple neuron subtypes, often alongside distant paralogs. Drosophila provides rare examples of both coexpression and multi-expression, suggesting that Drosophila and Aedes are not fundamentally different, but instead located at different positions along a continuum likely to encompass diverse insects. Dengue mosquito olfactory neurons are known to show complex patterns of receptor expression, but the details of this complexity remain unclear. Here, the authors build a comprehensive molecular atlas of antennal neurons, showing that half express multiple receptors and that some receptors are expressed in multiple neurons.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the complexity of olfactory receptor expression in dengue mosquitoes, challenging the traditional one-receptor-per-neuron rule observed in Drosophila.
Design and population
The research focused on the dengue mosquito, Aedes aegypti, analyzing the transcriptomes of 46,000 female antennal neurons to identify receptor expression patterns.
Core methods
The authors sequenced the transcriptomes of 46,000 female antennal neurons to resolve all olfactory neuron subtypes and identify expressed receptors.
Main results
Half of all subtypes coexpress multiple receptors, and some receptors are expressed in multiple neuron subtypes, often alongside distant paralogs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that dengue mosquitoes exhibit a similar continuum of receptor expression patterns to Drosophila, indicating a broader biological trend across insects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms underlying olfactory perception in dengue mosquitoes, which could inform strategies for vector control and disease prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to transcriptomic data and does not include functional validation, but the evidence is strong due to the comprehensive molecular atlas generated.

使用多变量分析预测城市地区 媒介 传播的登革热疾病 暴发

Prognosis of vector borne dengue disease outbreak in urban areas using multivariate analysis
作者： Pratik S. Machchar, Purvi N. Ramanuj, Rajan Patel, Jitendra Bhatia, Kuntesh Jani
摘要中文翻译媒介 登革热等传播疾病继续对巴西、秘鲁和印度等热带地区的气候敏感型公共卫生构成重大挑战。本研究探讨了使用来自波多黎各圣胡安 (SJ) 和秘鲁伊基托斯 (IQ) 的每周多元时间序列数据预测登革热 暴发 的可行性。登革热 发病率 与气象、环境和植被变量一起进行分析,以捕捉关键的气候影响。评估了多种机器学习和深度学习方法,包括 LightGBM。使用均方根误差(RMSE)和平均绝对误差(MAE)评估模型性能。结果表明,LightGBM 实现了最低的 RMSE/MAE,表明其具有很强的短期预测准确性和出色的可解释性。特征重要性分析和主成分分析 (PCA) 确定降水量、露点温度和湿度是登革热 发病率 最有影响力的预测因素。该研究表明,先进的机器学习模型可以作为 媒介 传播疾病的可靠早期预警系统。虽然这项研究的重点是登革热,但该方法适用于其他 媒介 骨数据集和疾病,为公共卫生当局提供了一个灵活的工具,可以在不同的城市环境中预测和减轻 暴发 的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了热带地区登革热(一种气候敏感的 媒介 传播疾病)持续造成的公共卫生负担,并询问多元时间序列模型是否可以预测城市登革热 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究分析了波多黎各圣胡安和秘鲁伊基托斯的每周多变量时间序列数据,以预测登革热 暴发。
核心方法
使用机器学习和深度学习方法(包括 LightGBM)对登革热 发病率 与气象、环境和植被变量进行建模,并通过均方根误差和平均绝对误差评估性能。
主要结果
LightGBM 实现了最低的 RMSE 和 MAE,表明短期预测精度很高,可解释性极佳。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明先进的机器学习模型可以作为可靠的登革热预警系统,通过特征重要性分析将降水、露点温度和湿度确定为关键预测因子。
科研与公卫意义
该方法适用于其他 媒介 传播的数据集和疾病,为公共卫生当局提供了一个灵活的工具来预测和缓解不同城市环境中的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告研究局限性、样本量、两个城市以外的地理范围或针对外部数据集的验证,这限制了对普遍性的信心。

Prognosis of vector borne dengue disease outbreak in urban areas using multivariate analysis

Authors: Pratik S. Machchar, Purvi N. Ramanuj, Rajan Patel, Jitendra Bhatia, Kuntesh Jani
Original abstract (English)
Vector borne disease like dengue continues to pose a significant climate-sensitive public health challenge in tropical regions such as Brazil, Peru, and India. This study examines the feasibility of predicting dengue outbreaks using weekly multivariate time-series data from San Juan (SJ), Puerto Rico and Iquitos (IQ), Peru. Dengue incidence was analyzed alongside meteorological, environmental, and vegetation-based variables to capture key climatic influences. Several machine learning and deep learning approaches were evaluated, including LightGBM. Model performance was assessed using root mean square error (RMSE) and mean absolute error (MAE). The results show that LightGBM achieved the low est RMSE/MAE, indicating strong short-term predictive accuracy and excellent interpretability. Feature importance analysis and principal component analysis (PCA) identified precipitation, dew point temperature, and humidity as the most influential predictors of dengue incidence. The study demonstrates that advanced machine learning models can serve as reliable early warning systems for vector-borne diseases. While this research focuses on dengue, the methodology is adaptable to other vector-bone datasets and diseases, offering a flexible tool for public health authorities to predict and mitigate outbreaks in diverse urban contexts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent public-health burden of dengue, a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease, in tropical regions and asks whether multivariate time-series models can predict urban dengue outbreaks.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly multivariate time-series data from San Juan, Puerto Rico and Iquitos, Peru to predict dengue outbreaks.
Core methods
Dengue incidence was modeled alongside meteorological, environmental, and vegetation-based variables using machine learning and deep learning approaches, including LightGBM, with performance assessed by root mean square error and mean absolute error.
Main results
LightGBM achieved the lowest RMSE and MAE, indicating strong short-term predictive accuracy and excellent interpretability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the results as demonstrating that advanced machine learning models can serve as reliable early warning systems for dengue, with feature importance analysis identifying precipitation, dew point temperature, and humidity as key predictors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The methodology is presented as adaptable to other vector-borne datasets and diseases, offering a flexible tool for public health authorities to predict and mitigate outbreaks in diverse urban contexts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations, sample size, geographic scope beyond the two cities, or validation against external datasets, which constrains confidence in generalizability.

气象和环境卫生因素对登革热发生影响的思考登革热天气和环境卫生的因素

REFLEXÕES SOBRE A INFLUÊNCIA DE FATORES METEOROLÓGICOS E DE SANEAMENTO AMBIENTAL NA OCORRÊNCIA DA DENGUE REFLECTIONS ON THE INFLUENCE OF METEOROLOGICAL AND ENVIRONMENTAL SANITATION FACTORS ON THE OCCURRENCE OF DENGUE REFLEXIONES SOBRE LA INFLUENCIA DE LOS FACTORES METEOROLÓGICOS Y DE SANEAMIENTO AMBIENTAL EN LA APARICIÓN DEL DENGUE
作者： Rogério Gomes Matias, Maria Julia da Silva Oliveira, Emile Menezes Santos Cruz, Beatriz Santana Pereira Moraes, Thiago Pires Santana, Ana Tereza Costa Silva, Aloísio Machado da Silva Filho, Gilney Figueira Zebende
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热是一种持续影响全球的主要虫媒病毒,它具有气候条件和环境卫生的潜力,有利于蚊子传播者埃及伊蚊的生活。目的:本文是与登革热环境因素影响相关的大学扩展教育基础的经验相关的文本。方法:以教育活动的发展为基础,以公共教育的形式,以桑塔纳-巴市的城市形式为学生提供教育活动。积极开展对话、互动测验应用等相关材料教学活动;通过问题来映射社会环境,可以使用文件夹中的材料信息,以获取官方信息。结果:展示了教学的最大潜力,并全面应用了测验以促进激烈的参与。学生们发现了一些与周围环境有关的健康状况的条件。但是,请注意观察无法进行定量分析的问题。结论:我们的成果是最有效地宣传和打击登革热传播者的蚊子传播途径,有利于加强相关性和条件。在此,我们将提供证据,以证明大学和学校的整合对于持续教育建设的重要性,包括与教育协会的合作伙伴的理解和减少。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了登革热(一种蚊媒虫媒病毒性疾病)在全球范围内持续造成的负担,并强调环境卫生缺陷和气候条件是埃及伊蚊扩散和 传播 的驱动因素。它提出了教育干预的必要性,以提高学龄人口对这些风险因素的认识。
设计与对象
该研究报告了在巴西巴伊亚州费拉德桑塔纳为公立学校学生举办的一项大学推广活动,作为教育讲座。该活动针对基础教育环境,讨论影响登革热发生的环境因素。
核心方法
干预措施包括对话讲座,辅以互动测验活动、通过问卷绘制社会环境图以及分发信息文件夹。材料的准备和交付是为了增强对讲座形式之外的官方信息的获取。
主要结果
该活动展示了很高的教学潜力,特别是通过引发自发参与的互动测验。然而,对书面调查问卷的遵守率较低,阻碍了对社会环境条件的定量分析。
解释与创新
作者将此活动解释为在学校内提高认识和抗击登革热 媒介、扩大对危险因素的了解的有效策略。他们强调整合大学和学校的努力以促进持续教育行动的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,以学校为基础的教育干预措施可以增强社区对登革热危险因素的了解,并支持 媒介 控制工作。他们强调大学和学校之间的伙伴关系对于持续公共卫生教育的价值。
局限与证据强度
由于问卷答复率低以及缺乏针对具体目标的实验室或流行病学测量,导致缺乏定量数据,该研究的证据强度受到限制。该设计限制了当地教育背景之外的普遍性。

REFLEXÕES SOBRE A INFLUÊNCIA DE FATORES METEOROLÓGICOS E DE SANEAMENTO AMBIENTAL NA OCORRÊNCIA DA DENGUE REFLECTIONS ON THE INFLUENCE OF METEOROLOGICAL AND ENVIRONMENTAL SANITATION FACTORS ON THE OCCURRENCE OF DENGUE REFLEXIONES SOBRE LA INFLUENCIA DE LOS FACTORES METEOROLÓGICOS Y DE SANEAMIENTO AMBIENTAL EN LA APARICIÓN DEL DENGUE

Authors: Rogério Gomes Matias, Maria Julia da Silva Oliveira, Emile Menezes Santos Cruz, Beatriz Santana Pereira Moraes, Thiago Pires Santana, Ana Tereza Costa Silva, Aloísio Machado da Silva Filho, Gilney Figueira Zebende
Original abstract (English)
Introdução: A dengue é uma das arboviroses de maior persistência com impacto global, cuja ocorrência da doença é potencializada por condições climáticas e precariedade do saneamento ambiental que favorecem o ciclo de vida do mosquito transmissor Aedes aegypti. Objetivo: Este texto tem por objetivo relatar a experiência de uma ação de extensão universitária voltada à educação básica para discutir a influência de fatores ambientais na ocorrência da dengue. Metodologia: Foi planejada uma ação de extensão para o desenvolvimento de atividades educativas no formato de palestra voltada para estudantes da rede pública de ensino, no município de Feira de Santana-Ba. Atividade foi estruturada a partir do levantamento de materiais didáticos para implementação da palestra, que ocorreu de forma dialogada, foi proposto à aplicação de quiz interativo; e mapeamento socioambiental via questionário, além da utilização de materiais informativos como folders, para ampliar o acesso a informações oficiais além da palestra. Resultados: Atividade demonstrou alta potencialidade pedagógica, sobretudo quanto aplicação do quiz que promoveu intensa participação espontânea. Os relatos dos estudantes revelam um cenário de vulnerabilidade quanto as condições de saneamento ambiental que rodeiam suas moradias. Além disso, observou-se baixa adesão ao questionário escrito impossibilitando uma análise quantitativa. Conclusão: Diante dos resultados a ação de extensão mostrou-se uma estratégia eficaz para a promoção da conscientização e combate ao mosquito transmissor da dengue no âmbito escolar, favorecendo a ampliação do conhecimento e dos seus fatores condicionantes. Dessa forma, evidencia-se a importância da integração entre universidade e escola na construção de ações educativas contínuas, para compreensão e redução dos fatores de risco associados à doença.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent global burden of dengue, a mosquito-borne arboviral disease, and highlights environmental sanitation deficits and climatic conditions as drivers of Aedes aegypti proliferation and transmission. It frames the need for educational interventions to raise awareness of these risk factors among school-aged populations.
Design and population
The study reports a university extension activity conducted as an educational lecture for public school students in Feira de Santana, Bahia, Brazil. The activity targeted basic education settings to discuss environmental factors influencing dengue occurrence.
Core methods
The intervention consisted of a dialogic lecture supplemented by interactive quiz activities, socio-environmental mapping via questionnaire, and distribution of informational folders. Materials were prepared and delivered to enhance access to official information beyond the lecture format.
Main results
The activity demonstrated high pedagogical potential, particularly through the interactive quiz that elicited spontaneous participation. However, low adherence to the written questionnaire prevented quantitative analysis of socio-environmental conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the activity as an effective strategy for raising awareness and combating the dengue vector within schools, expanding knowledge of risk factors. They emphasize the novelty of integrating university and school efforts to foster continuous educational actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that school-based educational interventions can enhance community understanding of dengue risk factors and support vector control efforts. They underscore the value of partnerships between universities and schools for sustained public health education.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s evidence strength is limited by the absence of quantitative data due to low questionnaire response rates and the lack of target-specific laboratory or epidemiological measurements. The design restricts generalizability beyond the local educational context.

学校预防登革热的恶作剧:库里蒂巴小学生登革热预防的有趣方法:库里蒂巴课程扩展经验的记录

A LUDICIDADE NA PREVENÇÃO DA DENGUE EM ESCOLARES: RELATO DE EXPERIÊNCIA DE CURRICULARIZAÇÃO DA EXTENSÃO EM CURITIBA PLAYFUL APPROACHES TO DENGUE PREVENTION IN SCHOOLCHILDREN: AN ACCOUNT OF CURRICULAR EXTENSION EXPERIENCE IN CURITIBA
作者： João Vitor Takehiko Rodrigues Yryu, Roberta Graf Beninca, Ana Luiza Lemos de Albuquerque, Beatriz Mayara Oliveira Castro, Júlia Laurentino Silveira, Maria Eduarda Adachi de Menezes
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是巴西的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要针对弱势群体采取持续的健康教育策略。本文报道了 2024 年第一学期在库里蒂巴卡朱鲁社区的 Escola Municipal Irati 对 32 名二年级小学生进行的以登革热预防为重点的健康教育推广经验,该经验与 Faculdades Pequeno Principe 医学课程的教学和社区融合课程单元相关。该干预措施将对话与一项名为“登革热通报”的有趣活动结合起来,在活动中,孩子们识别了校园内埃及伊蚊的繁殖地。观察到良好的依从性、对预防行为的逐步理解和高参与度。这次经历凸显了有趣的方法在促进儿童健康方面的潜力,并强化了课程扩展在塑造具有社会责任感的医疗专业人员方面的作用。

登革热 仍然是巴西的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要针对弱势群体采取持续的健康教育策略。

A LUDICIDADE NA PREVENÇÃO DA DENGUE EM ESCOLARES: RELATO DE EXPERIÊNCIA DE CURRICULARIZAÇÃO DA EXTENSÃO EM CURITIBA PLAYFUL APPROACHES TO DENGUE PREVENTION IN SCHOOLCHILDREN: AN ACCOUNT OF CURRICULAR EXTENSION EXPERIENCE IN CURITIBA

Authors: João Vitor Takehiko Rodrigues Yryu, Roberta Graf Beninca, Ana Luiza Lemos de Albuquerque, Beatriz Mayara Oliveira Castro, Júlia Laurentino Silveira, Maria Eduarda Adachi de Menezes
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a significant public health problem in Brazil, requiring ongoing health education strategies for vulnerable populations. This article reports a health education extension experience focused on dengue prevention, conducted with 32 second-grade elementary school students at Escola Municipal Irati, in the Cajuru neighborhood of Curitiba, during the first semester of 2024, linked to the Teaching and Community Integration curricular unit of the Medicine course at Faculdades Pequeno Principe. The intervention combined a dialogued conversation with a playful activity called Dengue Informant, in which children identified Aedes aegypti breeding sites across the school yard. Good adherence, progressive understanding of preventive behaviors, and high engagement were observed. The experience highlighted the potential of playful methodologies for child health promotion and reinforced the role of curricular extension in shaping socially committed medical professionals.

This article reports a health education extension experience focused on dengue prevention, conducted with 32 second-grade elementary school students at Escola Municipal Irati, in the Cajuru neighborhood of Curitiba, during the first semester of 2024, linked to the Teaching and Community Integration curricular unit of the Medicine course at Faculdades Pequeno Principe.

The supplied evidence does not report study design limitations, sample size justification, or long-term follow-up, limiting the strength of conclusions about sustained behavioral change.
