Kiến thức,thực hành về phòng,chống sốt xuất huyết dengue của sinh viên nội trú Trường Đại học Y Hà Nội năm 2025年知识和实践登革热 2025年河内医科大学宿舍学生预防情况

Kiến thức, thực hành về phòng, chống sốt xuất huyết dengue của sinh viên nội trú Trường Đại học Y Hà Nội năm 2025 Knowledge and practices on dengue Fever Prevention among Domitory Students at Hanoi Medical University in 2025
作者： Thị Kim Nhung Tạ, Lê Linh Giang, Phạm Thị Quân, Nguyễn Ngọc Anh, Phan Thị Mai Hương
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估预防知识和实践,解决了河内大学生中 登革热 的公共卫生问题。其目的是描述河内医科大学宿舍学生当前的登革热相关知识水平和预防措施。
设计与对象
2025年,对河内医科大学402名宿舍学生进行了一项横断面研究。通过在线问卷收集数据,并通过描述性统计和探索性因素分析进行​​分析。
核心方法
使用在线调查问卷收集有关登革热预防知识和实践的数据。应用描述性统计和探索性因素分析对答复进行分类和解释。
主要结果
53.5%的学生表现出良好的登革热预防知识,而只有13.2%的学生表现出良好的预防实践。常见的措施包括使用驱蚊剂(86.1%)、穿长衣(79.1%)、使用蚊帐(74.1%)、检查水容器(84.3%)、收集垃圾(83.6%)和翻转水容器(81.8%)。
解释与创新
尽管预防意识较高,但实际操作水平较低,认识与行为之间存在差距。该研究确定了三个实践领域:积极预防、蚊子和幼虫控制以及个人防护,平均分在2.0到2.9之间。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要有针对性的健康教育和行为改变策略,以提高河内大学生对登革热预防措施的采用。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能引入报告偏差。由于单中心设置和缺乏对报告实践的验证,证据强度中等。

Kiến thức, thực hành về phòng, chống sốt xuất huyết dengue của sinh viên nội trú Trường Đại học Y Hà Nội năm 2025 Knowledge and practices on dengue Fever Prevention among Domitory Students at Hanoi Medical University in 2025

Authors: Thị Kim Nhung Tạ, Lê Linh Giang, Phạm Thị Quân, Nguyễn Ngọc Anh, Phan Thị Mai Hương
Original abstract (English)
Nghiên cứu cắt ngang được thực hiện trên 402 sin h viên nội tr ú Trường Đại h ọc Y H à Nội nhằm mô tảkiế n thức và thực hành phòng chống sốt xuất huyết Dengue (SXHD). Dữ liệu được thu thập bằng bảnghỏi trực tuyến và phân tích bằng thống kê mô tả kết hợp với phân tích nhân tố khám phá. Kết quả cho thấy53,5% sinh viên có kiến thức tốt, trong khi chỉ 13,2% có thực hành phòng bệnh tốt. Phần lớn sinh viênbiết các biện pháp phòng chống SXHD như bôi thuốc chống muỗi (86,1%), mặc quần áo dài (79,1%), ngủmàn chống muỗi (74,1%), kiểm tra dụng cụ chứa nước (84,3%), thu gom phế thải (83,6%) và lật úp dụngcụ chứa nước (81,8%). Tuy nhiên, kiến thức về sử dụng hóa chất diệt bọ gậy còn hạn chế (49,0%). Phântích nhân tố khám phá xác định 3 nhóm thực hành gồm: “Thực hành phòng chống chủ động”, “Kiểm soátmuỗi và bọ gậy” và “Phòng tránh cá nhân”, với điểm trung bình từ 2,0 đến 2,9. Các thực hành được thựchiện ở mức “rất thường xuyên” với tỷ lệ cao hơn là ngủ màn vào ban đêm (12,4%), cọ rửa dụng cụ chứanước (11,2%), mặc quần áo dài và lật úp dụng cụ chứa nước (9,5%). Ngược lại, thả hóa chất diệt bọ gậyvào dụng cụ đọng nước có tỷ lệ thấp nhất, chỉ 2,7%. Kết quả cho thấy mặc dù hơn một nửa sinh viên cókiến thức tốt, thực hành phòng chống SXHD vẫn còn hạn chế. Do đó, cần tăng cường giáo dục sức khỏevà các chiến lược thay đổi hành vi để thúc đẩy việc áp dụng bền vững các biện pháp phòng ngừa sốt xuấthuyết trong sinh viên đại học.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of dengue fever among university students in Hanoi by assessing knowledge and practices for its prevention. It aimed to describe current levels of dengue-related knowledge and preventive practices among dormitory students at Hanoi Medical University.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 402 dormitory students at Hanoi Medical University in 2025. Data were collected using an online questionnaire and analyzed with descriptive statistics and exploratory factor analysis.
Core methods
An online questionnaire was used to collect data on knowledge and practices regarding dengue prevention. Descriptive statistics and exploratory factor analysis were applied to categorize and interpret the responses.
Main results
53.5% of students demonstrated good knowledge of dengue prevention, while only 13.2% exhibited good preventive practices. Commonly known measures included using mosquito repellent (86.1%), wearing long clothing (79.1%), using bed nets (74.1%), checking water containers (84.3%), waste collection (83.6%), and turning over water containers (81.8%).
Interpretation and novelty
Despite high awareness of preventive measures, actual practice levels were low, indicating a gap between knowledge and behavior. The study identified three practice domains: active prevention, mosquito and larval control, and personal protection, with average scores ranging from 2.0 to 2.9.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted health education and behavior-change strategies to improve the adoption of dengue prevention measures among university students in Hanoi.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit causal inference and may introduce reporting bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the single-center setting and lack of validation of reported practices.

B 细胞受体依赖性增强 登革病毒 感染的分子和生物物理要求

Molecular and biophysical requirements for B cell receptor-dependent enhancement of dengue virus infection
作者： Gaby Madrigal, Chad Gebo, Benjamin D. McElvany, Menelaos Symeonides, Sean A. Diehl, Adam T. Waickman
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 是登革热的病原体,登革热是一种蚊媒疾病,给全世界带来了巨大且日益严重的公共卫生负担。登革热的一个独特的病理生理学特征是免疫介导的增强,其中由原发感染引起的预先存在的免疫力可以增强异源DENV血清型的后续感染的严重性。这种现象的一个主要机制解释是 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),其中亚中和浓度的 DENV 特异性 IgG 抗体有利于 DENV 进入表达 FcγR 的细胞。因此,该模型假设吞噬性单核细胞是 DENV 的主要 储存宿主。然而,多个独立研究小组表明,B 细胞是急性登革热期间循环中病毒感染细胞中最大的 储存宿主,这表明我们对免疫介导的 DENV 趋向性的理解存在脱节。为了应对这种持续存在的知识差距,我们的团队之前发现了一种免疫介导增强的新机制,我们将其称为 DENV 感染的 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性增强 (BDE)。在这项研究中,我们发现 DENV 反应性 B 细胞的 DENV 感染对 BCR/DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白相互作用高度敏感。DENV 进入 B 细胞亚群是由动力介导的,需要近端 BCR 信号传导,并涉及晚期内体的定位。最后,我们证明永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞可以被活 DENV 有效感染,能够支持所有四种血清型的活跃病毒复制。我们认为,BDE 提供了一层额外的病原体特异性免疫介导的感染风险,补充了现有的 ADE 模型,并为 DENV 免疫发病机制的潜在机制提供了更多见解。重要性 这项研究提供了对 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染增强过程的机制见解。免疫介导的感染增强是登革热免疫发病机制的基石,最常归因于 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE)。DENV 感染的 BCR 依赖性增强是免疫介导增强的一种替代和补充机制,它解决了登革热领域中几个持续存在的知识空白,并可能为为什么有些人在感染后进展到 重症登革热 提供机制见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 是登革热的病原体,登革热是一种蚊媒疾病,给全世界带来了巨大且日益严重的公共卫生负担。免疫介导增强的一个主要机制解释是 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),其中亚中和浓度的 DENV 特异性 IgG 抗体有利于 DENV 进入表达 FcγR 的细胞。
设计与对象
本研究调查了 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性增强 DENV 感染的分子和生物物理要求。该研究重点关注 DENV 反应性 B 细胞及其在急性登革热期间与 DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白的相互作用。
核心方法
该研究检查了 DENV 进入 B 细胞的情况,强调它是动力介导的,需要近端 BCR 信号传导,并涉及晚期内体的定位。永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞用于证明活 DENV 的有效感染,支持所有四种血清型的活跃病毒复制。
主要结果
DENV 反应性 B 细胞的 DENV 感染对 BCR/DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白相互作用高度敏感。DENV 进入 B 细胞亚群是由动力介导的,需要近端 BCR 信号传导,并涉及晚期内体的定位。永生化的 DENV 反应性 B 细胞可被活 DENV 有效感染,能够支持所有四种血清型的活跃病毒复制。
解释与创新
作者提出,B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性增强 (BDE) 提供了一层额外的病原体特异性免疫介导的感染风险,补充了现有的 ADE 模型,并为 DENV 免疫发病机制的潜在机制提供了更多见解。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了对 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 依赖性 重症登革热 (DENV) 感染增强过程的机制见解,这可能有助于解释为什么有些人在感染后进展为 重症登革热,并为未来有关 DENV 免疫发病机制的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,这些发现基于对 B 细胞中 DENV 进入和复制的直接观察,支持高置信度评估。

Molecular and biophysical requirements for B cell receptor-dependent enhancement of dengue virus infection

Authors: Gaby Madrigal, Chad Gebo, Benjamin D. McElvany, Menelaos Symeonides, Sean A. Diehl, Adam T. Waickman
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus (DENV) is the causative agent of dengue, a mosquito-borne disease that represents a significant and growing public health burden around the world. A unique pathophysiological feature of dengue is immune-mediated enhancement, wherein preexisting immunity elicited by a primary infection can enhance the severity of a subsequent infection by a heterologous DENV serotype. A leading mechanistic explanation for this phenomenon is antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), where sub-neutralizing concentrations of DENV-specific IgG antibodies facilitate the entry of DENV into FcγR-expressing cells. Accordingly, this model posits that phagocytic mononuclear cells are the primary reservoir of DENV. However, multiple independent groups have shown that B cells are the largest reservoir of virally infected cells in circulation during acute dengue, representing a disconnect in our understanding of immune-mediated DENV tropism. In response to this persistent knowledge gap, our team has previously identified a novel mechanism of immune-mediated enhancement we have termed B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement (BDE) of DENV infection. In this study, we show that DENV infection of DENV-reactive B cells is highly sensitive to BCR/DENV envelope (E) protein interactions. DENV entry into this subset of B cells is dynamin-mediated, requires proximal BCR signaling, and involves localization to late endosomes. Finally, we show that immortalized DENV-reactive B cells are productively infected by live DENV, capable of supporting active viral replication of all four serotypes. We propose that BDE provides an additional layer of pathogen-specific immune-mediated infection risk that complements existing models of ADE and offers additional insight into potential mechanisms of DENV immunopathogenesis. IMPORTANCE This study provides mechanistic insight into the process of B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement of dengue virus (DENV) infection. Immune-mediated enhancement of infection is a cornerstone of dengue immunopathogenesis and is most often attributed to antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). BCR-dependent enhancement of DENV infection is an alternative and complementary mechanism for immune-mediated enhancement that addresses several persistent knowledge gaps in the dengue field and may offer mechanistic insight into why some people progress to severe dengue after infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is the causative agent of dengue, a mosquito-borne disease that represents a significant and growing public health burden around the world. A leading mechanistic explanation for immune-mediated enhancement is antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), where sub-neutralizing concentrations of DENV-specific IgG antibodies facilitate the entry of DENV into FcγR-expressing cells.
Design and population
This study investigates the molecular and biophysical requirements for B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement of DENV infection. The research focuses on DENV-reactive B cells and their interaction with DENV envelope (E) protein during acute dengue.
Core methods
The study examines DENV entry into B cells, highlighting that it is dynamin-mediated, requires proximal BCR signaling, and involves localization to late endosomes. Immortalized DENV-reactive B cells are used to demonstrate productive infection by live DENV, supporting active viral replication of all four serotypes.
Main results
DENV infection of DENV-reactive B cells is highly sensitive to BCR/DENV envelope (E) protein interactions. DENV entry into this subset of B cells is dynamin-mediated, requires proximal BCR signaling, and involves localization to late endosomes. Immortalized DENV-reactive B cells are productively infected by live DENV, capable of supporting active viral replication of all four serotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors propose that B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement (BDE) provides an additional layer of pathogen-specific immune-mediated infection risk that complements existing models of ADE and offers additional insight into potential mechanisms of DENV immunopathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides mechanistic insight into the process of B cell receptor (BCR)-dependent enhancement of dengue virus (DENV) infection, which may help explain why some people progress to severe dengue after infection and inform future research on DENV immunopathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the findings are grounded in the direct observation of DENV entry and replication in B cells, supporting a high confidence assessment.

登革热病毒:结构、致病、免疫应答、诊断和真空

Virus del dengue: estructura, patogenia, respuesta inmunitaria, diagnóstico y vacunas
作者： David José Troya León, Danny Santiago Cangui-Panchi, Willian Patricio Cuenca Poma, Cristina Jackeline Arias Godoy
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热构成了虫媒病毒对世界健康的重大影响。目的:分析登革热病毒结构、病原、免疫应答、诊断和真空的科学证据。方法:通过 PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science、SciELO、Dialnet、Redalyc 和 ERIC 进行文献描述修订和分析,并选择 20 个 PRISMA 协议研究室。结果:有证据表明,病毒具有复制和免疫逃逸的基本功能,但没有证据表明,登革热坟墓中的抗病毒能力和内皮子决定因素的功能障碍,以及分子的预防NS1 允许诊断时间。结论:要有效控制登革热,需要结合警戒、诊断、控制矢量和真空等综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决登革热对公共卫生的重大影响,以及对其结构 发病机制、免疫应答、诊断 和 疫苗 进行更新的科学理解的需要。这项研究的动机是全球登革热疾病负担。
设计与对象
该研究进行了描述性和分析性文献综述,根据 PRISMA 协议选择了 20 项研究。它重点关注 登革病毒 和登革热疾病的相关方面。
核心方法
作者使用 PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science、SciELO、Dialnet、Redalyc 和 ERIC 数据库进行了文献综述。他们按照 PRISMA 协议选择并分析了 20 项研究。
主要结果
结果表明,登革病毒 的结构和非结构蛋白对于复制和免疫逃避至关重要。抗体依赖性增强 和内皮功能障碍被确定为 重症登革热 的关键因素。分子测试和 NS1 抗原检测可实现早期 诊断。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了解病毒结构和免疫相互作用对于制定有效的登革热控制策略的重要性。这项研究全面概述了当前有关登革热的科学知识。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测、早期 诊断 和 疫苗 登革热的开发工作提供信息。他们强调需要采取综合方法来有效管理该疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对现有文献的回顾,不包括原始数据。由于使用 PRISMA 系统地选择了研究,因此证据很充分,但它可能无法反映最新的进展。

Virus del dengue: estructura, patogenia, respuesta inmunitaria, diagnóstico y vacunas

Authors: David José Troya León, Danny Santiago Cangui-Panchi, Willian Patricio Cuenca Poma, Cristina Jackeline Arias Godoy
Original abstract (English)
Introducción: El dengue constituye una de las arbovirosis de mayor impacto en la salud pública mundial. Objetivo: Analizar la evidencia científica reciente sobre la estructura, patogenia, respuesta inmunitaria, diagnóstico y vacunas del virus del dengue. Método: Se realizó una revisión documental descriptiva y analítica mediante la búsqueda en PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, SciELO, Dialnet, Redalyc y ERIC, con selección de 20 estudios según el protocolo PRISMA. Resultados: Se evidenció que las proteínas estructurales y no estructurales del virus cumplen funciones esenciales en la replicación y la evasión inmunitaria, que la potenciación dependiente de anticuerpos y la disfunción endotelial son determinantes en el dengue grave, y que las pruebas moleculares y el antígeno NS1 permiten el diagnóstico temprano. Conclusión: El control efectivo del dengue requiere estrategias integrales que combinen vigilancia, diagnóstico, control vectorial y vacunación.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the significant public health impact of dengue and the need for updated scientific understanding of its structure, pathogenesis, immune response, diagnosis and vaccines. The research was motivated by the global burden of dengue disease.
Design and population
The study conducted a descriptive and analytical documentary review, selecting 20 studies based on the PRISMA protocol. It focused on dengue virus and related aspects of dengue disease.
Core methods
The authors performed a documentary review using PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, SciELO, Dialnet, Redalyc and ERIC databases. They followed the PRISMA protocol to select and analyze 20 studies.
Main results
The results indicated that structural and non-structural proteins of the dengue virus are essential for replication and immune evasion. Antibody-dependent enhancement and endothelial dysfunction were identified as key factors in severe dengue. Molecular tests and NS1 antigen detection enable early diagnosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the importance of understanding the virus's structure and immune interactions to develop effective strategies for dengue control. This study provides a comprehensive overview of current scientific knowledge on dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, early diagnosis and vaccine development efforts for dengue. They emphasize the need for integrated approaches to manage the disease effectively.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a review of existing literature and does not include original data. The evidence is strong due to the systematic selection of studies using PRISMA, but it may not capture the most recent developments.

使用遥感、机器学习和时空模型预测秘鲁特定生态区的登革热风险

Ecoregion-specific dengue risk prediction in Peru using remote sensing, machine learning, and spatiotemporal modeling
作者： Shubham K. Singh, Peilei Fan, Jiquan Chen
摘要中文翻译:由于其高度局部化和周期性以及环境、人口和气候的变化,登革热 一直是秘鲁的一个重大公共卫生问题。虽然许多现有模型可以评估国家一级的登革热风险,但它们往往无法考虑导致 传播 发生率变化的精细景观变化。我们通过整合 20 年(2003-2022 年)登革热 监测 数据的高分辨率气候、土地覆盖和人口变量,开发了 1 公里空间分辨率的时空登革热风险模型。基于树的方法比线性模型实现了更高的预测精度,XGBoost 实现了最高 R 2 值 0.68。此外,当包含空间和时间滞后的预测变量时,模型性能会提高。地表温度和降水对登革热适宜性表现出强烈的邻域效应。具体而言,14个生态区的模型验证存在很强的空间依赖性,其中亚马逊潮湿森林和过渡性云加斯地区的预测精度较高(R 2 = 0.78),而高海拔安第斯普纳和沿海红树林区的预测精度较差,表现差于平均预测,这表明人口流动和进口传播等非气候因素也在登革热感染和传播中发挥作用。总体而言,登革热的可预测性在很大程度上取决于其发生的生态区,国家水平的平均值掩盖了当地的动态。我们的研究表明,与特定生态区域的绩效指标相结合,可进行可扩展的高分辨率风险测绘,以预测和告知针对气候敏感型 媒介 传播疾病的有针对性的 媒介 控制和公共卫生干预措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于其高度局部化和周期性以及环境、人口和气候的变化,登革热 一直是秘鲁的一个重大公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
虽然许多现有模型可以评估国家一级的登革热风险,但它们往往无法考虑导致 传播 发生率变化的精细景观变化。
核心方法
我们通过整合 20 年(2003-2022 年)登革热 监测 数据的高分辨率气候、土地覆盖和人口变量,开发了 1 公里空间分辨率的时空登革热风险模型。
主要结果
基于树的方法比线性模型实现了更高的预测精度,XGBoost 实现了最高 R 2 值 0.68。
解释与创新
此外,当包含空间和时间滞后的预测变量时,模型性能会提高。
科研与公卫意义
地表温度和降水对登革热适宜性表现出强烈的邻域效应。
局限与证据强度
具体而言,14个生态区的模型验证存在很强的空间依赖性,其中亚马逊潮湿森林和过渡性云加斯地区的预测精度较高(R 2 = 0.78),而高海拔安第斯普纳和沿海红树林区的预测精度较差,表现差于平均预测,这表明人口流动和进口传播等非气候因素也在登革热感染和传播中发挥作用。

Ecoregion-specific dengue risk prediction in Peru using remote sensing, machine learning, and spatiotemporal modeling

Authors: Shubham K. Singh, Peilei Fan, Jiquan Chen
Original abstract (English)
: Dengue fever has been a significant public health issue in Peru due to its highly localized and cyclical nature and to changes in the environment, demography, and climate. While many existing models can assess dengue risk at the country level, they often fail to account for the fine-scale landscape variation that drives varying transmission rates. We developed a spatiotemporal dengue risk model at a 1 km spatial resolution by integrating high-resolution climatic, land-cover, and demographic variables across 20 years (2003-2022) of dengue surveillance data. Tree-based approaches achieved higher predictive accuracy than linear models, with XGBoost achieving the highest R 2 value of 0.68. Additionally, model performance increased when spatially and temporally lagged predictors were included. Land surface temperature and precipitation showed strong neighborhood effects on dengue suitability. Specifically, there exists a strong spatial dependency in model validation across 14 ecoregions, with Amazon moist forest and transitional Yungas yielding high predictive accuracy (R 2 = 0.78), while high-altitude Andean Puna and coastal mangrove zones had poor predictive accuracy, performing worse than the mean prediction, suggesting non-climatic factors such as human mobility and imported transmission also play a role in dengue infection and transmissions. Overall, the predictability of dengue is strongly dependent on the ecoregion where it occurs, with national-level averages masking local dynamics. Our research demonstrates the potential for scalable high-resolution risk mapping when combined with ecoregion-specific performance metrics to predict and inform targeted vector control and public health interventions for climate-sensitive vector-borne diseases.

Main results
Tree-based approaches achieved higher predictive accuracy than linear models, with XGBoost achieving the highest R 2 value of 0.68.
Interpretation and novelty
Additionally, model performance increased when spatially and temporally lagged predictors were included.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

Efektivitas Kompres Hangat dan Tepid Water Sponge pada Anak dengan Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever: Studi Kasus

Authors: Fardani Nur Annisa, Irdawati Irdawati
Original abstract (English)
Pendahuluan: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) merupakan penyakit infeksi yang banyak menyerang anak dan ditandai dengan demam tinggi. Selain terapi farmakologis, intervensi non-farmakologis seperti kompres hangat dan teknik tepid sponge sering digunakan, namun bukti perbandingan efektivitas keduanya masih terbatas. Tujuan: Tujuan studi kasus ini adalah untuk mengetahui perbedaan efektivitas kompres hangat konvensional dan teknik tepid sponge dalam menurunkan suhu tubuh pada anak dengan DHF. Metode: Desain studi kasus deskriptif komparatif pada dua pasien anak dengan DHF yang di ruang rawat inap RS Indriati Solo Baru. Masing-masing pasien diberikan intervensi berbeda selama tiga hari. Pengukuran suhu tubuh dilakukan sebelum dan sesudah intervensi menggunakan termometer digital, serta respon subjektif dan objektif dicatat melalui lembar observasi. Analisis data dilakukan secara deskriptif dengan membandingkan perubahan suhu tubuh antar intervensi. Hasil: Kedua intervensi terbukti efektif menurunkan suhu tubuh. Pada pasien dengan kompres hangat, suhu menurun dari 38,9°C menjadi 37,2°C dengan rata-rata penurunan 0,87°C. Pada pasien dengan tepid sponge, suhu menurun dari 39,7°C menjadi 37,4°C dengan rata-rata penurunan 1,20°C. Respon klinis menunjukkan perbaikan kondisi, termasuk penurunan nadi dan peningkatan aktivitas. Simpulan: Teknik tepid sponge lebih efektif dalam menurunkan suhu tubuh dibandingkan kompres hangat, meskipun kompres hangat lebih nyaman bagi pasien anak.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) merupakan penyakit infeksi yang banyak menyerang anak dan ditandai dengan demam tinggi. Studi ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui perbedaan efektivitas kompres hangat konvensional dan teknik tepid sponge dalam menurunkan suhu tubuh pada anak dengan DHF.
Design and population
Studi kasus ini menggunakan desain deskriptif komparatif pada dua pasien anak dengan DHF yang dirawat di ruang rawat inap RS Indriati Solo Baru. Masing-masing pasien diberikan intervensi berbeda selama tiga hari.
Core methods
Pengukuran suhu tubuh dilakukan sebelum dan sesudah intervensi menggunakan termometer digital, serta respon subjektif dan objektif dicatat melalui lembar observasi. Analisis data dilakukan secara deskriptif dengan membandingkan perubahan suhu tubuh antar intervensi.
Main results
Kedua intervensi terbukti efektif menurunkan suhu tubuh. Pada pasien dengan kompres hangat, suhu menurun dari 38,9°C menjadi 37,2°C dengan rata-rata penurunan 0,87°C. Pada pasien dengan tepid sponge, suhu menurun dari 39,7°C menjadi 37,4°C dengan rata-rata penurunan 1,20°C.
Interpretation and novelty
Teknik tepid sponge lebih efektif dalam menurunkan suhu tubuh dibandingkan kompres hangat, meskipun kompres hangat lebih nyaman bagi pasien anak.
Scientific and public-health significance
Temuan ini dapat memberikan informasi untuk memilih metode pengobatan non-farmakologis yang lebih efektif dalam menurunkan suhu tubuh pada anak dengan DHF, sehingga dapat meningkatkan manajemen klinis dan kenyamanan pasien.
Limitations and evidence strength
Studi ini memiliki ukuran sampel kecil dan tidak mencakup analisis statistik formal, sehingga kekuatan bukti terbatas. Namun, hasilnya menunjukkan perbedaan efektivitas antara kedua intervensi dengan dasar pengamatan langsung.

宫内暴露于 登革病毒 和后代的神经发育

Intrauterine exposure to the dengue virus and the neurodevelopment of offspring
作者： Paulo Roberto Bignardi, Augusto Gottert Rochoel
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
It is estimated 2.5 billion people across 129 countries live in tropical and subtropical regions where the disease is endemic and are at risk of contracting dengue.
设计与对象
因此,这项研究的合理性在于需要收集和汇编有关怀孕期间登革热对后代神经发育影响的数据,为子宫内接触登革热病毒的儿童的护理提供科学证据。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
许多研究报告了接触 登革病毒 感染与妊娠并发症之间的关联。
解释与创新
鉴于所提出的背景,与未接触 DENV 的儿童相比,预计在妊娠期间接触 DENV 的儿童出现神经发育迟缓的风险更高。
科研与公卫意义
目的是绘制文献中有关宫内 DENV 暴露以及后代结构和功能神经发育结果的证据。
局限与证据强度
此外,有人提出,感染相关的母体免疫激活(MIA)会增加神经发育障碍的风险。

Intrauterine exposure to the dengue virus and the neurodevelopment of offspring

Authors: Paulo Roberto Bignardi, Augusto Gottert Rochoel
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is the most widespread and rapidly growing vector-borne infectious disease. It is estimated 2.5 billion people across 129 countries live in tropical and subtropical regions where the disease is endemic and are at risk of contracting dengue. An estimated 105 million people are infected worldwide each year. Many studies have reported associations between exposure to dengue virus infection and gestational complications. Similarly, there is evidence linking dengue to impaired placental blood flow and compromised fetal development, including the formation of fetal nervous system structures. Furthermore, it has been proposed that infection-related Maternal Immune Activation (MIA) increases the risk of neurodevelopmental disorders. Thus, this study is justified by the need to gather and compile data on the impact of dengue during pregnancy on offspring neurodevelopment, providing scientific evidence to inform the care of children exposed to DENV in utero. The objective is to map the evidence in the literature regarding intrauterine exposure to DENV and structural and functional neurodevelopmental outcomes in the offspring. Given the context presented, children exposed to DENV during gestation are expected to be at higher risk of neurodevelopmental delays compared to unexposed children.

It is estimated 2.5 billion people across 129 countries live in tropical and subtropical regions where the disease is endemic and are at risk of contracting dengue.
Design and population
Thus, this study is justified by the need to gather and compile data on the impact of dengue during pregnancy on offspring neurodevelopment, providing scientific evidence to inform the care of children exposed to DENV in utero.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Many studies have reported associations between exposure to dengue virus infection and gestational complications.
Interpretation and novelty
Given the context presented, children exposed to DENV during gestation are expected to be at higher risk of neurodevelopmental delays compared to unexposed children.
Scientific and public-health significance
The objective is to map the evidence in the literature regarding intrauterine exposure to DENV and structural and functional neurodevelopmental outcomes in the offspring.
Limitations and evidence strength
Furthermore, it has been proposed that infection-related Maternal Immune Activation (MIA) increases the risk of neurodevelopmental disorders.

新生儿大脑早期寨卡病毒感染期间 Ndel1 酶活性的菌株特异性降低

Strain-Specific Reduction of Ndel1 Enzymatic Activity During Early Zika Virus Infection in the Neonatal Brain
作者： Ingrid Sancho de Farias, Ana P. Ximenes, William Y. Oyadomari, Raíssa R. Christoff, João V. Nani, Tailene Rabello, Luiza M. Higa, Patrícia P. Garcez, Mirian A. F. Hayashi
摘要中文翻译早期发育过程中的寨卡病毒(ZIKV)感染与严重的神经发育障碍有关,包括先天性小头畸形,但将病毒暴露与神经元功能障碍联系起来的分子机制仍不完全清楚。Ndel1(nudE 神经发育蛋白 1 like 1)是一种寡肽酶,与神经元迁移、神经突生长和细胞骨架动力学以及正常大脑发育所必需的过程密切相关。基于先前观察到 Ndel1 酶活性在 ZIKV 感染的胚胎和新生儿大脑中降低,我们研究了这种调节是否代表保守的 宿主 反应或毒株特异性分子效应。使用瑞士小鼠出生后第 0 天的新生儿脑室内感染模型,我们比较了巴西 ZIKV 毒株 (ZIKV BR )、非洲谱系毒株 (ZIKV AF ) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 对大脑中 Ndel1 调节的影响。我们发现,仅在 ZIKV BR 感染的动物中,Ndel1 酶活性在感染后早期时间点(P3 和 P7)显着降低,而相对于对照组,ZIKV AF 和 DENV 并未改变酶活性。值得注意的是,在最早的时间点 (P3),这种减少发生在 Ndel1 mRNA 表达没有变化的情况下,表明最初的转录后或功能调节机制,尽管 P7 时 Ndel1 mRNA 表达也显着减少。尽管两种 ZIKV 毒株均诱导了强烈的炎症基因表达,但只有 ZIKV BR 感染导致 Ndel1 活性降低,从而将神经炎症信号传导与酶促调节分离。重要的是,这种菌株特异性的减少与 ZIKV BR 诱导小头畸形的独特能力相似,并支持 Ndel1 功能障碍作为与神经发育障碍相关的早期分子事件。总之,这些发现确定了关键神经元酶的病毒和毒株特异性调节,并为 ZIKV BR 相关神经发育结果的分子基础提供了机制见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
早期发育过程中的寨卡病毒(ZIKV)感染与严重的神经发育障碍有关,但病毒暴露与神经元功能障碍之间的分子机制仍不完全清楚。该研究旨在确定 ZIKV 感染期间观察到的 Ndel1 酶活性降低是否代表保守的 宿主 反应或毒株特异性分子效应。
设计与对象
该研究使用瑞士小鼠出生后第 0 天的新生儿脑室内感染模型,比较巴西 ZIKV 毒株 (ZIKV BR)、非洲谱系毒株 (ZIKV AF) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 对大脑中 Ndel1 调节的影响。
核心方法
该研究使用新生小鼠模型比较了 ZIKV BR、ZIKV AF 和 DENV 对大脑中 Ndel1 调节的影响。在感染后早期时间点(P3 和 P7)测量 Ndel1 酶活性和 mRNA 表达。还评估了炎症基因表达,以将神经炎症信号传导与酶调节分离。
主要结果
仅在 ZIKV BR 感染动物中,Ndel1 酶活性在感染后早期时间点(P3 和 P7)显着降低,而 ZIKV AF 和 DENV 相对于对照动物并未改变酶活性。在 P3 时,这种减少在 Ndel1 mRNA 表达没有变化的情况下发生,尽管 mRNA 水平在 P7 时显着降低。
解释与创新
仅在 ZIKV BR 中观察到 Ndel1 酶活性的菌株特异性降低表明关键神经元酶存在病毒和菌株特异性调节。这一发现为 ZIKV BR 相关神经发育结果的分子基础提供了机制见解,并将神经炎症与酶调节分开。
科研与公卫意义
结果将 Ndel1 功能障碍确定为可能导致 ZIKV BR 相关神经发育障碍的早期分子事件,突出了未来机制和治疗研究的特定目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖新生小鼠模型而受到限制,该模型可能无法完全重现人类神经发育过程。由于缺乏人类数据并且关注感染后早期时间点,证据强度中等。

Strain-Specific Reduction of Ndel1 Enzymatic Activity During Early Zika Virus Infection in the Neonatal Brain

Authors: Ingrid Sancho de Farias, Ana P. Ximenes, William Y. Oyadomari, Raíssa R. Christoff, João V. Nani, Tailene Rabello, Luiza M. Higa, Patrícia P. Garcez, Mirian A. F. Hayashi
Original abstract (English)
Zika virus (ZIKV) infection during early development is associated with severe neurodevelopmental impairment, including congenital microcephaly, yet the molecular mechanisms linking viral exposure to neuronal dysfunction remain incompletely understood. Ndel1 (nudE neurodevelopment protein 1 like 1) is an oligopeptidase critically involved in neuronal migration, neurite outgrowth, and cytoskeletal dynamics, processes essential for proper brain development. Building on previous observations that Ndel1 enzymatic activity is reduced in ZIKV-infected embryonic and neonatal brains, we investigated whether this modulation represents a conserved host response or a strain-specific molecular effect. Using a neonatal intracerebroventricular infection model in Swiss mice at postnatal day 0, we compared the effects of a Brazilian ZIKV strain (ZIKV BR ), an African lineage strain (ZIKV AF ), and dengue virus (DENV) on Ndel1 regulation in the brain. We found that Ndel1 enzymatic activity was significantly reduced at early post-infection time points (P3 and P7) exclusively in ZIKV BR -infected animals, whereas ZIKV AF and DENV did not alter enzyme activity relative to controls. Notably, at the earliest timepoint (P3), this reduction occurred in the absence of changes in Ndel1 mRNA expression, indicating an initial post-transcriptional or functional mechanism of regulation, although Ndel1 mRNA expression was also significantly reduced by P7. Although both ZIKV strains induced robust inflammatory gene expression, only ZIKV BR infection resulted in decreased Ndel1 activity, dissociating neuroinflammatory signaling from enzymatic modulation. Importantly, this strain-specific reduction parallels the unique capacity of ZIKV BR to induce microcephaly and supports Ndel1 dysfunction as an early molecular event associated with neurodevelopmental impairment. Together, these findings identify a virus- and strain-specific regulation of a key neuronal enzyme and provide mechanistic insight into the molecular basis of ZIKV BR -associated neurodevelopmental outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zika virus (ZIKV) infection during early development is linked to severe neurodevelopmental impairment, yet the molecular mechanisms connecting viral exposure to neuronal dysfunction remain incompletely understood. The study aimed to determine whether the observed reduction in Ndel1 enzymatic activity during ZIKV infection represents a conserved host response or a strain-specific molecular effect.
Design and population
The study used a neonatal intracerebroventricular infection model in Swiss mice at postnatal day 0 to compare the effects of a Brazilian ZIKV strain (ZIKV BR), an African lineage strain (ZIKV AF), and dengue virus (DENV) on Ndel1 regulation in the brain.
Core methods
The study compared the effects of ZIKV BR, ZIKV AF, and DENV on Ndel1 regulation in the brain using a neonatal mouse model. Ndel1 enzymatic activity and mRNA expression were measured at early post-infection time points (P3 and P7). Inflammatory gene expression was also assessed to dissociate neuroinflammatory signaling from enzymatic modulation.
Main results
Ndel1 enzymatic activity was significantly reduced at early post-infection time points (P3 and P7) exclusively in ZIKV BR-infected animals, whereas ZIKV AF and DENV did not alter enzyme activity relative to controls. At P3, this reduction occurred without changes in Ndel1 mRNA expression, though mRNA levels were significantly reduced by P7.
Interpretation and novelty
The strain-specific reduction in Ndel1 enzymatic activity, observed only with ZIKV BR, suggests a virus- and strain-specific regulation of a key neuronal enzyme. This finding provides mechanistic insight into the molecular basis of ZIKV BR-associated neurodevelopmental outcomes and dissociates neuroinflammation from enzymatic modulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results identify Ndel1 dysfunction as an early molecular event potentially contributing to ZIKV BR-associated neurodevelopmental impairment, highlighting a specific target for future mechanistic and therapeutic studies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a neonatal mouse model, which may not fully recapitulate human neurodevelopmental processes. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human data and the focus on early post-infection time points.

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。

作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

IFNL1 基因启动子单核苷酸多态性 rs7247086 通过 STAT 结合位点增强转录。

IFNL1 gene promoter single nucleotide polymorphism rs7247086 enhances transcription through a STAT-binding site.
作者： Sapana Kaushik, Seema Bharatiya, Debarati Guha Roy, Darshan C Panchariya, Ahona Roy, Dhara Raval, Avik Mondal, Aparna Palit, Sreedhar Chinnaswamy
摘要中文翻译人类干扰素λ1(IFN-L1,IFN-λ1)基因启动子区单核苷酸多态性(SNP)rs7247086(C/T)已有报道与重症登革热相关,并可能与银屑病和COVID-19相关。然而,其功能性质尚不清楚。本研究旨在通过启动子和增强子报告基因检测,检验rs7247086对转录的影响。研究发现,T等位基因构成了一个信号转导和转录激活因子(STAT)结合位点的共有序列。虽然我们未发现强有力证据表明该STAT结合位点驱动IFN-L1基因启动子的转录,但观察到其在报告基因检测中表现为增强子。在构建于HEK293和A549细胞系的增强子报告基因载体中,rs7247086的T等位基因较C等位基因显著增加了报告基因的转录。通过电泳迁移率变动分析,我们获得了证据表明T等位基因较C等位基因更强地结合STAT蛋白。在一项针对健康个体的队列研究中,携带T等位基因的个体(尤其是男性而非女性)在外周血单个核细胞受刺激后,IFN-λ1的分泌显著增加。最后,rs7247086仅在男性而非女性中与银屑病显著相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么 IFNL1 基因启动子 SNP rs7247086 (C/T) 可能会影响登革热和其他疾病的严重程度,尽管其功能作用尚不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究在 HEK293 和 A549 细胞系中使用启动子和增强子报告基因测定来检查 rs7247086 对转录的影响,同时使用电泳迁移率变动测定来评估 STAT 蛋白结合。
核心方法
在 HEK293 和 A549 细胞中测试了包含 rs7247086 的 T 或 C 等位基因的启动子和增强子报告基因构建体,并使用电泳迁移率变动测定法评估了 STAT 蛋白结合。
主要结果
与 HEK293 和 A549 细胞中的 C 等位基因相比,rs7247086 的 T 等位基因显着增加了报告基因转录,并​​且 T 等位基因在电泳迁移率变动测定中显示出与 STAT 蛋白更强的结合。
解释与创新
作者解释说,T 等位基因通过 STAT 结合位点增强 IFNL1 转录,充当增强子而不是启动子,并注意到 IFN-λ1 分泌和银屑病关联的性别特异性差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,rs7247086 可能调节 IFN-λ1 表达,可能影响与登革热 发病机制 和其他炎症性疾病相关的 宿主 反应,特别是在男性中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于体外测定和小型 队列研究,观察到了性别特异性效应,但没有从机械上解释,需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

IFNL1 gene promoter single nucleotide polymorphism rs7247086 enhances transcription through a STAT-binding site.

Authors: Sapana Kaushik, Seema Bharatiya, Debarati Guha Roy, Darshan C Panchariya, Ahona Roy, Dhara Raval, Avik Mondal, Aparna Palit, Sreedhar Chinnaswamy
Original abstract (English)
A single nucleotide polymorphism (SNP) within the human interferon lambda 1 (IFN-L1, IFN-λ1) gene promoter, rs7247086 (C/T) has been reported to be associated with severe dengue and possibly with psoriasis and COVID-19. However, its functional nature is unknown. The present study was undertaken to examine the effect of rs7247086 on transcription, by utilizing promoter and enhancer-reporter assays. We see that the T allele completes a consensus signal transducer and activator of transcription (STAT)-binding site. While we did not find strong evidence to show that the STAT-binding site drove transcription from the IFNL1 gene promoter, we saw that it acts like an enhancer in reporter assays. The T allele of rs7247086 within the STAT-binding site significantly increased transcription of the reporter gene compared to the C allele when incorporated into enhancer-reporter constructs in both HEK293 and A549 cell lines. Mechanistically, we obtained evidence from electrophoretic mobility shift assays to show that the T allele binds to STAT proteins more strongly than the C allele. In a cohort of healthy individuals, we saw that the T allele carriers, specifically males but not females, had significantly increased secretion of IFN-λ1 from their peripheral blood mononuclear cells after stimulation. Lastly, rs7247086 significantly associated with psoriasis, only in males but not in females.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why the IFNL1 gene promoter SNP rs7247086 (C/T) might influence disease severity in dengue and other conditions, despite its functional role being unknown.
Design and population
The study used promoter and enhancer-reporter assays in HEK293 and A549 cell lines to examine the effect of rs7247086 on transcription, alongside electrophoretic mobility shift assays to assess STAT protein binding.
Core methods
Promoter and enhancer-reporter constructs incorporating the T or C allele of rs7247086 were tested in HEK293 and A549 cells, and STAT protein binding was evaluated using electrophoretic mobility shift assays.
Main results
The T allele of rs7247086 significantly increased reporter gene transcription compared to the C allele in both HEK293 and A549 cells, and the T allele showed stronger binding to STAT proteins in electrophoretic mobility shift assays.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the T allele enhances IFNL1 transcription via a STAT-binding site, acting as an enhancer rather than a promoter, and note sex-specific differences in IFN-λ1 secretion and psoriasis association.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that rs7247086 may modulate IFN-λ1 expression, potentially influencing host responses relevant to dengue pathogenesis and other inflammatory conditions, particularly in males.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro assays and a small cohort, with sex-specific effects observed but not mechanistically explained, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

有吸引力的致命产卵器减少伊蚊种群的功效:秘鲁伊基托斯的一项对照试验

Efficacy of an attractive lethal ovitrap to reduce populations of Aedes mosquitoes: a controlled trial in Iquitos, Peru
作者： Dawn M. Wesson, Valerie A Paz-Soldan, Kanya C. Long, Loganathan Ponnusamy, Samuel B. Jameson, Justin K. Davis, Robin M. Moudy, Alfonso S. Vizcarra, Helvio Astete, Isabel Bazan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是由埃及伊蚊传播的主要虫媒病毒感染,需要改进 媒介 控制工具。该研究通过评估针对怀孕雌性蚊子的有吸引力的致死产卵器 (ALOT) 是否可以减少 媒介 种群和登革热疾病负担,解决了对新干预措施的迫切需求。
设计与对象
A proof-of-concept field efficacy trial was conducted in Iquitos, Peru, over 3.5 years. The study included a treatment area with a core (753 houses) and buffer zone (1,549 houses) where ALOT traps were deployed, and a control area (1,233 houses) without traps. Entomological surveys were performed every 2 months, and residents were monitored for symptomatic dengue three times weekly.
核心方法
在部署诱捕器之前进行了基线昆虫学调查。前 2.5 年由工作人员每隔 2-3 周进行一次陷阱维护,最后一年由居民进行。使用标准化调查和主动 监测 测量蚊子数量、胎次、性别比和人类登革热 监测。
主要结果
Total and female Aedes aegypti abundance decreased by 52% (RR = 0.48, 95% CI: 0.40–0.58, p < 0.001) and 47% (RR = 0.53, 95% CI: 0.43–0.66, p < 0.001), respectively, after trap placement. The effect diminished over time (interaction coefficient per month: 0.035 and 0.030, p < 0.001). Cumulative symptomatic dengue incidence was 1.40% in the ALOT area versus 3.20% in the control area, a 56% relative reduction (log-rank χ2 = 30.0, p < 0.001), with participants in the intervention area remaining dengue-free for an average of 15.5 additional days (95% CI: 11.1–20.0, p < 0.001).
解释与创新
正如预测的那样,ALOT 策略降低了 媒介 密度和登革热 发病率,但它并没有消除蚊子。该试验证明了通过降低治疗地区的登革热疾病对公共卫生的明显影响,突显了有针对性的 媒介 控制在预防登革热方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用 ALOT 诱捕器作为补充 媒介 控制工具,以减少流行城市环境中的登革热 媒介。该研究强调了持续实施和规划考虑将新的 媒介 控制工具纳入公共卫生战略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
ALOT 陷阱的效果随着时间的推移而减弱,表明长期功效和用户依从性方面存在潜在挑战。由于非随机设计、依赖社区维护以及缺乏登革热感染的昆虫学或病毒学证实,证据是中等的。

Efficacy of an attractive lethal ovitrap to reduce populations of Aedes mosquitoes: a controlled trial in Iquitos, Peru

Authors: Dawn M. Wesson, Valerie A Paz-Soldan, Kanya C. Long, Loganathan Ponnusamy, Samuel B. Jameson, Justin K. Davis, Robin M. Moudy, Alfonso S. Vizcarra, Helvio Astete, Isabel Bazan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue, one of the most important arboviral infections worldwide, is transmitted primarily by the mosquito Aedes aegypti , a vector closely associated with human habitations. Because vector control is the primary prevention strategy for this disease novel tools are urgently needed. We evaluated an Attractive Lethal OviTrap (ALOT) that targets epidemiologically relevant gravid female mosquitoes for public health impact against dengue disease. Methods We conducted a proof-of-concept field efficacy trial in the Amazonian city of Iquitos, Peru, to quantify the reduction of vector density, parity, and sex ratio and symptomatic human dengue infection over a 3.5 year follow-up period. After three baseline entomological surveys in core (753 houses), and buffer (1,549 houses) areas where traps were placed in and around homes, and a control (1,233 houses) area without traps, entomological surveys were carried out every 2 months, and household residents were monitored for dengue disease 3 times a week. Trap maintenance was conducted at 2-3 week intervals by study staff during the first 2.5 years and by residents in the final year. Results Total and female Ae. aegypti abundance demonstrated a strong initial effect with 52% and 47% fewer mosquitoes after trap placement, respectively (Total: RR = 0.48, 95% CI: 0.40 – 0.58, p < 0.001; Females: RR = 0.53, 95% CI: 0.43 – 0.66, p < 0.001). The effect diminished significantly, however, over the study period (interaction coefficient = 0.035 per month, p < 0.001; interaction coefficient = 0.030 per month, p < 0.001). Female-to-male ratio decreased after trap deployment and impact on non- Aedes abundance was like that of Aedes . Cumulative symptomatic dengue incidence was 1.40% (95% CI: 1.00%-1.70%) in the ALOT area versus 3.20% (95% CI: 2.60%-3.80%) in the control area, a 56% relative reduction (log-rank χ2=30.0, p<0.001). Restricted mean survival time analysis showed participants in the intervention area on average remained dengue-free for 15.5 additional days (95% CI: 11.1-20.0, p<0.001). Conclusions The ALOT strategy functioned as predicted, decreasing but not eliminating female vector densities, with a clear public health impact, lowering dengue disease in areas where traps were deployed. Our study also illustrates the challenges associated with conducting large-scale vector control trials and the need to consider programmatic implementation early in the process of bringing a new product to market. Author Summary Dengue is one of the most important mosquito transmitted infections worldwide. Aedes aegypti, the most important species involved, has a unique biology living in containers associated with households. New methods to control this mosquito are needed and we present our evaluation of a novel trap called the Attractive Lethal OviTrap (ALOT) that targets older female mosquitoes that are the most important for dengue disease transmission. We conducted a trial in the Amazonian city of Iquitos, Peru, to measure reductions in mosquito numbers, age, and sex ratio and symptomatic human dengue infections over a 3.5 years. After baseline entomological surveys prior to placing ALOT traps in homes in a treatment area, which included a central core area with 753 houses and a protective buffer area with 1,549 houses areas. We also had a control area without any traps in 1,233 houses. Mosquito surveys were carried out every 2 months, and household residents were monitored for dengue disease 3 times per week. The number of all Ae. aegypti were initially reduced by 52% after trap placement whereas females were reduced by 47%. The effect diminished significantly, however, over the study period. Female-to-male ratio decreased after trap deployment and impact on other mosquito species was like that of Aedes . Dengue incidence was 56% less in the area with traps compared to the control area, estimating that participants in the intervention area on average remained dengue-free for 15.5 additional days. The ALOT strategy functioned as predicted, decreasing but not eliminating female mosquitos and had a clear public health impact, lowering dengue disease in areas where traps were deployed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a major arboviral infection transmitted by Aedes aegypti, necessitating improved vector control tools. The study addressed the urgent need for novel interventions by evaluating whether an Attractive Lethal OviTrap (ALOT) targeting gravid female mosquitoes could reduce vector populations and dengue disease burden.
Design and population
A proof-of-concept field efficacy trial was conducted in Iquitos, Peru, over 3.5 years. The study included a treatment area with a core (753 houses) and buffer zone (1,549 houses) where ALOT traps were deployed, and a control area (1,233 houses) without traps. Entomological surveys were performed every 2 months, and residents were monitored for symptomatic dengue three times weekly.
Core methods
Baseline entomological surveys were conducted prior to trap deployment. Trap maintenance was performed at 2–3 week intervals by staff for the first 2.5 years and by residents in the final year. Mosquito abundance, parity, sex ratio, and human dengue incidence were measured using standardized surveys and active surveillance.
Main results
Total and female Aedes aegypti abundance decreased by 52% (RR = 0.48, 95% CI: 0.40–0.58, p < 0.001) and 47% (RR = 0.53, 95% CI: 0.43–0.66, p < 0.001), respectively, after trap placement. The effect diminished over time (interaction coefficient per month: 0.035 and 0.030, p < 0.001). Cumulative symptomatic dengue incidence was 1.40% in the ALOT area versus 3.20% in the control area, a 56% relative reduction (log-rank χ2 = 30.0, p < 0.001), with participants in the intervention area remaining dengue-free for an average of 15.5 additional days (95% CI: 11.1–20.0, p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The ALOT strategy reduced vector densities and dengue incidence as predicted, though it did not eliminate mosquitoes. The trial demonstrated a clear public health impact by lowering dengue disease in treated areas, highlighting the potential of targeted vector control for dengue prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of ALOT traps as a supplementary vector control tool to reduce dengue transmission in endemic urban settings. The study underscores the importance of sustained implementation and programmatic considerations for integrating new vector control tools into public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The effect of the ALOT traps diminished over time, indicating potential challenges with long-term efficacy and user compliance. The evidence is moderate due to the non-randomized design, reliance on community-based maintenance, and the absence of entomological or virological confirmation of dengue infections.

对佛得角疟疾和虫媒病毒 媒介 监测 的 MTego 和 BG-Sentinel 陷阱进行评估。

Evaluation of MTego and BG-Sentinel traps for malaria and arbovirus vector surveillance in Cabo Verde.
作者： Lara Ferrero Gómez, Adéritow Gonçalves, Hélio Rocha, Eliane Chantre, Eliane Gomes, Alba Reyes Concepción, Celivianne de Sousa, Suzineida Correia, Djeniffer Ramos, Adilson DePina
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒 暴发 在非洲各地呈上升趋势,该地区继续面临着巨大的疟疾负担。佛得角仍然容易受到蚊媒疾病的出现和再次出现的影响,包括登革热、寨卡和疟疾。2024年1月,世界卫生组织认证该国无疟疾;然而,重新引入的风险依然存在。维持无疟疾状态需要持续稳健的流行病学和昆虫学 监测 来预防当地的 监测。在这种情况下,提供准确感染数据的创新 媒介 监测和控制工具至关重要。我们评估了 MTego 诱集装置与佛得角常用的 BGS 诱集装置的性能,采用 4x4 拉丁方设计,具有四种配置:1) BGS 与 BG Lure;2) BGS 与 BG Lure 加 CO2;3) MTego 粘土球陷阱加上 PM6 气味诱饵;4) MTego 陷阱,包含粘土球、PM6 诱饵和二氧化碳。采样是在 2023 年雨季前后在普拉亚进行的。使用负二项式回归模型评估陷阱有效性。在所有配置中,共收集了 2,673 只蚊子。以 CO2 为诱饵的 BGS 诱集装置的平均夜间捕获量最高:20.4(95% CI:15.4-25.4),MTego 诱集装置的平均夜间捕获量最低:13.7(95% CI:10.0-17.5)。比较分析表明,MTego 诱集器在捕获阿拉伯按蚊方面优于 BGS 诱集器,其中补充 CO2 的 MTego 诱集器是最有效的配置。BGS 诱捕器对于埃及伊蚊和淡色库蚊效果最佳。观察到物种特异性的行为模式,Cx pipiens s.l. 的夜间捕获量较高。arabiensis,以及促性腺营养状态的差异。这项研究强调了 MTego 陷阱在佛得角治疗疟疾 监测 的卓越性能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在非洲虫媒病毒 暴发 不断上升的情况下,需要改进 媒介 监测 工具以维持佛得角无疟疾状态的问题。它强调了尽管获得了认证,但蚊媒疾病重新引入的持续风险,从而激发了对创新诱捕方法的评估。
设计与对象
采用 4x4 拉丁方设计来比较 2023 年雨季前后在佛得角普拉亚的 MTego 和 BG-Sentinel (BGS) 陷阱。测试了四种配置:带有 BG Lure 的 BGS、带有 BG Lure 加 CO2 的 BGS、带有粘土球加 PM6 气味诱饵的 MTego、以及带有粘土球、PM6 诱饵加 CO2 的 MTego。
核心方法
使用负二项式回归模型评估诱捕器的有效性,以比较不同配置的蚊子捕获量。采样在佛得角普拉亚进行,并旋转配置以控制空间和时间偏差。
主要结果
在所有配置中,共收集了 2,673 只蚊子。使用 CO2 的 BGS 诱捕器的平均夜间捕获量最高(20.4;95% CI:15.4–25.4),而 MTego 诱捕器的平均夜间捕获量最低(13.7;95% CI:10.0–17.5)。MTego 诱捕器在捕获阿拉伯按蚊方面优于 BGS 诱捕器,特别是在补充 CO2 时。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 MTego 诱捕器在佛得角针对疟疾 媒介 监测 的卓越性能,特别是针对阿拉伯按蚊。这代表了消除后环境中诱捕方法的新颖比较评估。
科研与公卫意义
研究表明,MTego 诱捕器,特别是与二氧化碳结合使用时,可以加强佛得角疟疾 监测 的工作,以防止疟疾再次传入。它还强调了针对 媒介 控制的物种特异性诱捕策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据因其集中于单一地点和季节而受到限制,这可能会限制普遍性。该研究设计虽然对于比较诱捕而言是稳健的,但并未涉及更广泛的流行病学或昆虫学 监测 系统,因此需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Evaluation of MTego and BG-Sentinel traps for malaria and arbovirus vector surveillance in Cabo Verde.

Authors: Lara Ferrero Gómez, Adéritow Gonçalves, Hélio Rocha, Eliane Chantre, Eliane Gomes, Alba Reyes Concepción, Celivianne de Sousa, Suzineida Correia, Djeniffer Ramos, Adilson DePina
Original abstract (English)
Arboviral outbreaks are rising across Africa, a region that continues to face a substantial malaria burden. Cabo Verde remains vulnerable to the emergence and re-emergence of mosquito-borne diseases, including dengue, Zika, and malaria. In January 2024, the World Health Organization certified the country as malaria-free; however, the risk of reintroduction persists. Maintaining malaria-free status requires continuous robust epidemiological and entomological surveillance to prevent local transmission. Innovative vector monitoring and control tools that deliver accurate infestation data are essential in this context. We evaluated the performance of the MTego trap against the BGS traps routinely used in Cabo Verde applying a 4x4 Latin square design with four configurations: 1) BGS with BG Lure; 2) BGS with BG Lure plus CO2; 3) MTego traps with Clay Balls plus PM6 odour bait; and 4) MTego traps with Clay Balls, PM6 bait plus CO2. Sampling was conducted in Praia, before and after the 2023 rainy season. Trap effectiveness was assessed using negative binomial regression models. Across all configurations, 2,673 mosquitoes were collected. Mean nightly catches were highest for BGS traps baited with CO2: 20.4 (95% CI: 15.4-25.4), and lowest for MTego traps: 13.7 (95% CI: 10.0-17.5). Comparative analysis showed that MTego traps outperformed BGS traps in capturing Anopheles arabiensis, with CO2-supplemented MTego trap being the most effective configuration. BGS traps performed best for Aedes aegypti and Culex pipiens sensu lato. Species-specific behavioural patterns were observed, with higher nigh-time catches of Cx pipiens s.l. arabiensis, alongside differences in gonotrophic state. This study highlights the superior performance of MTego traps for malaria surveillance in Cabo Verde.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved vector surveillance tools to maintain malaria-free status in Cabo Verde amid rising arboviral outbreaks across Africa. It highlights the persistent risk of reintroduction of mosquito-borne diseases despite certification, motivating the evaluation of innovative trapping methods.
Design and population
A 4x4 Latin square design was used to compare MTego and BG-Sentinel (BGS) traps in Praia, Cabo Verde, before and after the 2023 rainy season. Four configurations were tested: BGS with BG Lure, BGS with BG Lure plus CO2, MTego with Clay Balls plus PM6 odour bait, and MTego with Clay Balls, PM6 bait plus CO2.
Core methods
Trap effectiveness was assessed using negative binomial regression models to compare mosquito catches across configurations. Sampling was conducted in Praia, Cabo Verde, with configurations rotated to control for spatial and temporal biases.
Main results
Across all configurations, 2,673 mosquitoes were collected. Mean nightly catches were highest for BGS traps with CO2 (20.4; 95% CI: 15.4–25.4) and lowest for MTego traps (13.7; 95% CI: 10.0–17.5). MTego traps outperformed BGS traps in capturing Anopheles arabiensis, particularly when supplemented with CO2.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating superior performance of MTego traps for malaria vector surveillance in Cabo Verde, particularly for Anopheles arabiensis. This represents a novel comparative evaluation of trapping methods in a post-elimination setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that MTego traps, especially when combined with CO2, could enhance malaria surveillance efforts in Cabo Verde to prevent reintroduction. It also underscores the importance of species-specific trapping strategies for targeted vector control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by its focus on a single location and season, which may restrict generalisability. The study design, while robust for comparative trapping, does not address broader epidemiological or entomological surveillance systems, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

坦桑尼亚达累斯萨拉姆埃及伊蚊种群的杀虫剂敏感性和击倒抗性 (Kdr) 突变(F1534C 和 V1016I)

Insecticide susceptibility and knockdown resistance (Kdr) mutations (F1534C and V1016I) in Aedes aegypti populations from Dar es Salaam, Tanzania
作者： Frank S.C. Tenywa, Silvan Hälg, Makame Haji, Jason Moore, Osward Dogan, Mapipi Haruna, P C Müller, Sarah J. Moore
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热是伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,对全球公共卫生构成重大威胁。在缺乏有效的 治疗 或普遍可用的 疫苗 的情况下,撒哈拉以南非洲地区的疾病控制主要依赖于杀虫剂 媒介 控制。然而,持续使用杀虫剂会给杀虫剂抗药性带来选择压力,从而损害干预效果。本研究评估了坦桑尼亚达累斯萨拉姆埃及伊蚊种群的杀虫剂抗药性。方法 伊蚊表型抗性。使用 WHO 推荐的杀成虫剂和杀幼虫剂敏感性生物测定法以 1 倍区分浓度对埃及伊蚊进行评估。F 1 后代源自野生伊蚊。埃及幼虫用于测试。在杀成虫生物测定中,每组 25 只雌性伊蚊。将埃及伊蚊暴露于经过杀虫剂处理的 WHO 药敏试纸上 1 小时。杀幼虫生物测定将 25 只第三龄和第四龄幼虫暴露于基于以色列苏云金芽孢杆菌 (Bti) 或天美磷的杀幼虫剂(溶于 200 毫升水中)24 小时。暴露后 24 小时记录蚊子 死亡率。使用从表型蚊子中提取的 DNA 评估基因型抗性。使用诊断定量聚合酶链式反应 (qPCR) 分析筛选提取的 DNA 的敲低抗性 (Kdr) 突变。结果 达累斯萨拉姆 Ilala 和 Temeke 区的埃及伊蚊种群表现出对拟除虫菊酯和有机氯的表型抗性,成虫 死亡率 率分别低于 20% 和 5%,同时仍然对有机磷酸酯和氨基甲酸酯敏感。在 Kinondoni,除氨基甲酸酯外,所有类别均观察到耐药性,并且预先暴露于胡椒基丁醚 (PBO) 会使拟除虫菊酯 死亡率 平均增加 40%。Bti 和替美磷的 LD 50 和 LD 90 值显示耐药比 (RR) 低于 5。在所有研究区均检测到 F1534C 突变;Ilala (77%,n = 77)、Kinondoni (90%,n = 90) 和 Temeke (83%,n = 83),而 V1016I 突变 则观察到低频。结论 达累斯萨拉姆的埃及伊蚊种群对拟除虫菊酯和有机氯表现出广泛的抗性。通过预先暴露于 PBO,对拟除虫菊酯的敏感性部分恢复,表明代谢参与的证据。LD90 相对于 Bti 和 temephos 敏感参考菌株增加两倍,表明没有证据表明存在耐药性,尽管需要继续监测以检测敏感性的潜在变化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热是伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,对撒哈拉以南非洲地区的全球公共卫生构成重大威胁,该地区的疾病控制主要依靠杀虫剂 媒介 控制。
设计与对象
本研究利用野生伊蚊的 F1 后代评估了坦桑尼亚达累斯萨拉姆埃及伊蚊种群的杀虫剂抗药性。埃及幼虫用于表型和基因型抗性测试。
核心方法
使用 WHO 推荐的杀成虫剂和杀幼虫剂敏感性生物测定法以 1 倍区分浓度评估表型抗性,并在暴露后 24 小时记录蚊子 死亡率。通过使用诊断性 qPCR 检测筛选表型蚊子的 DNA 进行击倒抗性 (Kdr) 突变 来评估基因型抗性。
主要结果
来自伊拉拉和特梅克地区的埃及伊蚊种群表现出对拟除虫菊酯和有机氯的表型抗性,成虫 死亡率 分别低于 20% 和 5%,同时仍然对有机磷酸酯和氨基甲酸酯敏感。在 Kinondoni,除氨基甲酸酯外,所有类别均观察到耐药性,并且预先接触胡椒基丁醚可使拟除虫菊酯 死亡率 平均增加 40%。F1534C 突变 在 Ilala (77%)、Kinondoni (90%) 和 Temeke (83%) 中检测到,而 V1016I 突变 频率较低。
解释与创新
该研究解释了达累斯萨拉姆地区对拟除虫菊酯和有机氯的广泛耐药性。埃及伊蚊种群,PBO 预暴露后拟除虫菊酯敏感性部分恢复表明代谢参与的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了达累斯萨拉姆需要采取综合的 媒介 管理策略,包括监测抗药性趋势和评估替代杀虫剂以维持 媒介 控制有效性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于达累斯萨拉姆三个地区的表型和基因型评估,没有关于 Kdr 监测 或更广泛的生态因素之外的耐药机制的数据,需要谨慎解释和继续 监测。

Insecticide susceptibility and knockdown resistance (Kdr) mutations (F1534C and V1016I) in Aedes aegypti populations from Dar es Salaam, Tanzania

Authors: Frank S.C. Tenywa, Silvan Hälg, Makame Haji, Jason Moore, Osward Dogan, Mapipi Haruna, P C Müller, Sarah J. Moore
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue, Zika and chikungunya are Aedes -borne arboviruses that pose a major global public health threat. In the absence of effective treatments or universally available vaccines, disease control in sub-Saharan Africa relies primarily on insecticidal vector control. However, sustained insecticide application imposes selection pressure for insecticide resistance that compromises intervention effectiveness. This study assessed the insecticide resistance profile of Aedes aegypti populations in Dar es Salaam, Tanzania. Methods Phenotypic resistance of Ae. aegypti was evaluated using WHO-recommended adulticide and larvicide susceptibility bioassays at 1x discriminating concentrations. F 1 progeny derived from wild Ae. aegypti larvae were used for the tests. In adulticide bioassays, groups of 25 female Ae. aegypti were exposed for 1 h to insecticide-treated WHO susceptibility test papers. Larvicide bioassays exposed 25 third- and fourth-instar larvae to a larvicide based on Bacillus thuringiensis israelensis ( Bti ) or temephos for 24 h in 200 ml of water. Mosquito mortality was recorded 24 h post-exposure. Genotypic resistance was assessed using DNA extracted from phenotyped mosquitoes. The extracted DNA was screened for knockdown resistance ( Kdr ) mutations using diagnostic quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) assays. Results Aedes aegypti populations from Ilala and Temeke districts of Dar es Salaam demonstrated phenotypic resistance to pyrethroids, and organochlorine, with adult mortality rates below 20% and 5%, respectively, while remaining susceptible to organophosphates and carbamates. In Kinondoni, resistance was observed across all classes except carbamates, and pre-exposure to piperonlyl butoxide (PBO) increased pyrethroids mortality to an average of 40%. The LD 50 and LD 90 values for Bti and temephos showed resistance ratios (RR) below 5. The F1534C mutation was detected across all study districts; Ilala (77%, n = 77), Kinondoni (90%, n = 90) and Temeke (83%, n = 83), while low frequencies were observed for V1016I mutation. Conclusion Aedes aegypti populations from Dar es Salaam show widespread resistance to pyrethroids, and organochlorines. There was evidence of metabolic involvement suggested by partial restoration of susceptibility to pyrethroids through pre-exposure to PBO. A two-fold increase in LD90 relative to the susceptible reference strain for Bti and temephos indicates no evidence of resistance, although continued monitoring is warranted to detect potential shifts in susceptibility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue, Zika and chikungunya are Aedes-borne arboviruses that pose a major global public health threat in sub-Saharan Africa, where disease control relies primarily on insecticidal vector control.
Design and population
This study assessed the insecticide resistance profile of Aedes aegypti populations in Dar es Salaam, Tanzania, using F1 progeny derived from wild Ae. aegypti larvae for phenotypic and genotypic resistance testing.
Core methods
Phenotypic resistance was evaluated using WHO-recommended adulticide and larvicide susceptibility bioassays at 1x discriminating concentrations, with mosquito mortality recorded 24 hours post-exposure. Genotypic resistance was assessed by screening DNA from phenotyped mosquitoes for knockdown resistance (Kdr) mutations using diagnostic qPCR assays.
Main results
Aedes aegypti populations from Ilala and Temeke districts demonstrated phenotypic resistance to pyrethroids and organochlorines, with adult mortality below 20% and 5%, respectively, while remaining susceptible to organophosphates and carbamates. In Kinondoni, resistance was observed across all classes except carbamates, and pre-exposure to piperonyl butoxide increased pyrethroid mortality to an average of 40%. The F1534C mutation was detected in Ilala (77%), Kinondoni (90%) and Temeke (83%), while V1016I mutation frequencies were low.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets widespread resistance to pyrethroids and organochlorines in Dar es Salaam Ae. aegypti populations, with evidence of metabolic involvement suggested by partial restoration of pyrethroid susceptibility after PBO pre-exposure.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for integrated vector management strategies in Dar es Salaam, including monitoring resistance trends and evaluating alternative insecticides to sustain vector control effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to phenotypic and genotypic assessments in three districts of Dar es Salaam, with no data on resistance mechanisms beyond Kdr mutations or broader ecological factors, warranting cautious interpretation and continued surveillance.

媒介 传播病毒对先天免疫的时间重新布线,用于 宿主 导向的 抗病毒药物 治疗

Temporal Rewiring of Innate Immunity by Vector-Borne Viruses for Host-Directed Antiviral Therapy
作者： Eunji Kim, Andreas S. Baur, Jung-Hyun Lee
摘要中文翻译随着气候、土地利用、城市化和人类流动重塑 媒介 生态和人类暴露,媒介 传播的病毒感染的地理范围和 媒介 强度正在增加。尽管它们对公共卫生越来越重要,但由于病毒遗传多样性、快速进化能力、零星的 暴发 模式和经济限制,对于许多新出现和重新出现的 媒介 传播病毒来说,有效的 抗病毒药物 和预防方案仍然有限。虽然这些病毒在分类、基因组 组织、媒介 特异性、组织向性和临床表现方面有所不同,但它们利用了 宿主 抗病毒药物 防御中的共同弱点,特别是先天 抗病毒药物 免疫的时机,以支持病毒复制、免疫逃避、炎症失调和疾病进展。媒介携带的病毒不是简单地抑制抗病毒药物防御,而是可以延迟早期病毒核酸感应,减弱干扰素诱导或反应性,并延长病毒复制的初始阶段。随着病毒负荷的增加和受感染的组织承受压力或损伤,延迟的免疫激活可能会导致过度的炎症放大,从而导致疾病特异性病理。在本研究中,我们研究了代表性 媒介 传播病毒(例如 登革病毒、基孔肯雅病毒和严重发热伴血小板减少综合征病毒)中先天 抗病毒药物 免疫的时间重新布线,并提出了解这些保守的 宿主 依赖性可能会导致更广泛、阶段特异性、适应性强的 抗病毒药物 策略,以补充传统的病毒导向方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着病毒负荷的增加和受感染的组织承受压力或损伤,延迟的免疫激活可能会导致过度的炎症放大,从而导致疾病特异性病理。
设计与对象
在本研究中,我们研究了代表性 媒介 传播病毒(例如 登革病毒、基孔肯雅病毒和严重发热伴血小板减少综合征病毒)中先天 抗病毒药物 免疫的时间重新布线,并提出了解这些保守的 宿主 依赖性可能会导致更广泛、阶段特异性、适应性强的 抗病毒药物 策略,以补充传统的病毒导向方法。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
虽然这些病毒在分类、基因组 组织、媒介 特异性、组织向性和临床表现方面有所不同,但它们利用了 宿主 抗病毒药物 防御中的共同弱点,特别是先天 抗病毒药物 免疫的时机,以支持病毒复制、免疫逃避、炎症失调和疾病进展。
科研与公卫意义
尽管它们对公共卫生越来越重要,但由于病毒遗传多样性、快速进化能力、零星的 暴发 模式和经济限制,对于许多新出现和重新出现的 媒介 传播病毒来说,有效的 抗病毒药物 和预防方案仍然有限。
局限与证据强度
媒介携带的病毒不是简单地抑制抗病毒药物防御,而是可以延迟早期病毒核酸感应,减弱干扰素诱导或反应性,并延长病毒复制的初始阶段。

Temporal Rewiring of Innate Immunity by Vector-Borne Viruses for Host-Directed Antiviral Therapy

Authors: Eunji Kim, Andreas S. Baur, Jung-Hyun Lee
Original abstract (English)
The geographic range and outbreak intensity of vector-borne viral infections are increasing as climate, land use, urbanization, and human mobility reshape vector ecology and human exposure. Despite their growing importance to public health, effective antiviral and preventive options remain limited for many emerging and reemerging vector-borne viruses due to viral genetic diversity, rapid evolutionary capacity, sporadic outbreak patterns, and economic constraints. While these viruses differ in taxonomy, genome organization, vector specificity, tissue tropism, and clinical manifestations, they exploit a shared vulnerability in host antiviral defense, particularly the timing of innate antiviral immunity to support viral replication, immune evasion, inflammatory dysregulation, and disease progression. Rather than simply suppressing antiviral defense, vector-borne viruses can delay early viral nucleic acid sensing, attenuate interferon induction or responsiveness, and extend the initial phase for viral replication. As viral burden increases and infected tissues undergo stress or damage, delayed immune activation can shift toward excessive inflammatory amplification, contributing to disease-specific pathology. In this study, we examine this temporal rewiring of innate antiviral immunity in representative vector-borne viruses such as dengue virus, chikungunya virus, and severe fever with thrombocytopenia syndrome virus and propose that understanding these conserved host dependencies may lead to broader, stage-specific, adaptable antiviral strategies that complement conventional virus-directed approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As viral burden increases and infected tissues undergo stress or damage, delayed immune activation can shift toward excessive inflammatory amplification, contributing to disease-specific pathology.
Design and population
In this study, we examine this temporal rewiring of innate antiviral immunity in representative vector-borne viruses such as dengue virus, chikungunya virus, and severe fever with thrombocytopenia syndrome virus and propose that understanding these conserved host dependencies may lead to broader, stage-specific, adaptable antiviral strategies that complement conventional virus-directed approaches.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
While these viruses differ in taxonomy, genome organization, vector specificity, tissue tropism, and clinical manifestations, they exploit a shared vulnerability in host antiviral defense, particularly the timing of innate antiviral immunity to support viral replication, immune evasion, inflammatory dysregulation, and disease progression.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
Rather than simply suppressing antiviral defense, vector-borne viruses can delay early viral nucleic acid sensing, attenuate interferon induction or responsiveness, and extend the initial phase for viral replication.

Atypical Presentation of Chikungunya Virus Infection: More Than Meets the Eye

摘要中文翻译病例系列对受影响个体的影响。我们病例系列的目的是(1)检查患者中的 患病率 和非典型症状的频率,以及(2)确定人口特征和伴随疾病。病例描述 病例 1 一名 39 岁男性患者因高烧伴寒战 4 天(103.2 °F)到急诊科就诊。他有呕吐和头痛症状 4 天,随后感觉改变 1 天。检查时,患者出现迷失方向和烦躁,GCS 为 E4V3M6。CNS 检查显示双侧深部腱反射过度,巴宾斯基阳性。反射;实验室检查显示 HIV 反应状态,血清和脑脊液中存在抗基孔肯雅 IgM(表 1)。MRI 脑部介绍 基孔肯雅热 (CHIKF) 是由基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起的虫媒病毒疾病,基孔肯雅病毒是披膜病毒科的一种甲病毒。传播 通过人类蚊子-人类循环启动而发生由蚊虫叮咬引起。潜伏期 2-6 天后出现症状,特点是高烧和剧烈关节痛。这种情况具有自限性,通常会在 2 周内消退。1 慢性疾病可能持续数年,并伴有持续的多关节痛。登革热和疟疾的重叠症状和共同的 媒介 为诊断和治疗这种疾病提供了重大障碍。CHIKF 于 1963 年首次在印度报道。经过 32 年的休眠期后,CHIKV 于 2005 年在印度重新出现。由于疾病,暴发 造成严重的经济和生产损失。1 虽然基孔肯雅热通常不被认为危及生命,但已记录了导致相当大发病率的异常临床表现,特别是在 暴发 期间。CHIKV 感染可能会导致需要紧急护理的严重疾病,并伴有大量死亡。虽然一些死亡与 CHIKV 感染直接相关,但 死亡率 在 暴发 期间总体 死亡率 也会上升。2 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染通常被视为死亡率 属于良性疾病,而严重病例很少。3,4 最近全球范围内 CHIKV 暴发 的增加促使医生和流行病学家关注非典型症状,因为它们具有显着且偶尔致命的后遗症。5 有关 CHIKV 感染非典型症状的信息有限,只有一些孤立的 病例报告 和医院研究。6 对不寻常症状的细致评估对于理解 CHIKV 感染的实际属性至关重要。CHIKV 感染及其 1-
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在检查登革热感染患者的 患病率 和非典型症状的频率,并确定人口特征和伴随疾病。作者强调需要理解这些不寻常的表现,因为它们对受影响的个人有潜在影响。
设计与对象
该研究是在印度进行的一个病例系列,重点关注表现出非典型症状的登革热感染患者。病例描述提供了患者的详细临床资料,包括年龄、症状和神经系统检查结果。
核心方法
这些方法涉及详细的临床评估,包括神经学评估和实验室研究以确认登革热感染。该研究还包括 MRI 脑成像,以识别与登革热感染相关的任何神经并发症。
主要结果

解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了诊断登革热感染的复杂性,因为其症状与基孔肯雅热等其他虫媒病毒有重叠。他们强调了识别非典型表现以改善临床结果的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要加强诊断方案和对登革热感染非典型症状的认识,以防止误诊并确保及时和适当的 治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到病例系列设计的限制,可能无法提供可靠的统计证据。然而,详细的临床描述和实验室检查结果为了解登革热感染的非典型表现提供了宝贵的见解。

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine the prevalence and frequency of atypical symptoms among patients with dengue infection and to ascertain demographic characteristics and concomitant diseases. The authors highlighted the need for understanding these unusual presentations due to their potential impact on affected individuals.
Design and population
The study was a case series conducted in India, focusing on patients with dengue infection who exhibited atypical symptoms. The case descriptions provided detailed clinical profiles of patients, including their age, symptoms, and neurological findings.
Core methods
The methods involved detailed clinical evaluations, including neurological assessments and laboratory investigations to confirm dengue infection. The study also included MRI brain imaging to identify any neurological complications associated with dengue infection.
Main results
The case series described a 39-year-old male patient with high-grade fever, vomiting, headache, and altered sensorium. Laboratory findings confirmed the presence of antichikungunya IgM in serum and CSF, indicating a possible overlap with chikungunya virus infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the complexity of diagnosing dengue infection due to overlapping symptoms with other arboviruses like chikungunya. They emphasized the importance of recognizing atypical presentations to improve clinical outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for enhanced diagnostic protocols and awareness of atypical symptoms in dengue infection to prevent misdiagnosis and ensure timely and appropriate treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its case series design, which may not provide robust statistical evidence. However, the detailed clinical descriptions and laboratory findings offer valuable insights into the atypical manifestations of dengue infection.

重症登革热 和登革热-疟疾混合感染:哥伦比亚蒙特里亚一家转诊医院的病例系列

Severe Dengue and Dengue–Malaria Coinfection: A Case Series from a Referral Hospital in Montería, Colombia
作者： Paula A. Avilés-Vergara, Dina Ricardo-Caldera, Osnamir Elias Bru-Cordero, Juan Alberto Miranda, Angie Paola Martínez Villera, Kevin Alexander Angulo Álvarez, María Carolina Geney Caro
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:重症登革热 和登革热-疟疾合并感染代表了热带地方病环境中的主要诊断和治疗挑战,其中重叠的临床表现可能会延迟对恶化的识别。本研究旨在描述在哥伦比亚科尔多瓦蒙特里亚一家转诊医院接受治疗的 重症登革热、有警告信号的登革热以及登革热-疟疾合并感染患者的临床和流行病学特征。方法:通过回顾 2018 年至 2023 年间诊断为登革热的患者的病历,进行回顾性病例系列。排除没有警告信号的登革热病例。最终分析包括出现登革热警告体征、重症登革热 和登革热-疟疾合并感染的患者。登革热分类遵循2009年世界卫生组织标准,疟疾通过厚血涂片确诊。收集了社会人口学、临床、实验室、地理和结果相关变量。进行了描述性分析,并使用费舍尔精确检验对选定的分类变量进行了比较。结果:共纳入 53 名患者:25 名(47.17%)重症登革热 患者、14 名(26.42%)具有警告症状的登革热患者、14 名(26.42%)登革热-疟疾合并感染患者。重症登革热 在女性中更为常见,而具有警告信号和合并感染的登革热在男性中占主导地位。重症登革热病例中儿童所占比例最高,而合并感染病例主要分布在儿童期和青春期。所有患者均有发烧记录。重症登革热 病例中水肿、血细胞比容升高、严重血浆渗漏和血流动力学损害更为常见,而肌痛在不同临床组之间存在显着差异。记录了 10 例死亡,其中 6 例发生在合并感染的患者中。结论:重症登革热 和登革热-疟疾合并感染是临床上复杂的病症,具有重叠的表现和潜在的致命结果,强调了早期识别、鉴别 诊断 和密切监测的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究在转诊医院中检查了 重症登革热、有警告信号的登革热以及登革热-疟疾合并感染,重点关注登革热流行地区的临床和流行病学特征以及诊断挑战。
证据基础与方法
回顾性病例系列回顾了 2018 年至 2023 年登革热患者的医疗记录,排除了那些没有警告信号的患者,并使用 WHO 2009 标准对病例进行了分类,并通过厚血涂片确认疟疾;收集社会人口统计学、临床、实验室、地理和结果变量,并使用费舍尔精确检验进行描述性分析。
共识与结论
在53名患者中,重症登革热(47.17%)在女性和儿童中占主导地位,而有预警症状和合并感染的登革热在男性中更为常见,并且分布在儿童和青少年时期;重症登革热 中水肿、血细胞比容升高、严重血浆渗漏和血流动力学损害更为常见,所有患者均出现发热。
争议与缺口
该研究没有对疟原虫种类的合并感染结果进行分层,也没有评估长期后遗症,而且样本量小限制了普遍性;高 死亡率(10 例死亡,6 例合并感染患者)强调了合并感染管理中诊断和治疗的不确定性。
科研与实践启示
优先考虑流行环境中登革热-疟疾双重感染的差异诊断算法,评估物种特异性疟原虫对登革热严重程度的影响,并针对高危人群(患有 重症登革热 的儿童和双重感染的青少年)实施有针对性的 监测,以减少识别延迟并改善结果。

Severe Dengue and Dengue–Malaria Coinfection: A Case Series from a Referral Hospital in Montería, Colombia

Authors: Paula A. Avilés-Vergara, Dina Ricardo-Caldera, Osnamir Elias Bru-Cordero, Juan Alberto Miranda, Angie Paola Martínez Villera, Kevin Alexander Angulo Álvarez, María Carolina Geney Caro
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Severe dengue and dengue–malaria coinfection represent major diagnostic and therapeutic challenges in tropical endemic settings, where overlapping clinical manifestations may delay recognition of deterioration. This study aimed to describe the clinical and epidemiological characteristics of patients with severe dengue, dengue with warning signs, and dengue–malaria coinfection treated at a referral hospital in Montería, Córdoba, Colombia. Methods: A retrospective case series was conducted by reviewing medical records of patients diagnosed with dengue between 2018 and 2023. Cases classified as dengue without warning signs were excluded. The final analysis included patients with dengue-warning signs, severe dengue, and dengue–malaria coinfection. Dengue classification followed the 2009 World Health Organization criteria, and malaria was confirmed by thick blood smear. Sociodemographic, clinical, laboratory, geographic, and outcome-related variables were collected. Descriptive analyses were performed, and selected categorical variables were compared using Fisher’s exact test. Results: Fifty-three patients were included: 25 (47.17%) with severe dengue, 14 (26.42%) with dengue with warning signs, and 14 (26.42%) with dengue–malaria coinfection. Severe dengue was more frequent among females, whereas dengue with warning signs and coinfection predominated in males. Children accounted for the highest proportion of severe dengue cases, while coinfected cases were mainly distributed between childhood and adolescence. Fever was documented in all patients. Edema, elevated hematocrit, severe plasma leakage, and hemodynamic compromise were more frequent among severe dengue cases, whereas myalgia differed significantly across clinical groups. Ten deaths were recorded, six occurring in coinfected patients. Conclusions: Severe dengue and dengue–malaria coinfection are clinically complex conditions with overlapping manifestations and potentially fatal outcomes, highlighting the need for early recognition, differential diagnosis, and close monitoring.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study examines severe dengue, dengue with warning signs, and dengue–malaria coinfection in a referral hospital setting, focusing on clinical and epidemiological characteristics and diagnostic challenges in a dengue-endemic region.
Evidence base and method
A retrospective case series reviewed medical records of dengue patients from 2018 to 2023, excluding those without warning signs, and classified cases using WHO 2009 criteria with malaria confirmation via thick blood smear; sociodemographic, clinical, laboratory, geographic, and outcome variables were collected and analyzed descriptively with Fisher’s exact test.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 53 patients, severe dengue (47.17%) predominated in females and children, while dengue with warning signs and coinfection were more frequent in males and distributed across childhood and adolescence; edema, elevated hematocrit, severe plasma leakage, and hemodynamic compromise were more common in severe dengue, and all patients presented with fever.
Controversies and gaps
The study did not stratify coinfection outcomes by Plasmodium species or assess long-term sequelae, and the small sample size limits generalizability; the high mortality (10 deaths, 6 in coinfected patients) underscores diagnostic and therapeutic uncertainty in coinfection management.
Research and practice implications
Prioritize differential diagnostic algorithms for dengue–malaria coinfection in endemic settings, evaluate species-specific Plasmodium impacts on dengue severity, and implement targeted surveillance for high-risk groups (children with severe dengue and coinfected adolescents) to reduce delays in recognition and improve outcomes.
