Journal: Reviews in Medical Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-18  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70167  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 5, e70167
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

溶瘤病毒疗法中新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体:综合综述。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.
作者： Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu, Bo Li
摘要中文翻译溶瘤病毒疗法最近成为一种有前途的免疫肿瘤学策略,利用病毒病原体的天然溶细胞潜力来选择性地靶向和破坏恶性细胞。尽管各种工程病毒,如腺病毒、牛痘病毒和单纯疱疹病毒,目前正处于临床评估阶段,但具有内在溶瘤潜力的新出现和重新出现的病毒,包括寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)、登革病毒(DENV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)也显示出有希望的结果。这些新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体表现出特定的肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制,可用于下一代治疗平台。尽管如此,与安全性、病毒致病性、宿主 免疫应答 以及不受控制的病毒传播风险相关的担忧提出了重大的转化挑战。本综述全面概述了溶瘤病毒治疗中的新型候选病毒,包括 ZIKV、SARS-CoV-2、DENV 和 WNV、它们的肿瘤靶向分子机制,以及旨在提高治疗效果和生物安全性的基因工程的最新进展。此外,这篇综述描述了将新兴病毒治疗药物整合到精准肿瘤学中的临床观点、监管考虑因素和未来方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 登革病毒 (DENV) 及其在溶瘤病毒治疗中的作用,检查其肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制。它还探讨了 DENV 作为精准肿瘤学背景下的治疗剂的潜力。
证据基础与方法
该综述对溶瘤病毒疗法中新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体(包括 DENV)进行了全面分析。它讨论了肿瘤靶向的分子机制以及基因工程的最新进展,以提高治疗效果和生物安全性。该综述没有明确报告数据库、检索日期或纳入研究的资格标准。
共识与结论
登革病毒 和其他相关黄病毒,例如 SARS-CoV-2 和 ZIKV,已显示出有希望的溶瘤潜力。这些病毒具有特定的肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制,可用于下一代治疗平台。该综述强调了了解这些机制对于改善治疗结果的重要性。
争议与缺口
使用 DENV 进行溶瘤病毒治疗时,安全性问题、病毒致病性和 宿主 免疫应答 仍然是未解决的挑战。该评论指出,不受控制的病毒传播的风险和进一步临床评估的需要是转化的重大障碍。此外,缺乏有关 DENV 疗法的长期效果和最佳剂量的详细数据。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先开发具有增强治疗功效和生物安全性的基因工程 DENV 变体。需要临床试验来评估基于 DENV 的溶瘤病毒疗法在各种癌症类型中的安全性和有效性。还必须建立监管框架,以促进 DENV 融入精准肿瘤学实践。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.

Authors: Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu, Bo Li
Original abstract (English)
Oncolytic virotherapy has recently emerged as a promising immuno-oncological strategy, utilising the natural cytolytic potentials of viral pathogens to selectively target and destroy malignant cells. Although various engineered viruses, such as adenoviruses, vaccinia virus, and herpes simplex virus, are currently in clinical evaluation, emerging and re-emerging viruses with intrinsic oncolytic potential, including Zika virus (ZIKV), Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), Dengue virus (DENV), and West Nile virus (WNV), have also shown promising results. These emerging and re-emerging viral pathogens show specific tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms that could be harnessed for next-generation therapeutic platforms. Nonetheless, concerns related to safety, viral pathogenicity, host immune responses, and the risk of uncontrolled viral dissemination present significant translational challenges. The present review provides a comprehensive overview of novel viral candidates, including ZIKV, SARS-CoV-2, DENV, and WNV, in oncolytic virotherapy, their molecular mechanisms of tumour targeting, and current advancements in genetic engineering aimed at improving therapeutic efficacy and biosafety. Moreover, this review describes clinical perspectives, regulatory considerations, and future directions in integrating emerging virotherapeutic agents into precision oncology.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on dengue virus (DENV) and its role in oncolytic virotherapy, examining its tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms. It also explores the potential of DENV as a therapeutic agent in the context of precision oncology.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of emerging and re-emerging viral pathogens in oncolytic virotherapy, including DENV. It discusses the molecular mechanisms of tumour targeting and current advancements in genetic engineering to enhance therapeutic efficacy and biosafety. The review does not explicitly report on databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria for included studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Dengue virus and other related flaviviruses, such as SARS-CoV-2 and ZIKV, have shown promising oncolytic potential. These viruses possess specific tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and mechanisms of immunogenic cell death that could be leveraged for next-generation therapeutic platforms. The review highlights the importance of understanding these mechanisms to improve therapeutic outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
Safety concerns, viral pathogenicity, and host immune responses remain unresolved challenges in the use of DENV for oncolytic virotherapy. The review notes that the risk of uncontrolled viral dissemination and the need for further clinical evaluation are significant barriers to translation. Additionally, there is a lack of detailed data on the long-term effects and optimal dosing of DENV-based therapies.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritise the development of genetically engineered DENV variants with enhanced therapeutic efficacy and biosafety. Clinical trials are needed to evaluate the safety and effectiveness of DENV-based oncolytic virotherapy in various cancer types. Regulatory frameworks must also be established to facilitate the integration of DENV into precision oncology practices.

登革病毒 诱导的内皮功能障碍的机械驱动因素

Mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction
作者： Sofía Mayordomo-Barrero, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares
摘要中文翻译全球有超过十亿人面临感染登革热的风险。登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种由伊蚊传播的 ssRNA(+)-黄病毒。虽然大多数感染是轻微的(无并发症的登革热),但严重/出血性登革热携带大量 死亡率 且缺乏特定的 死亡率。重症登革热 的特点是内皮功能障碍,在原发和继发感染期间都可能出现。在后者中,感染的 抗体依赖性增强 是一个公认的危险因素,尽管在这两种情况下病毒感染与内皮屏障衰竭之间的机制联系仍不清楚。除了对内皮细胞造成炎症损伤外,DENV 和其他黄病毒还可以引发内皮细胞向间充质迁移表型的转分化,从而削弱细胞间的连接并损害血管完整性。在这篇综述中,我们讨论了黄病毒诱导的内皮转分化的机械成分及其对内皮功能障碍的影响,强调了治疗干预的前瞻性机械生物学目标。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了 重症登革热 (DENV) 感染,重点关注登革热疾病中内皮功能障碍的机械驱动因素,特别是 DENV-1 至 DENV-4 中的 重症登革热。核心问题涉及 DENV 感染期间机械和机械生物学过程如何导致内皮屏障失效,无论原发或继发感染情况如何。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一篇叙述性综合文章,讨论了 DENV 诱导的内皮转分化的机械生物学机制及其对血管完整性的影响。它强调了治疗干预的前瞻性机械生物学目标,但没有报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准或纳入研究计数。
共识与结论
DENV 感染可诱导内皮细胞向间充质迁移表型转分化,削弱细胞间连接并损害血管完整性。这一过程会导致内皮功能障碍,这是 重症登革热 的一个标志,无论感染是在初次还是二次暴露期间发生。
争议与缺口
DENV 感染与内皮屏障衰竭之间的机制联系仍不清楚,特别是继发感染中的 抗体依赖性增强 如何影响这些过程。此外,驱动内皮转分化的特定分子途径及其对血管损害的相对贡献尚未完全解决。
科研与实践启示
应优先考虑治疗干预的预期机械生物学目标,以减轻登革热的内皮功能障碍。需要进一步的研究来阐明 DENV 诱导的内皮转分化的分子机制及其在 重症登革热 发病机制 中的作用。

Mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction

Authors: Sofía Mayordomo-Barrero, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares
Original abstract (English)
More than one billion individuals worldwide are at risk of contracting dengue. Dengue virus (DENV) is a ssRNA(+)-flavivirus transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes. While most infections are mild (uncomplicated dengue), severe/hemorrhagic dengue carries substantial mortality and lacks specific treatment. Severe dengue is characterized by endothelial dysfunction, which can arise during both primary and secondary infection. In the latter, antibody-dependent enhancement of infection is a well-recognized risk factor, although the mechanistic links between viral infection and endothelial barrier failure remain unclear in either setting. Beyond inflammatory injury to the endothelium, DENV and other flaviviruses can trigger endothelial trans-differentiation toward a mesenchymal, migratory phenotype, weakening cell–cell junctions and compromising vascular integrity. In this review, we discuss the mechanical component of flavivirus-induced endothelial trans-differentiation and its impact on endothelial dysfunction, highlighting prospective mechanobiological targets for therapeutic intervention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines dengue virus (DENV) infection, focusing on the mechanical drivers of endothelial dysfunction in dengue disease, particularly severe dengue, across DENV-1 to DENV-4. The central question addresses how mechanical and mechanobiological processes contribute to endothelial barrier failure during DENV infection, irrespective of primary or secondary infection contexts.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis that discusses mechanobiological mechanisms underlying DENV-induced endothelial trans-differentiation and its impact on vascular integrity. It highlights prospective mechanobiological targets for therapeutic intervention without reporting databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
DENV infection can induce endothelial trans-differentiation toward a mesenchymal, migratory phenotype, weakening cell–cell junctions and compromising vascular integrity. This process contributes to endothelial dysfunction, a hallmark of severe dengue, regardless of whether infection occurs during primary or secondary exposure.
Controversies and gaps
The mechanistic links between DENV infection and endothelial barrier failure remain unclear, particularly regarding how antibody-dependent enhancement in secondary infections influences these processes. Additionally, the specific molecular pathways driving endothelial trans-differentiation and their relative contributions to vascular compromise are not fully resolved.
Research and practice implications
Prospective mechanobiological targets for therapeutic intervention should be prioritized to mitigate endothelial dysfunction in dengue. Further research is needed to elucidate the molecular mechanisms underlying DENV-induced endothelial trans-differentiation and its role in severe dengue pathogenesis.

继本土 监测 系统实施后,对伊朗南部登革热 监测 系统实施的定性评估

A qualitative evaluation of dengue surveillance system implementation in southern Iran following autochthonous transmission
作者： Mehrdad Salehi, Javad Moghri, Ehsan Mousa Farkhani, Amir Ghasemian, Seyed Saeed Tabatabaee, Elaheh Hooshmand
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种重新出现的蚊媒病毒性疾病,它对全球健康造成的威胁不断升级,气候变化和全球化加剧了这种威胁。虽然伊朗历史上只报告了输入性登革热病例,但继 2024 年首次确诊当地 传播 后,南部省份(特别是霍尔木兹甘)也记录了本土 传播 病例。因此,了解利益相关者对国家登革热 监测 系统实施的看法对于为循证准备和政策制定提供信息至关重要。这项回顾性定性研究于 2025 年 2 月至 3 月在霍尔木兹甘省进行。通过半结构化访谈收集数据,并使用 CDC 监测 评估框架指导下的定向内容分析进行分析。监测 的关键属性——包括简单性、数据质量、可接受性、敏感性、代表性和及时性——被用作分析类别来解释参与者对系统性能的看法。十九名参与者——包括高级和中层管理人员、公共卫生专家和省级官员——是通过有目的的抽样招募的,差异最大,直到达到数据饱和。使用 MAXQDA 2020 分析数据,并根据 Lincoln 和 Guba 的标准确保可信度。出现了五个主要主题:(1) 流程的简单性和系统适应性,(2) 数据质量、集成和可接受性挑战,(3) 早期检测、临床识别和诊断工作流程限制,(4) 全面的人群覆盖和快速响应,(5) 系统可持续性和数据利用挑战。参与者认为参考实验室和成功的地方举措是重要的优势,同时也面临着并行报告系统、私营部门参与有限、早期疾病期间快速诊断检测结果假阴性、流动人口覆盖不完整以及数字基础设施不稳定等挑战。研究结果表明,伊朗的登革热监测正在从关注输入病例转变为本地监测的管理。与会者强调了与数据整合、部门间协调和弱势群体覆盖相关的挑战,尽管现有 监测 能力。加强 监测 集成、数字报告系统、早期诊断能力和跨部门协作可以提高及时性、覆盖范围和整体准备情况。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查在伊朗南部本土 传播 的背景下审查了 登革病毒(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)和登革热疾病,重点关注国家登革热 监测 系统的实施和利益相关者的准备需求。中心问题解决了继 2024 年首次确认 监测 后,监测 系统属性如何影响本地获得的登革热病例的检测和管理。
证据基础与方法
这项回顾性定性研究于 2025 年 2 月至 3 月在伊朗霍尔木兹甘省进行。通过半结构化访谈收集了 19 名有意抽样的参与者,包括高级和中层管理人员、公共卫生专家和省级官员,直到数据饱和。分析使用以 CDC 监测 评估框架为指导的定向内容分析,并通过 MAXQDA 2020 和林肯和古巴的标准确保可信度。
共识与结论
该审查确定了表征登革热 监测 性能的五个主要主题:流程的简单性和适应性、数据质量和集成挑战、早期检测和诊断工作流程的局限性、全面人口覆盖和快速响应方面的差距以及可持续性和数据利用问题。主要优势包括参考实验室和成功的地方举措,而挑战包括并行报告系统、有限的私营部门参与、快速诊断检测结果的假阴性、移民人口覆盖不完整以及数字基础设施不稳定。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了尚未解决的问题,例如早期疾病期间快速诊断检测不可靠、流动人口覆盖不完全以及数字基础设施不稳定等。此外,尽管现有 监测 能力,但在部门间协调和数据集成方面仍存在差距。该研究的定性性质限制了普遍性,并且没有量化 监测 绩效指标。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议加强 监测 整合,增强数字报告系统,提高早期诊断能力,并促进跨部门合作,以解决本地传播登革热的及时性、覆盖范围和防备问题。它强调需要从关注输入病例转向管理本地 传播,特别是在霍尔木兹甘等南部省份。

A qualitative evaluation of dengue surveillance system implementation in southern Iran following autochthonous transmission

Authors: Mehrdad Salehi, Javad Moghri, Ehsan Mousa Farkhani, Amir Ghasemian, Seyed Saeed Tabatabaee, Elaheh Hooshmand
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever, a re-emerging mosquito-borne viral disease, poses an escalating global health threat exacerbated by climate change and globalization. While Iran has historically reported only imported dengue cases, autochthonous transmission has been documented in southern provinces, particularly Hormozgan, following the first confirmed local transmission in 2024. Understanding stakeholders’ perspectives on the implementation of the national dengue surveillance system is therefore critical for informing evidence-based preparedness and policy-making. This retrospective qualitative study was conducted in Hormozgan Province between February and March 2025. Data were collected through semi-structured interviews and analyzed using directed content analysis guided by the CDC surveillance evaluation framework. Key surveillance attributes—including simplicity, data quality, acceptability, sensitivity, representativeness, and timeliness—were used as analytical categories to interpret participants’ perceptions of system performance. Nineteen participants—including senior and mid-level managers, public health experts, and provincial officials—were recruited through purposive sampling with maximum variation until data saturation was achieved. Data were analyzed using MAXQDA 2020, and trustworthiness was ensured based on Lincoln and Guba’s criteria. Five major themes emerged: (1) Simplicity of processes and system adaptability, (2) Data quality, integration, and acceptability challenges, (3) Early detection, clinical recognition, and diagnostic workflow limitations, (4) Comprehensive population coverage and rapid response, and (5) System sustainability and data utilization challenges. Participants identified reference laboratories and successful local initiatives as important strengths, alongside challenges such as parallel reporting systems, limited private-sector engagement, perceived false-negative rapid diagnostic test results during early-stage illness, incomplete migrant population coverage, and unstable digital infrastructure. The findings suggest that dengue surveillance in Iran is transitioning from a focus on imported cases to the management of local transmission. Participants highlighted challenges related to data integration, intersectoral coordination, and the coverage of vulnerable populations despite existing surveillance capacities. Strengthening surveillance integration, digital reporting systems, early diagnostic capacity, and cross-sector collaboration may improve timeliness, coverage, and overall preparedness.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines dengue virus (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and dengue disease in the context of autochthonous transmission in southern Iran, focusing on the implementation of the national dengue surveillance system and stakeholders’ preparedness needs. The central question addresses how surveillance system attributes influence the detection and management of locally acquired dengue cases following the first confirmed transmission in 2024.
Evidence base and method
This retrospective qualitative study was conducted in Hormozgan Province, Iran, between February and March 2025. Data were collected via semi-structured interviews with 19 purposively sampled participants, including senior and mid-level managers, public health experts, and provincial officials, until data saturation. Analysis used directed content analysis guided by the CDC surveillance evaluation framework, with MAXQDA 2020 and trustworthiness ensured via Lincoln and Guba’s criteria.
Consensus and conclusions
The review identifies five major themes characterizing dengue surveillance performance: simplicity and adaptability of processes, data quality and integration challenges, limitations in early detection and diagnostic workflows, gaps in comprehensive population coverage and rapid response, and sustainability and data utilization issues. Key strengths include reference laboratories and successful local initiatives, while challenges include parallel reporting systems, limited private-sector engagement, perceived false-negative rapid diagnostic test results, incomplete migrant population coverage, and unstable digital infrastructure.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights unresolved issues such as the perceived unreliability of rapid diagnostic tests during early-stage illness, incomplete coverage of migrant populations, and unstable digital infrastructure. Additionally, there are gaps in intersectoral coordination and data integration, despite existing surveillance capacities. The qualitative nature of the study limits generalizability and does not quantify surveillance performance metrics.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends strengthening surveillance integration, enhancing digital reporting systems, improving early diagnostic capacity, and fostering cross-sector collaboration to address timeliness, coverage, and preparedness for locally transmitted dengue. It emphasizes the need to transition from a focus on imported cases to managing local transmission, particularly in southern provinces like Hormozgan.

共流行环境中基孔肯雅病毒血清学交叉反应:机制、诊断意义和流行病学后果

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences
作者： Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, O. Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 会引起急性发热性疾病并伴有关节痛,血清学检测仍然是其 诊断 和 监测 的核心。然而,在多种虫媒病毒共同循环的环境中,基于抗体的结果因交叉反应而变得复杂。本综述区分了影响 CHIKV 血清学 的三种相关但不同的现象:生物交叉反应性,由相关病毒之间真正的抗原重叠引起;与检测相关的非目标反应性,其中诊断平台在病毒之间没有建立生物学关系的情况下产生阳性信号;诊断错误分类,将两者混为一谈的下游流行病学后果。将该框架应用于五个病毒对,我们发现CHIKV-O'nyong-nyong病毒(ONNV)、CHIKV-Mayaro病毒(MAYV)和CHIKV-罗斯河病毒(RRV)关系得到了共享的甲病毒抗原结构的支持,并且在一些研究中还得到了功能性交叉中和的支持。相比之下,登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 相关血清中报告的 CHIKV 反应性缺乏既定的生物学基础,最好归类为未解决机制的检测相关非靶标反应性。这些区别很重要:未经调整的血清阳性率可能会夸大或掩盖 CHIKV 传播 的估计值,并且没有一个诊断平台可以解决每个病毒对的问题。我们认为,共流行环境中的 CHIKV 诊断 需要分层策略:急性期进行分子检测,随后进行抗原告知和多重 血清学,以及基于中和的确认(其中归因至关重要),并结合 监测 的交叉反应感知统计模型。加强分析验证和确认性检测,特别是在尼日利亚等 CHIKV、DENV 和相关甲病毒共同传播的非洲地区,对于提高诊断准确性和流行病学推断至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了在共流行环境中影响基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 诊断的血清学交叉反应性,重点关注病原体 CHIKV、其疾病表现,以及如何改进基于抗体的检测以避免在多种虫媒病毒共同传播的地区发生错误分类的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,使用 CHIKV 和相关甲病毒(包括 CHIKV-O'nyong-nyong 病毒 (ONNV)、CHIKV-Mayaro 病毒 (MAYV) 和 CHIKV-Ross River 病毒 (RRV))研究的证据区分了三种现象——生物交叉反应性、检测相关的非靶标反应性和诊断错误分类,同时指出 CHIKV 反应性缺乏生物学基础。登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 相关血清。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,CHIKV 与 ONNV、MAYV 和 RRV 具有相同的抗原结构,并得到多项研究中共享的甲病毒抗原结构和功能性交叉中和的支持,而 DENV 和 ZIKV 相关血清中的 CHIKV 反应性在没有既定生物学基础的情况下,更好地归类为与检测相关的非靶标反应性。
争议与缺口
该审查强调,未经调整的血清阳性率可能会夸大或掩盖 CHIKV 传播 的估计值,并且没有一个诊断平台可以解决每个病毒对的交叉反应性,特别是在多种虫媒病毒一起传播的共流行环境中。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议在共流行环境下对 CHIKV 采取分层诊断策略:急性期进行分子检测,之后进行抗原信息和多重 血清学,基于中和的确认(其中归因至关重要),以及 监测 的交叉反应感知统计模型,重点是加强尼日利亚等非洲地区的测定验证和确认性测试。

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences

Authors: Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, O. Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes an acute febrile illness with debilitating arthralgia, and serological assays remain central to its diagnosis and surveillance. However, antibody-based results are complicated by cross-reactivity in settings where multiple arboviruses co-circulate. This review distinguishes three related but distinct phenomena affecting CHIKV serology: biological cross-reactivity, arising from genuine antigenic overlap between related viruses; assay-associated non-target reactivity, in which a diagnostic platform generates a positive signal without an established biological relationship between the viruses; and diagnostic misclassification, the downstream epidemiological consequence of conflating the two. Applying this framework to five virus pairs, we find that CHIKV–O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), CHIKV–Mayaro virus (MAYV) and CHIKV–Ross River virus (RRV) relationships are supported by shared alphavirus antigenic architecture and, in several studies, by functional cross-neutralisation. By contrast, CHIKV reactivity reported in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera lacks an established biological basis and is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity of unresolved mechanism. These distinctions matter: unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves the problem for every virus pair. We argue that CHIKV diagnosis in co-endemic settings requires a layered strategy: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, and neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, combined with cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance. Strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing, particularly in African settings such as Nigeria where CHIKV, DENV and related alphaviruses circulate together, is essential for improving diagnostic accuracy and epidemiological inference.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines serological cross-reactivity affecting Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) diagnostics in co-endemic settings, focusing on the pathogen CHIKV, its disease manifestations, and the central question of how antibody-based assays can be improved to avoid misclassification in regions where multiple arboviruses co-circulate.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that distinguishes three phenomena—biological cross-reactivity, assay-associated non-target reactivity, and diagnostic misclassification—using evidence from studies on CHIKV and related alphaviruses, including CHIKV–O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), CHIKV–Mayaro virus (MAYV), and CHIKV–Ross River virus (RRV), while noting the lack of biological basis for CHIKV reactivity in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CHIKV shares antigenic architecture with ONNV, MAYV, and RRV, supported by shared alphavirus antigenic structure and functional cross-neutralisation in several studies, whereas CHIKV reactivity in DENV- and ZIKV-associated sera is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity without an established biological basis.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves cross-reactivity for every virus pair, particularly in co-endemic settings where multiple arboviruses circulate together.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends a layered diagnostic strategy for CHIKV in co-endemic settings: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, and cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance, with a focus on strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing in African settings such as Nigeria.

登革热 患者的特征性皮疹:病例系列

Characteristic skin rash among patients with dengue fever: case series
作者： Ahlam Alzahrani, Abdullah Albadri, Sultan Alfaer, Mawaddah Tallab, Mohammed K. Alghamdi, Hamza Alamoudi
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热 (DF) 是由 登革病毒 引起的,表现为发烧、不适和头痛等全身症状。超过50%的病例存在多种皮肤病变。病例介绍:我们介绍了一系列三个病例,这些病例到诊所就诊时出现发烧并伴有胃肠道症状,包括恶心、呕吐和腹痛。其他系统评价并无异常。患者随后出现瘙痒性皮疹,其特征是弥漫性融合性红斑,伴有瘀点和圆形岛屿。在排除疟疾、艾滋病毒和丙型肝炎等其他病因后,所有三名患者均被诊断为 DF。结论:该病例系列强调了 DF 中存在特征性皮疹。我们强调这种皮肤表现的临床描述,这在以前的文献中没有得到很好的描述。尽管皮肤表现在登革热中很常见,但识别这些特定的皮疹模式有助于准确和早期 诊断。在流行地区出现发烧和皮疹的患者应怀疑患有 DF。

Characteristic skin rash among patients with dengue fever: case series

Authors: Ahlam Alzahrani, Abdullah Albadri, Sultan Alfaer, Mawaddah Tallab, Mohammed K. Alghamdi, Hamza Alamoudi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever (DF) is caused by the dengue virus, which manifests with systemic features such as fever, malaise, and headache. More than 50% of cases present with a variety of skin lesions. Case Presentation: We present a series of three cases that presented to the clinic with fever associated with gastrointestinal symptoms, including nausea, vomiting, and abdominal pain. Other systematic reviews were unremarkable. The patients subsequently developed a pruritic skin rash characterized by diffuse confluent erythema with petechiae and rounded islands of sparing. After excluding alternative etiologies such as malaria, HIV, and hepatitis C, all three patients were diagnosed with DF. Conclusion: This case series highlights the presence of a characteristic rash in DF. We emphasize the clinical description of this cutaneous manifestation, which is not well-described in previous literature. Although cutaneous manifestations are common in dengue, identifying these specific rash patterns can contribute to accurate and early diagnosis. Patients presenting with fever and rashes in an endemic area should be suspected of having DF.

Dengue fever (DF) is caused by the dengue virus, which manifests with systemic features such as fever, malaise, and headache. More than 50% of cases present with a variety of skin lesions. The patients subsequently developed a pruritic skin rash characterized by diffuse confluent erythema with petechiae and rounded islands of sparing.
Evidence base and method
After excluding alternative etiologies such as malaria, HIV, and hepatitis C, all three patients were diagnosed with DF. Patients presenting with fever and rashes in an endemic area should be suspected of having DF.
Consensus and conclusions
This case series highlights the presence of a characteristic rash in DF. We emphasize the clinical description of this cutaneous manifestation, which is not well-described in previous literature.
Controversies and gaps
Other systematic reviews were unremarkable.
Research and practice implications
Although cutaneous manifestations are common in dengue, identifying these specific rash patterns can contribute to accurate and early diagnosis.

肝脏类器官:从疾病建模到再生医学

Liver Organoids: From Disease Modelling to Regenerative Medicine
作者： Tiepeng Wang, Xiaotian Qu, Jinhong Si, Junkang Meng, Ting Zhang, Shuliang Niu
摘要中文翻译摘要 肝脏类器官是三维微型肝脏模型,在体外概括了人类肝脏的复杂结构和关键功能,为基础研究和转化应用提供了强大的平台。这篇综述系统地总结了肝脏类器官当前的制造策略、疾病建模实用性和再生潜力,以及主要挑战和未来方向。近年来,该领域取得了多项突破。通过内皮共培养方法,已成功生成血管化和代谢分区的肝脏类器官,内皮覆盖率超过 85%。Prime 编辑能够精确校正患者来源的类器官中的致病性 突变,并且在 基因组 范围内未检测到脱靶效应。在疾病建模中,iPSC 衍生的肝脏类器官忠实地再现了代谢功能障碍相关脂肪肝病 (MASLD) 的病理进展,并验证了索马鲁肽的降脂功效。巨噬细胞整合类器官模型支持 HEV、SARS-CoV-2 和 登革病毒 的整个生命周期,为 抗病毒药物 药物筛选提供新工具。大规模患者来源的肿瘤类器官生物库成功保留了肝癌的异质性和临床耐药特征。在再生医学中,封装的肝细胞类器官和 UTOpiA 生物人工肝系统已有效挽救动物模型中的急性肝功能衰竭,而基因编辑的自体类器官则为威尔逊病等遗传性疾病提供了潜在的治疗策略。然而,肝细胞功能成熟度不足、血管网络构建困难以及缺乏标准化培养方案仍然是临床转化的主要障碍。通过连接基础肝脏生物学和临床实践,肝脏类器官技术为精准肝病学和再生治疗奠定了坚实的基础。仍需要持续的跨学科努力来克服当前的局限性并促进其临床采用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该范围研究了肝脏类器官作为人类肝脏生物学和转化应用的体外模型,其核心问题是它们在疾病建模和再生医学中的效用。主题边界不包括非肝脏系统和非类器官平台。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇叙述性综合文章,总结了肝脏类器官的制造策略、疾病建模实用程序和再生潜力。它报告了一些突破,例如内皮共培养实现了 >85% 的内皮覆盖率和没有脱靶效应的初等编辑,但没有提供数据库、搜索日期、资格标准或纳入研究计数。
共识与结论
达成一致的主要领域包括通过内皮共培养成功生成血管化和代谢分区的肝脏类器官,使用prime编辑精确校正患者来源的类器官中的致病性突变,以及重演MASLD病理学并验证索马鲁肽在iPSC来源的肝脏类器官中的功效。
争议与缺口
尚未解决的主要挑战包括肝细胞功能成熟度不足、血管网络构建困难以及缺乏标准化培养方案,这些都阻碍了临床转化。此外,虽然巨噬细胞整合的类器官模型支持某些病毒的整个生命周期,但它们与 登革病毒 的相关性仍未得到解决。
科研与实践启示
具体优先事项包括促进肝细胞功能成熟、标准化培养方案以及开发用于临床应用的血管化类器官系统。该审查还强调需要跨学科努力来克服当前的局限性并促进肝脏类器官技术的临床采用。

Liver Organoids: From Disease Modelling to Regenerative Medicine

Authors: Tiepeng Wang, Xiaotian Qu, Jinhong Si, Junkang Meng, Ting Zhang, Shuliang Niu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Liver organoids are three‐dimensional miniature liver models that recapitulate the complex architecture and key functions of the human liver in vitro, offering powerful platforms for both fundamental research and translational applications. This review systematically summarises current fabrication strategies, disease‐modelling utilities and regenerative potentials of liver organoids, alongside the major challenges and future directions. In recent years, the field has witnessed several breakthroughs. Through endothelial co‐culture approaches, vascularised and metabolically zonated liver organoids have been successfully generated, achieving endothelial coverage exceeding 85%. Prime editing enables precise correction of pathogenic mutations in patient‐derived organoids, with no off‐target effects detected at the genome‐wide level. In disease modelling, iPSC‐derived liver organoids faithfully recapitulate the pathological progression of metabolic dysfunction‐associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) and verify the lipid‐lowering efficacy of semaglutide. Macrophage‐integrated organoid models support the full life cycles of HEV, SARS‐CoV‐2 and dengue virus, providing new tools for antiviral drug screening. Large‐scale patient‐derived tumour organoid biobanks successfully preserve the heterogeneity and clinical drug‐resistance signatures of liver cancers. In regenerative medicine, encapsulated hepatocyte organoids and the UTOpiA bioartificial liver system have effectively rescued acute liver failure in animal models, while gene‐edited autologous organoids offer potential curative strategies for genetic disorders such as Wilson disease. Nevertheless, insufficient hepatocyte functional maturity, difficulties in constructing vascular networks, and the lack of standardised culture protocols remain major obstacles to clinical translation. By bridging fundamental liver biology and clinical practice, liver organoid technology lays a solid foundation for precision hepatology and regenerative therapies. Continued interdisciplinary efforts are still required to overcome current limitations and facilitate its clinical adoption.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The scope examines liver organoids as in vitro models for human liver biology and translational applications, with a central question on their utility in disease modelling and regenerative medicine. The topic boundary excludes non-liver systems and non-organoid platforms.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis summarising fabrication strategies, disease-modelling utilities, and regenerative potentials of liver organoids. It reports breakthroughs such as endothelial co-culture achieving >85% endothelial coverage and prime editing with no off-target effects, but does not provide databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
Major areas of agreement include the successful generation of vascularised and metabolically zonated liver organoids through endothelial co-culture, precise correction of pathogenic mutations in patient-derived organoids using prime editing, and the recapitulation of MASLD pathology with verification of semaglutide efficacy in iPSC-derived liver organoids.
Controversies and gaps
Key unresolved challenges include insufficient hepatocyte functional maturity, difficulties in constructing vascular networks, and the lack of standardised culture protocols, which hinder clinical translation. Additionally, while macrophage-integrated organoid models support the full life cycles of certain viruses, their relevance to dengue virus remains unaddressed.
Research and practice implications
Specific priorities include advancing hepatocyte functional maturity, standardising culture protocols, and developing vascularised organoid systems for clinical applications. The review also highlights the need for interdisciplinary efforts to overcome current limitations and facilitate clinical adoption of liver organoid technology.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

针对新兴 RNA 病毒的网络驱动的 抗病毒药物 发现管道:来自 Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de 抗病毒药物 (RMDA) 的关键视角。

A network-driven antiviral discovery pipeline for emerging RNA viruses: a critical perspective from the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA).
作者： Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Alan Orlando Santos-Mena, Carlos Noé Farfán-Morales, Moisés León-Juárez, José Manuel Reyes-Ruíz, Juan Fidel Osuna-Ramos, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Flor Itzel Lira-Hernández, Rosa María Del Ángel
摘要中文翻译简介:RNA 病毒对拉丁美洲新出现和重新出现的传染病造成了不成比例的负担,但该地区的 抗病毒药物 发现仍然支离破碎、目标有限,并且经常与转化途径脱节。本视角讨论了 Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de 抗病毒药物 (RMDA) 作为网络驱动模型来组织针对临床相关 RNA 病毒的 抗病毒药物 发现。涵盖领域:作者描述了 RMDA 的概念架构,这是一项墨西哥多机构倡议,整合了计算优先级、BSL-2/BSL-3 条件下基于细胞的 抗病毒药物 评估、机械病毒学、药理学协同评估、体内验证以及通过医疗保健系统(例如 Instituto Mexicano del Seguro Social)的临床联系。该框架得到了参与小组先前关于 宿主 导向和病毒导向候选药物的工作的支持,包括代谢调节剂、降脂剂、核转运抑制剂、天然产物、多酚、褪黑激素和针对 登革病毒、寨卡病毒、SARS-CoV-2、呼吸道合胞病毒和相关 RNA 病毒进行评估的合理药物组合。专家意见:RMDA 为中低收入环境中的 抗病毒药物 药物发现提供了一个可扩展且区域适应性强的模型。通过协调分布式专业知识、标准化工作流程和转化决策点,该框架可以加速候选者的优先级排序、减少重复并加强墨西哥和拉丁美洲针对 RNA 病毒威胁的准备。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本文重点关注 登革病毒 作为拉丁美洲代表性的新兴 RNA 病原体,解决了零散且目标有限的 抗病毒药物 发现前景,并探讨网络驱动模型 (RMDA) 如何协调计算优先级、体外测试、机制研究和临床联系,以改善登革热和相关病毒的 抗病毒药物 开发。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据表明该文件是一篇前瞻性文章,而不是系统综述;没有报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准或正式的研究选择程序。作者将 RMDA 描述为一个概念框架,但没有详细说明系统的证据收集方法。
共识与结论
作者一致认为,RMDA 构成了一个可扩展、区域适应性强的平台,集成了计算筛选、BSL-2/3 基于细胞的检测、机械病毒学、协同评估、体内验证和临床联系,并且该模型可以增强中低收入环境中的 抗病毒药物 发现能力。
争议与缺口
摘要没有提供登革热特异性候选药物的实证结果,也没有讨论相互矛盾的数据或方法学局限性,从而在 登革病毒 的 RMDA 管道的已证明功效以及与其他 抗病毒药物 方法的比较性能方面留下了差距。
科研与实践启示
通过协调分散的专业知识、标准化工作流程和定义转化决策点,RMDA 框架可以加快候选者的优先顺序、减少重复工作,并加强墨西哥和拉丁美洲针对登革热和其他 RNA 病毒威胁的准备。

A network-driven antiviral discovery pipeline for emerging RNA viruses: a critical perspective from the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA).

Authors: Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Alan Orlando Santos-Mena, Carlos Noé Farfán-Morales, Moisés León-Juárez, José Manuel Reyes-Ruíz, Juan Fidel Osuna-Ramos, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Flor Itzel Lira-Hernández, Rosa María Del Ángel
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: RNA viruses impose a disproportionate burden of emerging and reemerging infectious diseases in Latin America, yet antiviral discovery in the region remains fragmented, target-limited, and frequently disconnected from translational pathways. This perspective discusses the Red Mexicana de Desarrollo de Antivirales (RMDA) as a network-driven model to organize antiviral discovery against clinically relevant RNA viruses. AREAS COVERED: The authors describe the conceptual architecture of RMDA, a multi-institutional Mexican initiative that integrates computational prioritization, cell-based antiviral evaluation under BSL-2/BSL-3 conditions, mechanistic virology, assessment of pharmacological synergy, in vivo validation, and clinical linkage through healthcare systems such as the Instituto Mexicano del Seguro Social. The framework is supported by prior work from participating groups on host-directed and virus-directed candidates, including metabolic modulators, lipid-lowering agents, nuclear transport inhibitors, natural products, polyphenols, melatonin, and rational drug combinations evaluated against dengue virus, Zika virus, SARS-CoV-2, respiratory syncytial virus, and related RNA viruses. EXPERT OPINION: RMDA provides a scalable and regionally adaptable model for antiviral drug discovery in low- and middle-income settings. By aligning distributed expertise, standardized workflows, and translational decision points, this framework may accelerate candidate prioritization, reduce duplication, and strengthen preparedness against RNA virus threats in Mexico and Latin America.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The article focuses on dengue virus as a representative emerging RNA pathogen in Latin America, addressing the fragmented and target‐limited antiviral discovery landscape and asking how a network‐driven model (RMDA) can coordinate computational prioritization, in‐vitro testing, mechanistic studies, and clinical linkage to improve antiviral development for dengue and related viruses.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence indicates that the document is a perspective article rather than a systematic review; no databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or formal study selection procedures are reported. The authors describe RMDA as a conceptual framework without detailing a systematic evidence‐gathering methodology.
Consensus and conclusions
The authors agree that RMDA constitutes a scalable, regionally adaptable platform that integrates computational screening, BSL‐2/3 cell‐based assays, mechanistic virology, synergy assessment, in‐vivo validation, and clinical linkage, and that this model can strengthen antiviral discovery capacity in low‐ and middle‐income settings.
Controversies and gaps
The abstract provides no empirical outcomes for dengue‐specific candidates, nor does it discuss conflicting data or methodological limitations, leaving a gap in demonstrated efficacy of the RMDA pipeline for dengue virus and in comparative performance versus other antiviral approaches.
Research and practice implications
By aligning distributed expertise, standardizing workflows, and defining translational decision points, the RMDA framework may accelerate candidate prioritization, reduce duplication of effort, and enhance preparedness against dengue and other RNA virus threats in Mexico and Latin America.

National Dengue Review 2026 identifies priorities to strengthen Sri Lanka's dengue preparedness and response - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报世界卫生组织 (WHO) 于 2026 年 8 月 15 日发布了《2026 年国家登革热审查》,概述了加强斯里兰卡登革热防备和应对的优先事项。该审查是一份官方声明,未提供具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。它强调需要改进管理登革热 暴发 的策略,强调准备和应对机制的重要性。该审查并未表明正在进行的调查或采取的具体行动,但它表明世界卫生组织正在积极努力加强斯里兰卡的登革热管理。重点是通过战略规划和运营改进来解决登革热的潜在影响。

时间
《2026年登革热审查报告》于2026-08-15发布。该报告讨论了加强登革热防范与应对的优先事项,这是一项当前倡议。
地点与对象
此次审查的重点是斯里兰卡,该国正在考虑进行登革热防备和应对工作。

世界卫生组织 (WHO) 发布了《2026 年国家登革热审查》,确定了加强斯里兰卡登革热防备和应对工作的优先事项。这是世界卫生组织的官方声明。
规模、影响与风险
该审查概述了加强登革热准备和应对机制的必要性,但没有提供具体的病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。风险没有明确说明,但审查意味着重点是减轻登革热 暴发 的潜在影响。
应对、状态与不确定性
该审查是一份战略文件,概述了加强登革热防备和应对的优先事项。它没有表明正在进行的调查或采取的具体行动,但它表明世界卫生组织正在积极致力于改善斯里兰卡的登革热管理。
A high priorityPublic-health news

National Dengue Review 2026 identifies priorities to strengthen Sri Lanka's dengue preparedness and response - World Health Organization (WHO)

News Brief
The World Health Organization (WHO) published a National Dengue Review 2026 on August 15, 2026, which outlines priorities to strengthen Sri Lanka's dengue preparedness and response. The review is an official statement and does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers. It emphasizes the need for improved strategies to manage dengue outbreaks, highlighting the importance of preparedness and response mechanisms. The review does not indicate an ongoing investigation or specific actions taken, but it suggests that the WHO is actively working on enhancing dengue management in Sri Lanka. The focus is on addressing the potential impact of dengue through strategic planning and operational improvements.

太平洋地区的登革热:多国情况(截至 2026 年 8 月 14 日) - ReliefWeb

Dengue in the Pacific: Multicountry Situation (As of 14 August 2026) - ReliefWeb
新闻简报截至2026年8月14日,自2025年1月以来,已有12起登革热暴发影响太平洋岛屿国家和地区,2025年记录了21,258例确诊病例和21例死亡,2026年1月至7月初报告了4,545例病例、175例住院治疗和3例死亡。太平洋综合症监测系统已记录迄今为止,已有9,050例登革热类疾病病例,与去年同期相比有所下降。登革热血清型 1 和 2 正在流行,美属萨摩亚、库克群岛、萨摩亚和新喀里多尼亚占 2026 例病例的 94%。暴发 已在库克群岛和汤加正式宣布结束,而瓦努阿图谢法省仍然活跃于 DENV-1 传播。低级别的 传播 在多个地区继续存在,强调了持续 监测 和 媒介 控制的必要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 8 月 14 日发布;活动期间2025年1月1日至2026年8月14日。
地点与对象
太平洋岛屿国家和地区 (PIC):美属萨摩亚、库克群岛、斐济、法属波利尼西亚、基里巴斯、纳奥罗、萨摩亚、汤加、图瓦卢、新喀里多尼亚、谢法省(瓦努阿图)、瓦利斯和富图纳。
事件
SPC和WHO报告的多国登革热暴发;截至 2026 年 8 月 14 日,多个 PIC 中正在进行 传播。
规模、影响与风险
2025年确诊病例21,258例,死亡21例;2026年1月1日至7月2日期间,确诊病例4,545例,住院治疗175例,死亡3例;通过 PSSS 报告了 9,050 例登革热类疾病病例;DENV-1和DENV-2正在循环;2026 例病例中 94% 集中在美属萨摩亚、库克群岛、萨摩亚和新喀里多尼亚。
应对、状态与不确定性
暴发在库克群岛(2026年5月25日)和汤加(2026年7月31日)宣布结束;谢法省(瓦努阿图)的 DENV-1 仍在继续传播;低级别 传播 在美属萨摩亚、基里巴斯、新喀里多尼亚、萨摩亚、瓦努阿图以及瓦利斯和富图纳持续存在;监测 通过 PSSS;血清型 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 得到确认。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Dengue in the Pacific: Multicountry Situation (As of 14 August 2026) - ReliefWeb

News Brief
As of 14 August 2026, twelve dengue outbreaks have affected Pacific Island Countries and Areas since January 2025, with 21,258 confirmed cases and 21 deaths recorded in 2025 and 4,545 cases, 175 hospitalisations, and 3 deaths reported from January to early July 2026. The Pacific Syndromic Surveillance System has logged 9,050 dengue-like illness cases to date, a decline compared with the same period last year. Dengue serotypes 1 and 2 are circulating, with American Samoa, Cook Islands, Samoa, and New Caledonia accounting for 94% of 2026 cases. Outbreaks have been officially declared over in the Cook Islands and Tonga, while Shefa Province in Vanuatu remains active with DENV-1 transmission. Low-level transmission continues in several territories, underscoring the need for sustained surveillance and vector control.
Original source text (English)

A total of 21,258 confirmed cases and 21 deaths were recorded across the Pacific. Fatalities were reported in Fiji (n=8), Samoa (n=7), Tonga (n=3), Naoero (n=2), and Kiribati (n=1). • Between 1 January 2026 and 2 July 2026, a total of 4 545 dengue confirmed cases, 175 hospitalisations and 3 deaths have been reported across the Pacific. • The Pacific Syndromic Surveillance System (PSSS) has reported a total of 9 050 Dengue-like Illness (DLI) cases to date, a lower trend compared to the same period last year. • In 2026 dengue outbreaks were officially declared in New Caledonia, Tonga and Shefa province Vanuatu. • The most affected countries and areas in 2026 include: American Samoa, Cook Islands, Samoa, and New Caledonia. These account for 94% of confirmed cases. • Dengue serotype-1 (DENV-1) and serotype-2 (DENV-2) are currently in circulation. • The Cook Islands officially declared the end of its dengue outbreak on 25 May 2026. • Tonga has officially declared the end of its dengue outbreak on 31 July 2026. • As of 14 August 2026: o The dengue outbreak remains ongoing in Shefa Province, Vanuatu, with dengue virus serotype 1 (DENV-1) continuing to circulate. o Low levels of transmission are observed in American Samoa, Kiribati, New Caledonia, Samoa, Vanuatu and Wallis and Futuna. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

抗病毒药物 在登革热预防中的潜力和 治疗 - Contagion Live

The Potential for Antivirals in Dengue Prophylaxis and Treatment - Contagion Live
新闻简报《新英格兰医学杂志》发表的一项 2a 期随机、安慰剂对照试验发现,口服 抗病毒药物 mosnodenvir 显着降低了暴露于受控低减毒登革热攻击的健康成年人的 DENV-3 RNA 水平和登革热相关症状,证明了剂量依赖性预防效果。该研究在美国对未接触过登革热的 18-55 岁志愿者进行,结果显示莫诺登韦总体耐受性良好,尽管发生了两起严重不良事件。尽管有这些令人鼓舞的早期结果,该药物的开发商已经停止了进一步的开发。研究结果强调,在全球 抗病毒药物 不断扩大的情况下,登革热 抗病毒药物 的需求尚未得到满足,其中包括欧洲和美国本地获得的病例,而这些地区目前尚无批准的 抗病毒药物 预防药物或 抗病毒药物。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-08-17;该事件被描述为 2025 年进行的 2a 期试验,并在 2025-2026 年文献中进行了报道。
地点与对象
美国(约翰·霍普金斯大学,马里兰州巴尔的摩;佛蒙特大学,佛蒙特州伯灵顿);18-55 岁的健康成年人,以前没有登革热暴露史或流行病旅行史。
事件
一项 2a 期随机、双盲、安慰剂对照试验评估了 抗病毒药物 mosnodenvir 预防登革热的作用;结果显示 DENV-3 RNA 水平和登革热相关症状呈剂量依赖性降低,支持潜在的预防性使用。德宾等人报道。在新英格兰医学杂志上。
规模、影响与风险
试验中没有报告临床病例或死亡;小样本量(健康志愿者)限制了人口层面的影响评估。莫诺登韦通常耐受性良好;报告了两起严重不良事件(SARS-CoV-2 感染;脂肪酶和葡萄糖升高)。登革热在全球范围内蔓延,欧洲和美国本地出现病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
调查正在进行中;2a 期试验已完成,并取得了有希望的早期结果。普遍性受到小样本、低减毒 DENV-3 攻击的使用以及受控暴露时间的限制。需要对流行地区、血清阳性个体和现实世界的暴露场景进行进一步研究。尽管取得了令人鼓舞的结果,强生公司还是停止了莫诺登韦的进一步开发。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

The Potential for Antivirals in Dengue Prophylaxis and Treatment - Contagion Live

News Brief
A phase 2a randomized, placebo-controlled trial published in the New England Journal of Medicine found that the oral antiviral mosnodenvir significantly reduced DENV-3 RNA levels and dengue-related symptoms in healthy adults exposed to a controlled hypoattenuated dengue challenge, demonstrating a dose-dependent prophylactic effect. Conducted in the United States with 18–55-year-old volunteers without prior dengue exposure, the study reported mosnodenvir was generally well tolerated, though two severe adverse events occurred. Despite these encouraging early results, the drug’s developer has discontinued further development. The findings underscore the unmet need for dengue antivirals amid expanding global transmission, including locally acquired cases in Europe and the United States, where no approved antiviral prophylaxis or treatments currently exist.
Original source text (English)

Pylori Norovirus Healthcare Associated Infections (HAI) Prevention Antibiotics Immunizations Vaccines Respiratory Infections Community-Acquired Pneumonia Measles Influenza Sexually Transmitted Diseases HIV / AIDS HPV HSV PrEP Skin & Soft Tissue Diseases MRSA Zoonotic & Vector-Borne Diseases Lyme Disease ZIKA Choose Topic Spotlight - Vaccines in America| Antibiotics Deconstructed| Bench to Bedside With SIDP| Antimicrobial Stewardship| Hepatitis Advertisement News|Articles|July 22, 2026 Contagion Contagion, Spring 2026 Digital Edition Volume 11 Issue 1 The Potential for Antivirals in Dengue Prophylaxis and Treatment Author(s)Noah Rosenberg, MD A study found that the investigational antiviral mosnodenvir reduced dengue virus infection RNA levels and symptoms in a controlled setting, supporting its potential as a prophylactic option. Advertisement Dengue virus infection, transmitted by the Aedes mosquito and caused by 4 distinct serotypes (DENV- 1-4), has an expanding global incidence and geographic range1 with locally acquired cases to Europe and the United States.2 While its cases are often mild or asymptomatic, dengue has the potential to cause severe illness, including shock and capillary leak syndrome, in both dengue-naive individuals and those with prior infection. Two vaccines have received worldwide approval for the prevention and long-term protection against reinfection. CYD-TDV (Dengvaxia; Sanofi) is approved for use in certain endemic areas among those aged 9 to 45 years with confirmed prior dengue infection. However, it is not approved for travelers and is being discontinued due to low demand. TAK-003 (Qdenga; Takeda) is approved in many countries for both seropositive and seronegative people. Despite vaccine development and availability, there are no approved antiviral prophylaxis or treatments against dengue, especially in those who are nonimmune. Mosnodenvir is an orally administered small-molecule agent developed to target dengue viral replication. The drug inhibits the de novo formation of the NS4B-NS3 complex. NS4B is a transmembrane protein in the endoplasmic reticulum, which binds NS3 to enhance NS3 helicase activity and increase viral replication.3 Preclinical studies have shown serotype-independent antiviral activity through in vitro and in vivo models, and recent phase 1 trials have demonstrated an acceptable safety profile.4 This FIGURE represents the viral replication complex of Dengue virus and the target of mosnodenvir. The NS4B complex is ER (endoplasmic reticulum) bound.5 Durbin and colleagues conducted a phase 2a trial that assessed the antiviral activity and safety of mosnodenvir in healthy participants who received a challenge virus with a hypoattenuated strain of DENV-3.6 This was designed as a doubleblind, randomized, placebo-controlled trial at Johns Hopkins University and the University of Vermont, with funding from Johnson & Johnson. Healthy adults aged 18 to 55 years with no prior dengue exposure or endemic travel were enrolled and assigned to mosnodenvir or placebo in a stepwise, dose-escalation design. Participants were randomly assigned 1:1 to receive a 200-mg loading dose of oral mosnodenvir or placebo for the 5 days prior to challenge, and received an inoculation of underattenuated DENV-3 on day 0. The participants were then enrolled 2:1 into a high-dose cohort to establish safety. Further participants were randomly assigned to a high (100 mg), medium (50 mg), or low (10 mg) dose vs placebo in a 3:3:1 ratio for maintenance from day 1 to day 21. Patients were then followed through day 85. Primary outcomes included a reduction in DENV-3 RNA levels, clinical symptoms of infection, serologic response, and safety. The trial was powered to 85% probability to detect a 30% difference in RNA load between groups. High-dose mosnodenvir was associated with a significant reduction in DENV-3 RNA levels compared with placebo, with a clear dose-dependent antiviral effect regarding reduction in RNA levels and dengue infection–related symptoms. Participants with undetectable viral RNA did not have any detectable neutralizing antibodies, suggesting that successful inhibition of viral replication may prevent the development of humoral immunity. Mosnodenvir was generally well tolerated, with mostly mild to moderate adverse events. Only 2 severe events were noted: infection with SARS-CoV-2, and severe elevation in lipase and glucose levels. Together, the findings support mosnodenvir as a promising antiviral for dengue prophylaxis against DENV-3 infection, especially at high doses. However, the small sample size, use of a hypoattenuated DENV-3 challenge strain, and controlled timing of exposure limit generalizability to real-world settings with unpredictable exposure and multiple circulating serotypes. There is further work to be done to study those who have already been infected, those living in endemic regions, and the timing of prophylaxis. Despite these encouraging early results, Johnson & Johnson has discontinued further development of mosnodenvir. 7 However, other dengue antiviral strategies remain in development, including agents that target antiviral replication by Novartis, which are undergoing a phase 2 trial.8 There is also a monoclonal antibody undergoing evaluation.9 These show promise for the future of prophylaxis and treatment for dengue virus in the seropositive and seronegative populations. Paper: Durbin AP, Van Wesenbeeck L, Pierce KK, et al. Daily mosnodenvir as dengue prophylaxis in a controlled human infection model. View five news elements
Time
Published 2026-08-17; event described as a phase 2a trial conducted in 2025 and reported in 2025–2026 literature.
Location and population
United States (Johns Hopkins University, Baltimore, MD; University of Vermont, Burlington, VT); healthy adults aged 18–55 years with no prior dengue exposure or endemic travel.
Event
A phase 2a randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial evaluated the investigational antiviral mosnodenvir for dengue prophylaxis; results showed dose-dependent reductions in DENV-3 RNA levels and dengue-related symptoms, supporting potential prophylactic use. Reported by Durbin et al. in the New England Journal of Medicine.
Scale, impact and risk
No clinical cases or deaths reported in the trial; small sample size (healthy volunteers) limits population-level impact assessment. Mosnodenvir generally well tolerated; two severe adverse events (SARS-CoV-2 infection; elevated lipase and glucose) were reported. Dengue globally expanding with locally acquired cases in Europe and the United States.
Response, status and uncertainty
Investigation ongoing; phase 2a trial completed with promising early results. Generalizability limited by small sample, use of hypoattenuated DENV-3 challenge, and controlled exposure timing. Further studies needed in endemic regions, seropositive individuals, and real-world exposure scenarios. Johnson & Johnson discontinued further development of mosnodenvir despite encouraging results.

皮内拉斯县报告两例登革热病例,为 15 年来首例 - tampabaybeacons.com

Pinellas County reports two cases of dengue, first in 15 years - tampabaybeacons.com
新闻简报据佛罗里达州卫生部称,佛罗里达州皮内拉斯县报告了两例登革热病例,这是 15 年来的首例。这些病例疑似由蚊子传播,症状包括发烧、严重头痛和关节痛。卫生官员将监测和预防工作的重点放在棕榈港,他们在那里进行挨家挨户的检查并喷洒监测以减少蚊子滋生地。佛罗里达州卫生部指出,哨兵鸡中西尼罗河病毒的数量有所增加,但今年尚未报告人类感染西尼罗河病毒的病例。建议居民清除积水、穿上防护服并使用驱虫剂以防止进一步传播。目前尚无针对登革热的具体 治疗,病例正在调查中。应对措施包括加强蚊虫控制措施和公共卫生建议。
查看新闻五要素
时间
皮内拉斯县于 2026 年 8 月 18 日报告了两例登革热病例,症状通常在受感染的蚊子叮咬后 15 天内出现。
地点与对象
佛罗里达州皮内拉斯县,重点关注棕榈港,作为 监测 下的登革热预防工作区域。
事件
据佛罗里达州卫生部称,皮内拉斯县确认了两例登革热病例,这是 15 年来首次出现登革热病例。这些病例疑似由蚊子传播,卫生官员正在该地区开展预防工作。
规模、影响与风险
皮内拉斯县报告了两例确诊的登革热病例,没有具体的 治疗。正在通过蚊虫控制措施解决进一步传播的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
灭蚊工作优先考虑在棕榈港进行挨家挨户检查和 治疗,以减少孳生地。卫生官员建议居民采取防蚊措施。目前尚无针对登革热的具体 治疗,新病例的状况正在调查中。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Pinellas County reports two cases of dengue, first in 15 years - tampabaybeacons.com

News Brief
Pinellas County, Florida, has reported two cases of dengue, the first in over 15 years, according to the Florida Department of Health. The cases are suspected to be mosquito-borne, with symptoms including fever, severe headache, and joint pain. Health officials are focusing their surveillance and prevention efforts on Palm Harbor, where they are conducting door-to-door inspections and fogging treatments to reduce mosquito breeding sites. The Florida Department of Health noted that there has been an uptick in West Nile Virus in sentinel chickens, but no human cases of West Nile virus have been reported this year. Residents are advised to remove standing water, wear protective clothing, and use insect repellent to prevent further spread. There is no specific treatment for dengue, and the cases are under investigation. The response includes increased mosquito control measures and public health advisories.
Original source text (English)
About UsContact UsDeliverye-EditionsAdvertisingNewsletters Tampa Bay BEACONS COVERING YOUR COMMUNITY Search St. Petersburg HomeNewsSportsBusinessFoodSchoolsPetOutdoorsMultimedia | Seminole & Beaches HomeNewsSportsBusinessFoodSchoolsPetOutdoorsMultimedia | Belleair & Largo HomeNewsSportsBusinessFoodSchoolsPetOutdoorsMultimedia | More Clearwater & DunedinTarpon Springs & East LakePalm Harbor, Safety Harbor & Oldsmar | A&E Local Events News✕ Pinellas County› Pasco County› Hillsborough County› Hernando County› Manatee County› Topics› Special Sections E-Editions Advertise› 10 cases of dengue have been confirmed between Pinellas County and Hillsborough County, according to the Florida Department of Health. [ Photo by PAWICH SATTALERD ] Pinellas County reports two cases of dengue, first in 15 years Dengue is a viral infection spread by mosquito bites. Symptoms include fever, severe headache and joint pain. By Mia Taubenblat Yesterday The Florida Department of Health reported two cases of mosquito-borne dengue in Pinellas County on Aug. 12. Dengue fever is a viral illness transmitted through the bite of aedes aegypti and aedes albopictus mosquitoes, which are both found in Florida. Symptoms of dengue include severe muscle aches, joint pain, fever, nausea, pain behind the eyes, vomiting and rash. They typically present within 15 days of an infected mosquito bite. Pinellas County hasn’t reported a case of locally-acquired dengue in over 15 years, according to data from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. It is unclear if the new cases were identified in Palm Harbor, but health officials are focusing their surveillance in that area. The Hillsborough health department also issued a mosquito-borne illness alert Aug. 12, announcing an eighth case of dengue in the county. There is no specific treatment for dengue. Rest, drinking fluids and easing symptoms with acetaminophen are recommended. Tampa Bay residents presenting symptoms should see a healthcare provider and contact the health department. Going door-to-door on Pinellas residents’ private property, where mosquitoes are most commonly found, will be the priority of Pinellas County Mosquito Control’s dengue prevention efforts. Mosquito control will conduct daytime backpack fogging treatments and door-to-door inspections in Palm Harbor. The inspections aim to reduce areas where mosquitoes breed, such as standing water in animal dishes, flower pots and buckets. Following the cases, mosquito control is advising Palm Harbor residents to increase protection efforts, such as removing standing water on their properties, dress in light-colored, long-sleeve clothing and closed-toed shoes and use insect repellent that contains DEET, Picaridin, IR3535 or oil of lemon eucalyptus. There has also been an uptick in West Nile Virus in Pinellas County sentinel chickens, the mosquito control reported. Officials will increase overnight truck treatments and daytime aerial treatments to combat the spread of viruses. Pinellas County has reported no human cases of West Nile virus this year. 阻止登革热和其他蚊媒疾病

Stopping Dengue and other mosquito-borne illnesses
新闻简报本新闻报道讨论了 登革病毒 的传播,这是一种主要由埃及伊蚊传播的蚊媒疾病。报告强调,本月早些时候确认了弗吉尼亚州首例本地感染登革热病例,感染源可追溯到弗吉尼亚州北部。弗吉尼亚州卫生部的州公共卫生兽医朱莉娅·墨菲 (Julia Murphy) 博士解释说,虽然登革热的临床范围很广,但大多数感染都与旅行有关,只有约 5% 的病例会导致 重症登革热,这可能会导致休克和内出血。卫生官员强调,预防至关重要,包括使用驱蚊剂、维护纱窗以及消除积水以减少蚊子繁殖。尽管登革热在英联邦传播的风险并不大,但西尼罗河病毒等其他蚊媒疾病也令人担忧。该报告强调了遵循指南以减轻这些疾病传播的重要性。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Stopping Dengue and other mosquito-borne illnesses

News Brief
This news report discusses the spread of dengue virus, a mosquito-borne illness primarily transmitted by Aedes Aegypti mosquitoes. It highlights that the first locally acquired case of dengue in Virginia was confirmed earlier this month, with the infection traced to Northern Virginia. Dr. Julia Murphy, the State Public Health Veterinarian for the Virginia Department of Health, explains that while dengue can have a wide clinical spectrum, most infections are travel-associated, with only about 5% of cases resulting in severe dengue, which can lead to shock and internal bleeding. Health officials emphasize that prevention is crucial, including the use of mosquito repellent, maintaining window screens, and eliminating stagnant water to reduce mosquito breeding. Although the risk of dengue spreading in the Commonwealth is not large, other mosquito-borne illnesses like West Nile virus are also a concern. The report underscores the importance of following guidelines to mitigate the spread of these diseases.
Original source text (English)
ROANOKE, Va. ( WFXR ) - Dengue virus is a mosquito-borne illness that is normally found within tropical and sub-tropical areas. “Dengue viruses are spread to people through the bites of infected Aedes species mosquitoes,” says Dr. Julia Murphy, the State Public Health Veterinarian for the Virginia Department of Health. Mosquitos are considered vectors, meaning they’re organisms that can transmit diseases like dengue. “The one that spreads dengue the most efficiently and is considered the primary vector, if you will, of dengue virus is called Aedes Aegypti .” The species is native to Africa, but has appeared in the U.S. sporadically over the years as an invasive species. But, the U.S. is home to another species of Aedes mosquito; Aedes Albopictus. “The kind of Aedes mosquito that we have in abundance, is one that is a competent vector, you could say, but it's really not the main driver of dengue,” says Murphy. “It has a tendency to have a broader array of animals that it bites.” According to the World Health Organization, up to 400 million people around the world are at risk of becoming infected with dengue each year. Stopping the spread of vector-borne disease But unlike other viruses that spread from person to person, dengue spreads through mosquito bites. “You have a person who's infected with dengue, a person for whom dengue virus is circulating in their blood, a mosquito vector mosquito bites that person, “says Murphy. “Then after a relatively short period of time, that mosquito becomes infected with the dengue virus, and then if that mosquito bites another person, then that person gets infected.” Earlier this month, VDH confirmed the first case of dengue to be locally acquired here in Virginia. “We had a person in Northern Virginia who tested positive for the dengue virus and it appears that that infection was locally acquired, which means that person got infected in Virginia,” says Murphy. However, health officials say while this case is historic, it’s not unprecedented. “Over the years, 2010 through 2025, of all of the dengue virus infections that were reported as happening in people in the United States about 95% of them were travel associated,” says Murphy. VDH says while other potentially locally acquired cases are possible, it’s unlikely to see other cases at this time. Rabies: A closer look at the ‘deadliest disease’ “A lot of times that people at highest risk for additional transmission are folks in the household or folks who are in close proximity to a person with dengue virus in circulation,” says Murphy. Not everyone will have a reaction to the virus, as some people can appear asymptomatic to the infection. “Dengue can have a wide clinical spectrum that can occur,” says Murphy. “A lot of people don't become sick at all.” However, for those that are impacted by the disease, symptoms can vary. “Oftentimes they'll get a fever, that's a real hallmark of dengue,” says Murphy. “Then they have general aches and pains, sort of especially one feature of dengue can be in some people, a lot of pain behind the eyes, what's called retro ball pain.” Other symptoms can include nausea, vomiting, muscle and joint pain, and a skin rash. “A smaller subset, probably around five-percent of people, can get what's called severe dengue,” says Murphy. “As the name would suggest, that is a severe illness that can result in things like shock and internal bleeding.” But while the risk of dengue spreading in the Commonwealth isn’t large, there are other mosquito-borne illnesses to be on the lookout for, including West Nile Virus. VDH says prevention is key, and there are plenty of tools people can use to not only help prevent dengue, but all mosquito-born illnesses. Back-to-school health, getting students ready for the school year “Some good prevention measures include things like when you're outdoors using mosquito repellent,” says Murphy. “What I would say is always follow the directions on the label.” Not every repellant is able to be used by all people, which is why experts say it’s important to know what ingredients are inside before spraying it on yourself, a child, or a pet. “It's important to look at that label and also look at the concentrations of of the various ingredients, because there are some precautions and recommendations for children,” says Murphy. “There may also be some cautions and recommendations associated with any pets you have.” Dr. Murphy suggests checking out the EPA’s website to determine which insect repellant is right for you. Another thing to utilize for protection is making sure you have screens on your windows, and that there aren’t any holes in them. “A lot of people like to have their door open or their windows open,” says Murphy. “Make sure that screen is in good repair, make sure your window screens are being repaired because you want to try to keep mosquitoes out.” Finally, it’s important to manage pools of water near your home, as stagnant water can become a breeding ground for mosquito larva. “You can help to drive down the populations of mosquitoes in and around your yard by helping to eliminate or routinely refreshing any standing water in your backyard,” says Murphy. Mosquito eggs need just seven to ten days to transition into a full-grown adult mosquito. “So if mosquitoes are laying their eggs in there and you're disrupting that cycle at least once a week, it'll really decrease the likelihood that any eggs that are in there would go on to become adults,” says Murphy. B routine priorityPublic-health news

DOH reports another travel-related dengue virus case - Maui Now

News Brief
The Hawaiʻi Department of Health reported another travel-related dengue virus case on Oʻahu, bringing the total number of dengue cases in Hawaiʻi to seven in 2026. This case highlights the potential for dengue virus to spread through human travel, particularly in the wake of recent weather events like Hurricane Lala. The report does not specify the exact strain of the virus or provide details on the investigation or response measures taken. The case was identified as part of ongoing health monitoring efforts, and no explicit risk level was mentioned. The Department of Health continues to track and report on dengue cases, emphasizing the need for vigilance and preventive measures in the community.

登革热:坦帕湾报告了 10 起本地感染病例 - AOL.com

Dengue fever: 10 locally-acquired cases reported in Tampa Bay - AOL.com
新闻简报登革热 坦帕湾地区本地感染病例有所增加,希尔斯伯勒县报告了 8 例确诊病例,皮尼拉斯县确认了 2 例。这些病例归因于该地区的蚊虫叮咬,而不是旅行。卫生官员正在加强灭蚊工作,包括增加 监测 和喷洒,并敦促居民消除积水并使用驱虫剂。虽然皮内拉斯县今年尚未报告西尼罗河病毒的人类病例,但哨兵鸡中存在该病毒表明存在更广泛的蚊媒健康问题。佛罗里达州卫生部和当地蚊虫控制小组正在共同努力管理 暴发 并防止进一步传播。建议居民采取预防措施,例如检查积水和穿着防护服,以降低感染风险。

官员们表示,居民可以通过倒掉积水、使用驱蚊剂以及穿着宽松的长袖衣服来提供帮助。

坦帕湾地区,特别是美国佛罗里达州的希尔斯伯勒县和皮内拉斯县。受影响人群包括这些县可能接触过埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的居民。
事件

规模、影响与风险
坦帕湾地区共确诊10例本地感染登革热病例。该风险与埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的存在有关,已知这些蚊子会传播登革热。没有明确说明具体的风险级别,但病例的增加表明公共卫生问题日益严重。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Dengue fever: 10 locally-acquired cases reported in Tampa Bay - AOL.com

News Brief
Dengue fever has seen a rise in locally-acquired cases in the Tampa Bay area, with Hillsborough County reporting eight confirmed cases and Pinellas County confirming two. These cases are attributed to mosquito bites in the region, not travel. Health officials are intensifying mosquito control efforts, including increased surveillance and spraying, and are urging residents to eliminate standing water and use repellent. While no human cases of West Nile virus have been reported in Pinellas County this year, the presence of the virus in sentinel chickens suggests a broader mosquito-borne health concern. The Florida Department of Health and local mosquito control teams are working together to manage the outbreak and prevent further spread. Residents are advised to take preventive measures, such as checking for standing water and wearing protective clothing, to reduce their risk of infection.
Original source text (English)
The Brief Hillsborough County health officials have confirmed eight locally-acquired cases of dengue fever, up from two reported last week. Pinellas County has also confirmed two locally-acquired cases, prompting increased mosquito surveillance and treatment in the Palm Harbor area. Officials say residents can help by dumping standing water, using mosquito repellent and wearing loose-fitting, long-sleeved clothing. TAMPA, Fla. - Health officials are increasing mosquito control efforts across parts of the Tampa Bay area following a jump in locally acquired dengue fever cases. Tampa Bay dengue cases What we know The Florida Department of Health in Hillsborough County announced an eighth confirmed locally-acquired case of dengue, while Pinellas County health officials have reported two locally-acquired cases. Both counties remain on alert as mosquito control teams increase surveillance and treatment. Locally-acquired cases mean the infections were transmitted by mosquitoes in the area rather than associated with travel. Undetected spread and testing Why it matters Dengue is spread through the bite of infected Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquito. The Aedes aegypti mosquito is particularly concerning because it is active during the daytime, can breed in small amounts of standing water commonly found around homes, and thrives in neighborhoods. "It's a container breeder, which, unfortunately, means it likes to breed in things like buckets, planters, even trash you left out, maybe a kid's toy that gets left upside down," said Gabriela Henderson with Hillsborough County Mosquito Services. Henderson says even something as small as a bottle cap can become a breeding container for Aedes aegypti mosquitoes. Adding to the challenge of combatting Aedes aegypti mosquitoes, Pinellas County officials say traditional adulticide treatments and aerial larviciding treatments are not effective in targeting these species. Neighborhood prevention steps What you can do Officials say one of the most effective steps residents can take is checking their property for standing water and dumping it out. That includes water collected in buckets, flowerpots, toys, tires, grill and pool covers, coolers and other containers. Even small amounts can provide a place for mosquitoes to breed. Residents should also: Wear light-colored, loose-fitting long sleeves and pants when possible. Use an EPA-registered mosquito repellent with an effective ingredient such as DEET, picaridin, IR3535 or oil of lemon eucalyptus. Repair damaged screens on windows and doors. Regularly dump or cover anything around the home that can collect rain or sprinkler water. "The biggest thing you can do is look for that standing water around your home," Henderson said. Recognizing dengue fever symptoms What are the symptoms of dengue? Dengue can cause a flu-like illness that includes fever, severe muscle and joint pain and, in some cases, a rash. Symptoms generally develop within about two weeks of being bitten by an infected mosquito. Dengue is not spread directly from person to person through ordinary contact. Henderson said people who feel sick after recently being bitten by mosquitoes should tell their doctor. "If you're feeling under the weather, you notice you've been bit by mosquitoes recently, it's worth mentioning to your doctor because they can then give the proper test," Henderson said. "We work closely with the health department so they will be alerted, and then we are alerted as well." Pinellas County virus tracking What's being done Hillsborough County's health department and mosquito management teams say they are coordinating surveillance and prevention efforts, including spraying, to reduce mosquito activity. In Pinellas County, mosquito control crews have increased inspections and treatments following the two confirmed cases. The county says crews are conducting daytime backpack fogging, daily surveillance and door-to-door inspections in affected areas. Pinellas County is also increasing mosquito control efforts because surveillance has detected an uptick in West Nile virus activity in sentinel chickens, which are used as an early warning system for mosquito-borne viruses. The county says no human cases of West Nile virus have been reported in Pinellas County so far this year. You can learn more about mosquito control efforts in Hillsborough and Pinellas counties via the following links. The Source Information in this article comes from the Florida Department of Health in Hillsborough County, Pinellas County Mosquito Control, the Florida Department of Health in Pinellas County, and prior reporting by FOX 13

Event

Scale, impact and risk
A total of 10 locally-acquired dengue cases were confirmed in the Tampa Bay area. The risk is associated with the presence of Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes, which are known to transmit dengue. No specific risk level was explicitly stated, but the increase in cases indicates a heightened public health concern.
Response, status and uncertainty
Health officials are increasing mosquito control efforts, including increased surveillance, spraying, and prevention measures. They are also advising residents to eliminate standing water and use mosquito repellent. The situation remains under active investigation and monitoring.

登革热还是病毒热?医生解释了为什么他们看起来“相似”,并分享了 9 个迹象来区分他们 |健康 - 印度斯坦时报

Dengue or viral fever? Doctor explains why they may appear to be ‘similar’ and shares 9 signs to tell them apart | Health - Hindustan Times
新闻简报本文由《印度斯坦时报》于 2026 年 8 月 18 日发表,讨论了登革热和病毒热之间的区别。报告强调,这两种情况都可能出现类似的症状,例如发烧、身体疼痛和虚弱,这常常导致公众感到困惑。然而,文章强调,如果不及时诊断和治疗,登革热可能会危及生命,这与大多数具有自限性的病毒性发烧不同。医生 Chirag Tandon 博士警告不要自行用药,并强调早期 诊断 对于预防血浆渗漏、休克、内出血和器官功能障碍等并发症的重要性。文章还概述了可能表明登革热的具体症状,例如严重头痛、眼眶后疼痛和皮疹,并解释说,治疗登革热的 治疗 需要仔细输液和监测,而病毒性发烧通常用扑热息痛治疗。这篇文章作为健康咨询,敦促个人咨询医疗专业人士,而不是依赖自我 诊断 或 治疗。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Dengue or viral fever? Doctor explains why they may appear to be ‘similar’ and shares 9 signs to tell them apart | Health - Hindustan Times

News Brief
This article, published on August 18, 2026, by Hindustan Times, discusses the differences between dengue and viral fever. It highlights that both conditions can present with similar symptoms such as fever, body aches, and weakness, which often leads to confusion among the public. However, the article emphasizes that dengue can be life-threatening if not diagnosed and treated promptly, unlike most viral fevers, which are self-limiting. The doctor, Dr. Chirag Tandon, warns against self-medication and stresses the importance of early diagnosis to prevent complications like plasma leakage, shock, internal bleeding, and organ malfunction. The article also outlines specific symptoms that may indicate dengue, such as severe headache, retro-orbital pain, and a rash, and explains that treatment for dengue involves careful fluid administration and monitoring, whereas viral fevers are typically managed with paracetamol. The piece serves as a health advisory, urging individuals to consult medical professionals rather than relying on self-diagnosis or treatment.
Original source text (English)
Dengue or viral fever? Doctor explains why they may appear to be ‘similar’ and shares 9 signs to tell them apart Dengue fever should not be confused with viral fever, otherwise the health consequences may be adverse. Know how you can tell them apart. Published on: Aug 18, 2026, 10:32:24 IST By Adrija Dey Prefer HT on Google Share via Copy link This monsoon season, you will hear almost everyone around you falling sick, with fever, sore throat, and vomiting. The most common response would be a lackadaisical one: that it is just a characteristic seasonal ailment. But this distinction is not for you to surmise otherwise; self-treatment can have adverse outcomes. ALSO READ: How to know if your medicine is genuine? 6 questions to ask before buying it Know the difference between viral fever and dengue fever. (Picture credit: Freepik) Dengue is also common during the monsoon season, and the symptoms can overlap with those of viral infection. Let's hear from a seasoned specialist with over 18 years of experience whose health advisory strictly cautions people from self-treating if they experience any flu-like symptoms. “Many people self-medicate and wait for symptoms to settle. Most viral fevers are self-limiting; dengue follows a very different course and can become life-threatening if warning signs are overlooked," Dr Chirag Tandon, director, internal medicine, ShardaCare-HealthCity, alerted, advocating for proactive initiative to get any symptoms evaluated by a health professional. Since dengue can be life-threatening if not treated on time can be dangerous. He ruled out self-medication at this time, even for viral infections, as many symptoms resemble. “The challenge is that both illnesses begin with similar symptoms, making it difficult to recognise when medical evaluation is necessary,” he instructed strictly. If you get it checked early, Dr Chirag informed that early diagnosis ensures successful treatment outcomes. Providing the key distinction, the doctor helped explain the root cause: “Dengue is due to the dengue virus, which is transmitted through the bite of an Aedes mosquito. On the other hand, viral fever refers to the condition characterized by fever due to different types of viruses.” Here are some of the questions related to dengue and viral fever that the doctor answered for us: Why do people confuse dengue and viral fever? Dr Chirag believed the most common symptom that causes confusion and leads many people to mistake dengue with viral fever is that both cause significant fever, aches in the body, and weakness. But dengue's signs are a bit higher on the severity scale than fever and can be recognised easily, despite the surface-level similarities. How to recognise if you may have dengue? To assess the symptoms, dengue's symptoms are more intense, while also being located in different regions. “Dengue symptoms include the sudden onset of high fever, severe headache, pain in the retro-orbital area, a high degree of muscle and joint pain, vomiting, and skin rash. When fever is caused by a viral infection, it usually features a sore throat, cough, rhinitis, and nasal blockage. However, respiratory symptoms in the case of dengue are relatively uncommon and constitute an important clinical clue,” Dr Chirag outlined the major differences that can help you understand if your symptoms lean more towards dengue or not. This necessary distinction also alleviates panic, as not all viral infections in the monsoon will be dengue. Further, the doctor provided the clinical picture of the laboratory findings. It is the last step to rule out dengue as a viral infection, and dengue appears very differently in blood tests. “Dengue often causes a reduction in platelet count and white blood cell count, but platelet numbers alone should never be used to judge disease severity. Equal importance is given to haematocrit levels, as a rising haematocrit may indicate plasma leakage, one of the hallmark complications of severe dengue. Viral fevers rarely produce this combination of laboratory findings.” Recurring fever should be taken as a sign to get tested for dengue. (Picture credit: Pexel) What is the most common misconception? As per the expert, dengue's recovery is often measured against fever, which is incorrect. He clarified and explained why it can be dangerous: “Many think dengue improves once fever subsides, but the timeframe between the third and the seventh day of the disease, when temperatures begin to stabilize, is often the most serious. At the time, plasma leakage could occur, resulting in an increased possibility of shock, internal bleeding, and organ malfunction.” This clinical insight is crucial as it tells us that even a seemingly recovering patient can deteriorate rapidly. Other than fever, which other symptoms indicate dengue? Dizziness is one of the symptoms of dengue. (Picture credit: Shutterstock) Dengue fever is accompanied by several other ‘warning signs,' which the doctor advised never to ignore. He cautioned about these signs, which may progressively get worse and require hospital-based management: Persistent vomiting Severe abdominal pain Increasing drowsiness Unusual restlessness Bleeding from the gums or nose Reduced urine output Cold clammy skin Breathlessness Sudden dizziness How are the treatments of dengue and viral fevers different? Just to put into perspective, so you know how far apart the treatments are, the doctor pointed out that dengue fever needs a combination of careful administration of fluids and continuous monitoring of the condition, and patients are asked to stay away from medications like ibuprofen and aspirin since they can increase the risk of bleeding. But regular viral fevers go down with the expert-recommended paracetamol dosage. Note to readers: This article is for informational purposes only and not a substitute for professional medical advice. Always seek the advice of your doctor with any questions about a medical condition. ABOUT THE AUTHOR Adrija Dey Adrija Dey’s proclivity for observation fuels her storytelling instinct. 葛兰素史克 (GSK) 从 Chugai 获得临床前登革热抗体 - AllSci

GSK secures preclinical dengue antibody from Chugai - AllSci
新闻简报GSK 已从日本中外制药有限公司获得 AID351 的全球独家许可,AID351 是一种针对所有四种登革热血清型的临床前单克隆抗体。该协议于 2025 年 1 月签署,标志着 GSK 的重点从登革热预防转向 治疗。AID351是通过中外制药新加坡子公司和ASTAR新加坡免疫学网络的联合项目开发的,新加坡国立大学提供了先导抗体。该项目获得了全球健康创新技术基金的资助。葛兰素史克将负责第一阶段和随后的临床试验,而中外制药保留未来销售的特许权使用费。没有披露任何财务细节,该交易符合葛兰素史克的全球健康战略,其中包括加速登革热等疾病的研发。目前,还没有批准用于登革热的单克隆抗体 治疗,AID351 的开发可能代表着治疗选择方面向前迈出了重要一步。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该新闻报道于 2026 年 8 月 19 日发布。合作于 2025 年 1 月签署,开发承诺正在进行中。
地点与对象
此次合作涉及日本中外制药株式会社和英国葛兰素史克 (GSK)。重点是登革病毒,它在全球热带和亚热带地区流行。
事件
GSK 已从 Chugai Pharmaceutical Co.,Ltd. 获得了 AID351 的全球独家许可,AID351 是一种临床前泛血清型抗 登革病毒 单克隆抗体。这标志着 GSK 的兴趣从预防转向登革热 治疗。
规模、影响与风险
没有报告具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。AID351 的开发可能会影响登革热的 治疗 格局,目前登革热缺乏批准的单克隆抗体疗法。没有明确说明风险,但注意到了治疗进展的潜力。
应对、状态与不确定性
AID351的开发由GSK负责I期及后续临床试验。该交易的财务细节尚未披露,该项目是葛兰素史克更广泛的全球健康战略的一部分。该状态仍在进行中,未提及任何解决方案。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

GSK secures preclinical dengue antibody from Chugai - AllSci

News Brief
GSK has secured an exclusive, worldwide license for AID351, a preclinical monoclonal antibody targeting all four dengue serotypes, from Japan-based Chugai Pharmaceutical Co., Ltd. This agreement, signed in January 2025, marks a shift in GSK's focus from dengue prevention to treatment. AID351 was developed through a joint project involving Chugai's Singapore-based subsidiary and ASTAR Singapore Immunology Network, with the National University of Singapore contributing the lead antibody. The project received funding from the Global Health Innovative Technology Fund. GSK will handle Phase I and subsequent clinical trials, while Chugai retains royalty rights on future sales. No financial details were disclosed, and the deal aligns with GSK's Global Health strategy, which includes accelerating R&D for diseases like dengue. Currently, no approved monoclonal antibody treatment for dengue exists, and the development of AID351 could represent a significant step forward in therapeutic options.
